# V4 Milestone 3 — Dispatch & Fulfillment Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Route every placed order through exactly one **job** that an admin assigns to an eligible provider («مجری») from the new «مرکز تخصیص سفارش», that the provider accepts or rejects and drives through production and handover in «سرو مجری», with delivery by courier, by a provider or by customer pickup, a provider-price snapshot taken at assignment, timeouts back to the queue, notifications for provider/admin/customer and per-provider performance counters — without breaking any M1/M2 behaviour.

**Architecture:** A new `modules/dispatch` holds the fulfilment engine: pure modules (`job-model.ts` flow/mirror/tabs, `eligibility.ts` capability + availability checks, `provider-pricing.ts` provider quote + snapshot, `job-seed.ts`, `performance.ts`) and four services — `JobsService` (job creation, the single transition path with forward-only order mirroring, cancellation, reconcile), `DispatchService` (admin reads, eligibility and assignment writes), `ProviderJobsService` (provider-scoped views and actions behind `ProviderScopeGuard`) and `DispatchScheduler` (timeouts, deadline/late/unavailable alerts, auto-complete, reconcile). Orders keep their status track (D10); the job is the fulfilment track and moves the order only forward where a step maps one-to-one. Orders, payments, courier and uploads call into `JobsService`/the `Job` model; nothing imports the orders module back (no cycles). Notifications reuse the inbox/web-push infrastructure, generalized to provider and admin audiences with DB templates.

**Tech Stack:** NestJS 12 (ESM, `.js` import suffixes), Mongoose 9 / MongoDB 8, ioredis, `@nestjs/schedule` (`@Interval`), vitest; React 19, Vite 8 (multi-page), Tailwind v4, shadcn/ui, TanStack Query, zustand.

**Spec:** `Digital Sarv- Update.txt` (Master Specification V4 — §40, §51–65, §67–72, §75, §86, §88–89, §91) + `docs/v4-design.md` (decisions D10–D12, D15, D17–D21; sections «Dispatch & fulfillment», «Architecture», «Ledger & settlement» for what M4 reads). Read both, plus the M2 plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-v4-m2-providers.md` for the conventions this plan continues.

## Owner decisions for M3 (2026-10-16)

Answers to the decisions raised with the first draft of this plan; they extend `docs/v4-design.md` D1–D21.

| # | Decision |
|---|---|
| D22 | **Fees unchanged.** Every order keeps today's pickup fee and delivery fee exactly as the current quote computes them (plan discounts, free-pickup / free-delivery rules unchanged), whatever the delivery method (courier, provider, customer pickup) and whether or not a home pickup happens. For the same draft the quote and total are byte-identical to M2. |
| D23 | **Provider prices for non-print lines are deferred to M4.** School binding, flyer, cartridge, repair, thesis page printing, catalog extras and spiral-colour surcharges stay «قیمت مجری تنظیم نشده»; M3 assigns them with the explicit missing-price confirmation (`confirmMissingPrice`) and stores `providerSubtotal` / `payoutBasis = null` (see «Handoff to M4»). |
| D24 | **Provider privacy.** A provider sees its own provider price on the job; only the provider that delivers gets the customer's name, phone, address and delivery note; a provider handing over at its shop gets name and phone only; a production-only provider gets no contact data; child names are visible to the producer (book labels). |
| D25 | **Timings.** Deadline = order time + 48 h (urgent 24 h); provider response timeout 120 min; deadline reminder 120 min before; auto-complete 24 h after delivery — all editable in «تنظیمات» (`dispatch.*`). |

Decisions 3–10, 12 and 14–16 of the first draft are accepted as recommended (home pickup only for school books / cartridges / repairs; pickup points only from Digital Sarv, provider shop chosen by the admin; the customer's delivery-vs-pickup choice is fixed, courier ↔ provider is chosen by the admin; hard/soft eligibility checks; capacity units by assignment day; delivery-only providers do not accept; v1 centre assignments migrate as accepted jobs without a snapshot; forward-only status mirror with QC by the provider; `provider_order_*` are admin permissions; «سفارش‌ها» is the provider home tab; no `centerId` for customers; Gregorian date filters in V1).

## Global Constraints

- Contract first: `docs/api-contract.md` gets «v4.2 — dispatch and fulfillment» in Task 1, before any code; every later task matches it (paths, bodies, permissions, error codes, Persian messages, the status mapping table).
- Extend, never rebuild: every M1/M2 unit and e2e test stays green (the few M1 assertions this plan deliberately changes are named in their task); `PATCH /admin/orders/:id/assign`, `/admin/production`, the courier app and the customer track screen keep working.
- V1 creates exactly **one job per order** (`seq: 1`) covering every child and service line (D11, D18). The schema keeps `seq` + line indexes so splitting later needs no migration.
- Admin picks the provider; **never auto-select** (no cheapest-first sorting on the server, §52/§54/§70).
- A provider that fails the capability check can **never** receive the job, not even with override (§86). Other failed checks (busy, not taking orders, hours, zone, capacity) need `override: true` + a reason (§71).
- Missing provider price = «قیمت مجری تنظیم نشده»: never another provider's price, a global fallback, 0 or a guess (§53). Assignment then needs `confirmMissingPrice: true` and the snapshot stores `providerSubtotal: null`.
- Provider prices are snapshotted at assignment into `Order.providerPricing` (`select: false`) and `Job.providerPricing`; the customer snapshot (`services[i].pricing`, `quote`, `chargedAmount`) is never touched (§40, §73).
- Visibility (§26–27, §62, §64): customer responses never contain `centerId`, `providerPricing`, provider ids, provider prices or job internals; provider responses never contain customer prices, the quote, margins, other providers' names/prices, admin users or notes; a provider sees customer contact/address only when it delivers or hands over at its shop (§62).
- Provider identity on `/provider/*` comes **only** from `ProviderScopeGuard`; a job that is not the caller's answers **404 «سفارش یافت نشد»** (no existence leak, §65/§91).
- Money stays atomic: COD «paid» is set only inside the order's conditional `findOneAndUpdate` to `delivered`; no read-modify-write on money. M3 records stages and payout bases only — no ledger, no payout (M4, D20).
- Every job write is a compare-and-set on `status` (+ the current `providerId`/`assignment.id` where relevant); a lost race answers **409 «وضعیت سفارش هم‌زمان تغییر کرد؛ دوباره تلاش کنید»**. Every atomic `findOneAndUpdate`/`updateOne` on orders `$inc`s `__v` (optimistic concurrency, see `order.schema.ts`).
- Scheduled work is idempotent and multi-instance safe: each effect is guarded by a conditional update and only the winner notifies.
- Order statuses are mirrored **forward only** along `STATUS_FLOW`; never out of `pending_payment`, `awaiting_approval`, `delivered`, `cancelled`.
- Integer toman everywhere. ESM relative imports end in `.js`. RTL logical utilities only (`ms-/me-/ps-/pe-/start-/end-/text-start`). Persian numerals in the UI via `lib/format.ts` (`fa`, `money`, `toNum`, `jalali`, `jalaliDayTime`).
- Nothing hard-coded that admins edit: timeout/lead times/pickup points live in `Settings`; notification texts are `notificationTemplates` rows seeded by `seed:base`.
- Follow `.claude/skills/digital-sarv-design/SKILL.md`; reuse the admin kit (`PageHeader`, `AdminCard`, `CardTitle`, `CardNote`, `QueryView`, `AdminTable`, `AdminRow`, `Pager`, `SearchInput`, `FormDialog`, `Field`, `NumInput`, `AdminSelect`, `Tag`, `StatusTag`, `AddButton`, `ConfirmDelete`, `DsSwitch`, `Chip`, `InfoBanner`) and the mobile kit (`AppShell`, `MobileHeader`, `ScreenBody`, `Panel`, `BottomTabs`, `ToneTag`, `LoadingBlock`, `ErrorState`, `EmptyState`).
- **Fees unchanged (D22):** M3 never touches `computeQuote` / `quoteDraft`; `delivery` in a draft is ignored by pricing and no field is added to `Quote`. The web derives home pickup with the same rule as the server (`needsHomePickup`), not from the quote.
- Machine limits (~1.4 GB free disk): no Docker image builds, no `playwright install`, no new npm dependencies. Browser checks use Playwright with `channel: 'msedge'` and the `localStorage['sarv-auth'] = {state:{token,user},version:0}` seeding from `CLAUDE.md`.
- Commit after every task; message ends with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

## Review Focus

1. **Two admins assign the same job at once, or a provider accepts in the second the timeout fires** — exactly one write wins, the other gets 409, and only the winner's notifications go out (Task 10 test `concurrent assigns: one wins`, Task 15 test `timeout vs accept: one wins, no double alert`).
2. **A provider acting from a stale screen after the job was reassigned or timed out** — accept/status/QC/file download answer 404/403 and change nothing; the provider's «لغوشده» tab shows only the order code (Task 11 test `stale provider after reassignment`, Task 14 test `released provider loses file access`).
3. **The customer (or an admin) cancels while the provider is producing** — the job becomes `cancelled` with `stage: 'in_production'`, the provider is notified once, and a later provider status update is refused (Task 12 test `cancel during production records the stage`, Task 11 test `no status after cancel`).
4. **A school-book / cartridge / repair order whose provider tries to start production before the courier collected the items** — refused with «اقلام سفارش هنوز از مشتری تحویل گرفته نشده است» (Task 8 test `production blocked before home pickup`).
5. **A print-only checkout without a pickup slot, choosing «تحویل حضوری» at a point that was switched off meanwhile** — 400 «محل تحویل حضوری معتبر نیست»; with a valid point the order stores the point and the quote is exactly the M2 quote, fees included (D22) (Task 6 tests `print-only checkout`, `switched-off pickup point`, `fees are byte-identical to M2`).

### Task 1: Contract v4.2 and plan status (docs)

**Files:**
- Modify: `docs/api-contract.md` (append the section below at the end), `docs/plan.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: every name, path, permission, error code and Persian message below is binding for Tasks 2–25.

- [ ] **Step 1: Append to `docs/api-contract.md`**

````markdown
## v4.2 — dispatch and fulfillment (Master Specification V4, Milestone 3)

Design: `docs/v4-design.md` (D10–D12, D15, D17–D21). Everything is additive; v1–v4.1 behaviour is unchanged unless stated.

### Jobs («کار» = the fulfilment of an order; D11, D18)

```ts
type JobStatus = 'unassigned' | 'assigned' | 'accepted' | 'in_production' | 'ready' | 'out_for_delivery'
  | 'ready_for_pickup' | 'delivered' | 'completed' | 'rejected' | 'cancelled' | 'failed';
// تخصیص‌نیافته · تخصیص‌یافته (در انتظار پاسخ) · پذیرفته‌شده · در حال تولید · آماده · در مسیر تحویل · آماده تحویل حضوری ·
// تحویل شد · تکمیل‌شده · ردشده · لغوشده · ناموفق
type DeliveryMethod = 'courier' | 'provider' | 'customer_pickup';   // ارسال با پیک · ارسال توسط مجری · تحویل حضوری
type PickupAt = 'point' | 'provider';                               // Digital Sarv pickup point · the provider's shop (D17)
type JobPriority = 'normal' | 'high' | 'urgent';                    // عادی · بالا · فوری
type RejectReason = 'equipment_unavailable' | 'paper_unavailable' | 'capacity_full' | 'machine_problem' | 'outside_hours' | 'other';
// تجهیزات در دسترس نیست · کاغذ موجود نیست · ظرفیت تکمیل است · دستگاه خراب است · خارج از ساعت کاری · سایر (§59)
type CancelStage = 'before_production' | 'in_production' | 'after_production';
```

- **One job per order** (`seq: 1`, unique `{orderId, seq}`) covering every child and service line (`childIndexes`,
  `lineIndexes`). It is created when the order is **placed**: wallet/COD orders at `POST /orders`, gateway orders when
  the payment is verified (`pending_payment → registered`). A reconcile pass (at boot and every minute) creates any
  missing job for orders that are not `pending_payment`/`cancelled`/`delivered` — including orders placed before v4.2:
  an order with a v1 `centerId` becomes a job of that provider (v1 had no acceptance) with status
  `registered|confirmed|courier_assigned|picked_up|awaiting_approval → accepted`, `preparing|binding|extras|qc → in_production`,
  `packing → ready`, `out_for_delivery → out_for_delivery`, history action `migrated`, and **no** provider price snapshot.
- `rejected` and `failed` are **history statuses**: the job itself returns to `unassigned` at once (D21) and the
  provider joins `releasedProviderIds`. `attempts` counts assignments.
- `deadline` = order `createdAt` + `dispatch.leadHours` (`dispatch.urgentLeadHours` for urgent orders); `priority`
  starts `urgent` for urgent orders, else `normal`. Both are admin-editable.
- `late` = now (or `readyAt`) is after `deadline` and the job is not cancelled.
- The current production provider is mirrored into `Order.centerId` (v1 field; admins only). `deliveryProviderId`
  (§61) is the provider that delivers when `deliveryMethod = 'provider'` (default: the production provider; may be a
  delivery-only provider).

**Job flow** (only these moves; anything else is 400 «تغییر وضعیت از «X» به «Y» مجاز نیست»):

| From → To | By | Condition |
|---|---|---|
| unassigned → assigned | admin (`assign`) | |
| assigned → accepted | production provider, admin on behalf | |
| assigned → unassigned (history `rejected`) | production provider, admin on behalf | reason required |
| assigned → unassigned (history `timeout`) | system | no answer within `dispatch.responseTimeoutMinutes` |
| assigned/accepted/in_production → unassigned (history `unassigned`) | admin (`unassign`) | reason required |
| assigned/accepted/in_production → assigned (other provider, history `reassigned`) | admin (`reassign`) | reason required |
| accepted → in_production | production provider, admin | a home-pickup order must be past the courier pickup (400 «اقلام سفارش هنوز از مشتری تحویل گرفته نشده است») |
| accepted/in_production → unassigned (history `failed`) | production provider, admin | reason required |
| in_production → ready | production provider, admin | the order's QC checklist complete (400 «کنترل کیفیت کامل نشده است») |
| ready → out_for_delivery | delivery provider (method `provider`), courier (method `courier`, `POST /courier/orders/:id/collected`), admin | |
| ready → ready_for_pickup | production provider (method `customer_pickup` at `provider`), admin (any pickup) | |
| ready → delivered | courier (method `courier`, legacy direct delivery), admin | |
| out_for_delivery → delivered | delivery provider / courier (per method), admin | |
| ready_for_pickup → delivered | production provider (at its shop), admin | |
| delivered → completed | admin, system (`dispatch.autoCompleteHours` after delivery; 0 = off) | |
| any live → cancelled | the order was cancelled | `cancel.stage` recorded (M4 compensation) |

**Order status mapping (D10 — forward only, along the v1 happy path; never out of `pending_payment`,
`awaiting_approval`, `delivered`, `cancelled`):**

| Job reaches | Order moves to | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `accepted` | `confirmed` (only from `registered`) | customer event `assigned` |
| `in_production` | `preparing` (from anything earlier) | template `preparing` |
| `ready` | `packing` | template `packing` |
| `out_for_delivery` | `out_for_delivery` | template `out_for_delivery` |
| `ready_for_pickup` | (stays `packing`) | customer event `ready_for_pickup` with the place appended |
| `delivered` | `delivered` (+ COD paid, atomic) | template `delivered`; the order moves **first**, then the job |
| `completed` | (stays `delivered`) | |
| `cancelled` | — (the order was cancelled first) | |

Reverse direction: an order cancelled anywhere cancels its live job; an order set `delivered` by the courier or the
admin sets its job `delivered`; an admin `out_for_delivery` on an order whose job is `ready` with method `courier`
moves the job too. The reconcile pass repairs any job whose order is already `cancelled`/`delivered`. The admin
production board, `binding`/`extras`/`qc` and `PATCH /admin/orders/:id/status` keep working; the board items gain
`providerName?` and `jobStatus?`.

### Delivery on the order (D12, D15, D17)

```ts
interface PickupPoint { id: string; name: string; address: string; phone: string; hours: string; on: boolean }  // «محل‌های تحویل حضوری» in تنظیمات
interface PlaceSnapshot { id: string; name: string; address: string; phone: string; hours: string; kind: 'point' | 'provider' }
interface OrderDelivery {
  method: DeliveryMethod;       // the customer chooses 'courier' (= «ارسال به آدرس») or 'customer_pickup'; the admin may switch courier ↔ provider
  homePickup: boolean;          // children (school books) or cart / repair lines need the courier's home pickup
  pickupAt?: PickupAt;          // customer_pickup only
  pickupPoint?: PlaceSnapshot;  // the Digital Sarv point the customer chose (customer_pickup)
  providerShop?: PlaceSnapshot; // set when the admin moves the pickup to the provider's shop (allowCustomerPickup)
  note: string;                 // «توضیحات تحویل» (≤ 300)
}
```
- `OrderDraft.delivery?: { method: 'courier' | 'customer_pickup'; pickupPointId?: string; note?: string }` (default `courier`).
- `homePickup = children.length > 0 || some service kind ∈ {cart, repair}`. Orders saved before v4.2 read
  `{ method: 'courier', homePickup: true, note: '' }`.
- `POST /orders` (Persian 400s, in this order after the existing checks): homePickup → `pickup.date` + `pickup.slot`
  required («زمان تحویل‌گیری را انتخاب کنید») and validated as v3.3; homePickup or method `courier` →
  `pickup.address` required («آدرس تحویل‌گیری را وارد کنید» / «آدرس ارسال را وارد کنید»); `customer_pickup` →
  `pickupPointId` must be an `on` point («محل تحویل حضوری معتبر نیست»). `pickup.phone` stays required. `PickupDto`
  `address`, `date`, `slot` become optional at the DTO level.
- **Quote and fees unchanged (D22):** `POST /orders/quote` and the order's `quote` are byte-identical to v4.1 for the
  same draft — the pickup fee and delivery fee are charged exactly as today (plan / rule discounts unchanged) whatever
  the delivery method and whether a home pickup happens; `delivery` in a draft is ignored by pricing. Clients derive
  home pickup with the rule above (it is not part of the quote).
- `GET /catalog` gains `pickupPoints: Omit<PickupPoint, 'on'>[]` (only `on` points, in stored order).

### Customer order view

`GET /orders/mine`, `GET /orders/:id`, `POST /orders`, `POST /orders/:id/cancel` for the customer **no longer contain
`centerId`** and never `providerPricing`. They gain `delivery: OrderDelivery` and
`fulfillment?: { status: JobStatus; label: string; place?: PlaceSnapshot }` (labels: در صف تولید · تأیید شد · در حال
تولید · آماده شد · آماده تحویل حضوری · در مسیر تحویل · تحویل شد · لغو شد; `place` = the provider shop when
`pickupAt = 'provider'`, else the chosen point). Admin and courier views are unchanged.

### Provider price snapshot at assignment (§40, §52–53, §66)

```ts
interface ProviderPricingLine { serviceIndex: number; comboKey: string; label: string; versionId: string; version: number;
  unitPrice: number /* provider_unit_price */; quantity: number; subtotal: number }
interface ProviderPricingMissing { serviceIndex?: number; childIndex?: number; comboKey: string | null; label: string }
interface ProviderPricingSnapshot {
  providerId: string;             // provider_id
  snapshotAt: string; currency: 'toman';
  lines: ProviderPricingLine[];   // provider_price_version = versionId + version per line
  missing: ProviderPricingMissing[];
  providerSubtotal: number | null;            // provider_subtotal; null when anything is missing
  delivery: { providerId: string; fee: number } | null;   // method 'provider'
  payoutBasis: number | null;     // provider_payout_basis = providerSubtotal + delivery.fee when the production provider delivers (M4 reads it)
  confirmedMissing: boolean;
}
```
- Provider price of a job = Σ over every customer price-book line (`bw|`, `color|`, `spiral|`, `addon|`) of the
  provider's **effective** unit price at assignment × the line quantity. Children (school binding), services without
  price-book lines (flyer, cartridge, repair, hardcover docs) and non-price-book lines (`extra|…`, `spiral_color|…`,
  `docs_print`) are **missing** («قیمت مجری تنظیم نشده»), never 0 (§53).
- Stored on `Order.providerPricing` (never serialized to customers or couriers) and `Job.providerPricing`; every
  snapshot of the job is kept in `Job.pricingHistory`. The customer snapshot is never rewritten.

### Eligibility (§51–54)

`checks` per provider (every provider is listed — eligible ones first; the server never sorts by price):

| key | hard? | ok when |
|---|---|---|
| `capability` | **always hard** | every line matches a capability: school → `school`; docs/flyer/cart/repair → that service; B/W and colour → size, side, (colour) colour type + paper, add-ons (spiral, glue, hardcover, staple, lam_cover, lam_sheet, mixed). Legacy `print` lines are read as their v4 kind. |
| `status` | hard for `inactive`/`suspended`, soft for `busy` | `active` |
| `available` | soft | `availableForOrders` |
| `hours` | soft | open now (Tehran) by `workingHours`; no hours = ok («ساعت کاری ثبت نشده است») |
| `zone` | soft | no `serviceZoneIds` = all zones; order zone unknown = ok; else the order's zone (by name) is served |
| `capacity` | soft | today's (Tehran, by `assignment.assignedAt`) usage + this job ≤ `capacity.dailySheets` (B/W + colour sheets × copies), ≤ `capacityPerDay` jobs (v1 field, 0 = off), ≤ each `capacity.perService` (units: print = sheets, school = books, docs = copies, flyer = quantity, cart = cartridges, repair = devices) |
| `delivery` | hard | only when the requested method is `provider` and this provider would deliver: `canDeliver` and `active` |

`hourlySheets` and equipment are shown, not checked. `eligible` = every check ok; `assignable` = no hard check failed.

### Admin endpoints (`/admin/dispatch`, role admin)

| Method | Path | Permission | Body / query → response |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/admin/dispatch/jobs?tab=&providerId=&service=&status=&priority=&deliveryMethod=&deliveryStatus=&from=&to=&q=&page=&limit=` | `dispatch_view` | `{ items: DispatchJobRow[], total, page, limit, counts: Record<DispatchTab, number> }` |
| GET | `/admin/dispatch/jobs/:id` | `dispatch_view` | `DispatchJobDetail` (`:id` = job id **or** order id) |
| GET | `/admin/dispatch/jobs/:id/eligibility?deliveryMethod=` | `dispatch_view` | `Eligibility` |
| POST | `/admin/dispatch/jobs/:id/assign` | `dispatch_assign` | `AssignJobBody` → `DispatchJobDetail` |
| POST | `/admin/dispatch/jobs/:id/reassign` | `dispatch_reassign` | `AssignJobBody & { reason }` → `DispatchJobDetail` |
| POST | `/admin/dispatch/jobs/:id/unassign` | `dispatch_reassign` | `{ reason }` → `DispatchJobDetail` |
| PATCH | `/admin/dispatch/jobs/:id/delivery` | `dispatch_assign` | `{ deliveryMethod?, deliveryProviderId?, pickupAt?, reason? }` → `DispatchJobDetail` (until `ready`) |
| PATCH | `/admin/dispatch/jobs/:id` | `dispatch_assign` | `{ priority?, deadline? (ISO) }` → `DispatchJobDetail` |
| POST | `/admin/dispatch/jobs/:id/respond` | `provider_order_accept` | `{ accept: boolean, reasonCode?, reason }` (on behalf of the provider) → `DispatchJobDetail` |
| POST | `/admin/dispatch/jobs/:id/status` | `provider_order_status` | `{ status: 'in_production'\|'ready'\|'out_for_delivery'\|'ready_for_pickup'\|'delivered'\|'completed'\|'failed', reason, reasonCode? }` → `DispatchJobDetail` |
| GET | `/admin/dispatch/performance?from=&to=` | `provider_order_view` | `ProviderPerformance[]` (`yyyy-mm-dd` Tehran; default the last 30 days) |

```ts
type DispatchTab = 'new' | 'unassigned' | 'assigned' | 'in_progress' | 'ready' | 'delivery' | 'completed' | 'cancelled';
// سفارش‌های جدید (unassigned, never assigned) · تخصیص‌نیافته (unassigned) · تخصیص‌یافته (assigned) · در حال انجام (accepted, in_production)
// · آماده (ready) · تحویل (out_for_delivery, ready_for_pickup, delivered) · تکمیل‌شده · لغوشده (§55)
type DeliveryStatus = 'pending' | 'ready' | 'on_the_way' | 'done';   // before ready · ready · out_for_delivery/ready_for_pickup · delivered/completed
interface AssignJobBody {
  providerId: string; deliveryMethod?: DeliveryMethod; deliveryProviderId?: string; pickupAt?: PickupAt;
  override?: boolean; reason?: string; confirmMissingPrice?: boolean;
}
interface DispatchJobRow {
  id: string; orderId: string; orderCode: string; customerName: string; zone?: string; services: ProviderService[]; summary: string;
  status: JobStatus; orderStatus: OrderStatus; priority: JobPriority; deadline: string; late: boolean; attempts: number;
  provider: { id: string; name: string } | null; deliveryProvider: { id: string; name: string } | null;
  deliveryMethod: DeliveryMethod; pickupAt?: PickupAt; respondBy?: string;
  lastException?: { kind: 'rejected' | 'timeout' | 'failed' | 'unassigned'; reason: string; providerName: string; at: string };
  createdAt: string;
}
type JobAction = 'created' | 'migrated' | 'assigned' | 'reassigned' | 'accepted' | 'rejected' | 'timeout' | 'unassigned' | 'status'
  | 'failed' | 'delivery_changed' | 'updated' | 'cancelled' | 'completed';
interface JobHistoryView { at: string; action: JobAction; status: JobStatus; label: string; providerName?: string; deliveryProviderName?: string;
  byName?: string; byRole: 'admin' | 'provider' | 'courier' | 'customer' | 'system'; reason?: string; reasonCode?: RejectReason; override?: boolean; stage?: CancelStage }
interface AssignmentView { providerId: string; providerName: string; assignedByName?: string; assignedAt: string; acceptedAt?: string;
  rejectedAt?: string; completedAt?: string; endedAt?: string; reason?: string; reasonCode?: RejectReason; override?: boolean;
  outcome: 'pending' | 'active' | 'rejected' | 'timeout' | 'withdrawn' | 'failed' | 'cancelled' | 'completed' }   // §75
interface DispatchJobDetail extends DispatchJobRow {
  homePickup: boolean; sheets: number; units: Partial<Record<ProviderService, number>>; requirements: string[];
  order: { status: OrderStatus; urgent: boolean; payMethod: PayMethod; paid: boolean; total: number; delivery: OrderDelivery;
    pickup: { address: string; phone: string; date?: string; slot?: string }; qcDone: boolean; courierId?: string };
  providerPricing: ProviderPricingSnapshot | null; history: JobHistoryView[]; assignments: AssignmentView[];
  cancel?: { at: string; stage: CancelStage; providerName?: string };
}
interface ProviderCheck { key: 'capability' | 'status' | 'available' | 'hours' | 'zone' | 'capacity' | 'delivery'; ok: boolean; hard: boolean; message: string }
interface EligibilityRow {
  providerId: string; name: string; businessName: string; status: ProviderStatus; current: boolean;
  eligible: boolean; assignable: boolean; checks: ProviderCheck[];
  price: { subtotal: number | null; missing: string[]; lines: { label: string; unitPrice: number; quantity: number; subtotal: number }[] };
  capacityLeft: { sheets: number | null; jobs: number | null; perService: { service: ProviderService; left: number }[] };
  hourlySheets?: number; openNow: boolean | null; activeJobs: number; hasLogin: boolean;
  canDeliver: boolean; deliveryFee: number; allowCustomerPickup: boolean;
}
interface Eligibility { jobId: string; requirements: string[]; eligible: EligibilityRow[]; others: EligibilityRow[];
  deliveryProviders: { id: string; name: string; deliveryFee: number; zoneOk: boolean }[] }
interface ProviderPerformance {
  providerId: string; name: string; assigned: number; accepted: number; rejected: number; timeouts: number; failed: number;
  rejectReasons: { code: RejectReason; label: string; count: number }[]; completed: number; cancelled: number; late: number;
  avgResponseMinutes: number | null; avgProductionMinutes: number | null; avgDeliveryMinutes: number | null;
  activeJobs: number; todaySheets: number;
}   // §67 — collected and shown to admins only; never used to penalize
```

Errors (Persian):
- 404 «سفارش یافت نشد» (unknown job / order), 404 «مجری یافت نشد».
- 400 «این سفارش لغو یا تحویل شده است» · 400 «این سفارش هنوز پرداخت نشده است».
- 409 «این سفارش قبلاً تخصیص داده شده است؛ برای تغییر مجری از «تغییر مجری» استفاده کنید» (assign when not `unassigned`).
- 400 «این سفارش در وضعیتی نیست که بتوان مجری آن را تغییر داد» (reassign/unassign outside assigned/accepted/in_production).
- 400 «این سفارش هم‌اکنون به همین مجری سپرده شده است».
- 400 `{ statusCode: 400, code: 'NOT_CAPABLE', message: 'این مجری توانایی انجام این سفارش را ندارد', problems: string[] }` — never overridable (§86).
- 409 `{ statusCode: 409, code: 'PROVIDER_NOT_ELIGIBLE', message: 'این مجری شرایط دریافت سفارش را ندارد؛ برای تخصیص با وجود این شرایط دلیل را بنویسید', problems: string[] }` → resend with `override: true` + `reason` (400 «دلیل نادیده‌گرفتن شرایط را بنویسید»).
- 409 `{ statusCode: 409, code: 'PROVIDER_PRICE_MISSING', message: 'قیمت مجری تنظیم نشده', missing: string[] }` → resend with `confirmMissingPrice: true`.
- 400 «دلیل را بنویسید» (reassign, unassign, respond, status).
- 400 «مجری تحویل باید فعال باشد و امکان ارسال داشته باشد» · 400 «تحویل حضوری در محل این مجری مجاز نیست» ·
  400 «روش تحویل انتخابی مشتری (ارسال یا تحویل حضوری) تغییر نمی‌کند» · 400 «روش تحویل این سفارش دیگر قابل تغییر نیست» (after `ready`).
- 409 «وضعیت سفارش هم‌زمان تغییر کرد؛ دوباره تلاش کنید» (any lost compare-and-set).

`PATCH /admin/orders/:id/assign { centerId }` (v1) delegates: a provider id → `assign` (or `reassign` with reason
«تغییر از صفحه سفارش» when held) under the same checks and permissions (`dispatch_assign` / `dispatch_reassign`, 403
«به این بخش دسترسی ندارید»); `centerId: null` → `unassign` with reason «لغو از صفحه سفارش». `courierId` keeps v1
behaviour, except that assigning a courier to an order without home pickup does not add `confirmed`/`courier_assigned`.

### Provider endpoints (`/provider/jobs`, role provider, `ProviderScopeGuard`)

| Method | Path | Body → response |
|---|---|---|
| GET | `/provider/jobs?tab=&page=&limit=` | `{ items: (ProviderJobView \| ProviderWithdrawnView)[], total, page, limit, counts: Record<ProviderTab, number> }` |
| GET | `/provider/jobs/:id` | `ProviderJobDetail` (the first open of an `assigned` job sets `assignment.seenAt`) |
| POST | `/provider/jobs/:id/accept` | → `ProviderJobDetail` |
| POST | `/provider/jobs/:id/reject` | `{ reasonCode: RejectReason, note? }` (note required for `other`: 400 «برای «سایر» توضیح بنویسید») → `{ ok: true }` |
| POST | `/provider/jobs/:id/status` | `{ status: 'in_production'\|'ready'\|'out_for_delivery'\|'ready_for_pickup'\|'delivered'\|'failed', reasonCode?, note? }` → `ProviderJobDetail` (`failed` needs `reasonCode`; then `{ ok: true }`) |
| PATCH | `/provider/jobs/:id/qc` | `{ index, done }` → `ProviderJobDetail` (production provider, while `accepted`/`in_production`) |

```ts
type ProviderTab = 'new' | 'pending' | 'accepted' | 'in_production' | 'ready' | 'delivery' | 'completed' | 'cancelled';
// سفارش‌های جدید (assigned, unopened) · در انتظار پاسخ (assigned, opened) · پذیرفته‌شده · در حال تولید · آماده (ready, ready_for_pickup)
// · تحویل (out_for_delivery; a delivery provider's ready jobs too) · تکمیل‌شده (delivered, completed) · لغوشده (§57)
interface ProviderJobView {
  id: string; orderCode: string; status: JobStatus; statusLabel: string; roles: ('production' | 'delivery')[];
  priority: JobPriority; deadline: string; late: boolean; assignedAt?: string; respondBy?: string;
  services: ProviderService[]; summary: string; delivery: { method: DeliveryMethod; label: string; pickupAt?: PickupAt };
  providerSubtotal: number | null;   // the caller's own snapshot (production role); never customer prices
}
interface ProviderWithdrawnView { id: string; orderCode: string; withdrawn: true; at: string; label: string }   // only the code + time
interface ProviderJobLine { kind: 'child' | 'service'; label: string; detail: string; quantity: number;
  options: Record<string, string | number | boolean>; childName?: string; note?: string }
interface ProviderJobDetail extends ProviderJobView {
  lines: ProviderJobLine[];             // production role (§57: service, options, quantity, paper, size, colour, side, binding)
  files: Upload[];                      // production role: the order's line files (never courier pickup photos)
  qc: { labels: string[]; done: boolean[] } | null;     // production role
  pricing: { lines: { label: string; unitPrice: number; quantity: number; subtotal: number }[]; missing: string[]; subtotal: number | null } | null;
  contact: { name: string; phone: string; address: string; note: string } | null;  // delivery role, or production role handing over at its shop (§62)
  collectFrom: { name: string; address: string; phone: string } | null;           // delivery-only role: where to collect
  homePickup: boolean; itemsReceived: boolean;   // false while a home-pickup order is not collected yet
  history: { at: string; status: JobStatus; label: string; note?: string }[];      // the caller's own events only
}
```
- A job is the caller's when `providerId` = caller (production) or `deliveryProviderId` = caller and status ∈
  `ready, out_for_delivery, delivered, completed` (delivery). Anything else — another provider's job, a job taken
  away, an unknown id — answers **404 «سفارش یافت نشد»**.
- `accept`/`reject` only while `assigned` (400 «این سفارش منتظر پاسخ شما نیست»); reject requires a `reasonCode`
  (400 «دلیل رد را انتخاب کنید»).
- Provider responses never contain customer prices, the quote, `chargedAmount`, margins, other providers' prices or
  names (except `collectFrom` for a delivery-only provider), admin names, admin notes or other history.

### Courier additions

- `CourierTask` gains `from?: { name; address; phone }` (delivery task of a `courier` job that is `ready` at the
  provider — collect it there) and `dropoff?: { name; address; phone }` (pickup task of an order whose job has a
  provider — bring the items there). Pickup tasks exist only for orders with `homePickup`. Delivery tasks also list
  `packing` orders whose job is `ready` with method `courier`.
- `POST /courier/orders/:id/collected` → `Order` — the courier took the parcel at the provider: job `ready →
  out_for_delivery`, order `packing → out_for_delivery` (400 «این سفارش هنوز آماده تحویل نیست» otherwise).
- `POST /courier/orders/:id/delivered` additionally sets the job `delivered`.

### Files (§65)

`GET /uploads/:id` for role `provider`: allowed only for the linked provider of a job that references the upload's
order, with job `providerId` = caller and status ∈ `assigned, accepted, in_production, ready, ready_for_pickup`, and
never for `pickup`-purpose uploads (courier photos). Otherwise 403 «به این فایل دسترسی ندارید».

### Notifications (§68, D19)

- `NotificationTemplate.event` and `UserNotification.event` accept `NotificationEvent = OrderStatus | DispatchEvent`:
  provider — `job_assigned` «سفارش جدیدی به شما سپرده شد؛ لطفاً بررسی و پاسخ دهید.», `job_delivery_assigned`
  «ارسال یک سفارش به شما سپرده شد.», `job_withdrawn` «تخصیص این سفارش به شما لغو شد.», `job_cancelled` «این سفارش لغو
  شد؛ کار روی آن را متوقف کنید.», `job_changed` «جزئیات این سفارش (تحویل یا مهلت) تغییر کرد.», `job_deadline` «مهلت
  انجام این سفارش نزدیک است.»; admin — `job_rejected` «مجری سفارش را رد کرد؛ سفارش به تخصیص‌نیافته‌ها برگشت.»,
  `job_timeout` «مجری در مهلت پاسخ نداد؛ سفارش به تخصیص‌نیافته‌ها برگشت.», `job_failed` «مجری اعلام کرد سفارش قابل
  انجام نیست؛ سفارش به تخصیص‌نیافته‌ها برگشت.», `job_late` «مهلت سفارش گذشته و هنوز آماده نشده است.»,
  `provider_unavailable` «مجری این سفارش در دسترس نیست؛ تخصیص را بررسی کنید.»; customer — `assigned` «سفارش شما به
  واحد تولید سپرده شد.», `ready_for_pickup` «سفارش شما آماده تحویل حضوری است.» (the place is appended:
  « — <name>، <address>»). Customer «received / in production / ready / out for delivery / completed» reuse the
  `registered`, `preparing`, `packing`, `out_for_delivery`, `delivered` templates. `seed:base` inserts the missing
  templates (`on: true`).
- `UserNotification` gains `audience: 'customer' | 'provider' | 'admin'` (absent = customer), `url: string`, `jobId?`.
  Provider rows: url `/provider/jobs/<jobId>`, push title «سرو مجری»; admin rows (every admin with `dispatch_view`, or
  a super-admin): url `/admin/dispatch?job=<jobId>`, push title «دیجیتال سرو · مدیریت». Customer rows unchanged.
- `GET /notifications/mine`, `POST /notifications/mine/read`, `POST /notifications/push/subscribe|unsubscribe` accept
  roles `customer`, `provider`, `admin`; each role sees only its own audience. SMS stays OTP-only.
- Alerts are sent once per job and cause: `deadlineNotifiedAt`, `lateAlertedAt`, `unavailableAlertedAt` guard them.
- Admin «اعلان‌ها» lists and edits every template, grouped by audience.

### Settings («تنظیمات»)

```ts
interface DispatchSettings {
  responseTimeoutMinutes: number;   // 10..1440, default 120 (D21)
  leadHours: number;                // 1..720, default 48
  urgentLeadHours: number;          // 1..720, default 24
  deadlineReminderMinutes: number;  // 0..1440, default 120 (0 = no reminder)
  autoCompleteHours: number;        // 0..720, default 24 (0 = admins complete by hand)
}
```
`GET /admin/settings` / `PUT /admin/settings` gain `dispatch: DispatchSettings` and `pickupPoints: PickupPoint[]`
(≤ 30; replaced as a whole; a point without `id` gets one; 400 «شناسه محل تحویل تکراری است», «نام محل تحویل را
وارد کنید», «آدرس محل تحویل را وارد کنید», «شماره تماس محل تحویل معتبر نیست»). A timeout change applies to
assignments made afterwards.

### Permissions (§81)

`dispatch_view` (dispatch center, eligibility, admin alerts), `dispatch_assign` (assign, delivery, priority, deadline),
`dispatch_reassign` (reassign, unassign), `provider_order_view` (provider performance and workload),
`provider_order_accept` (accept/reject on behalf of a provider), `provider_order_status` (production/delivery status
and completion on behalf of a provider) — all admin permissions, shown in «کارکنان و دسترسی‌ها».

### M4 reads (no money in M3)

`Order.providerPricing.payoutBasis` (+ `delivery.fee` of a separate delivery provider), `Job.deliveredAt`,
`Job.cancel.stage` (`before_production` → no provider credit; `in_production`/`after_production` → admin decides) and
the `stage` on `reassigned`/`unassigned`/`failed` history entries (the previous provider's stage).

### Seed (dev)

Demo `seed`: «چاپ نگین» also does school binding and docs and allows pickup at its shop; «پرینت لند» = colour A3/A4 on
تحریر ۸۰; «کپی‌سنتر پارس» = delivery-only (`canDeliver`, no capabilities); one pickup point «دفتر مرکزی دیجیتال
سرو»; چاپ نگین's provider price for `color|A4|full|glossy200|single`. Jobs of the demo orders are created by the
reconcile pass when the API starts.

### Admin menu and apps

Admin: «مرکز تخصیص سفارش» (`/admin/dispatch`, after «سفارش‌ها»; tabs + «عملکرد مجریان»; alerts bell). Provider app:
«سفارش‌ها» becomes the home tab (`/provider` → jobs, `/provider/jobs/:id`), «قیمت‌ها» moves to `/provider/prices`,
new «اعلان‌ها» (`/provider/notifications`, browser push toggle). Customer: «روش و آدرس تحویل» step for orders without
home pickup; the track screen shows `fulfillment` and the pickup place. Courier: «دریافت از مجری» step.
````

```ts
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { matches } from '../../../test/fake-model.js';
import { audienceOf } from '../../common/constants.js';
import {
  assignmentHistory, canMove, cancelStage, customerFulfillmentLabel, deadlineFor, deliveryStatusFilter, dispatchTabFilter,
  isLate, legacyJobStatus, mirrorSources, productionBlocked, providerTabFilter, type JobHistoryEntry,
} from './job-model.js';

const t = (m: number) => new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 16, 8, m));

describe('job flow (contract v4.2 «Job flow»)', () => {
  it('production provider: accept → produce → ready; never the courier steps', () => {
    const c = { method: 'courier' as const };
    expect(canMove('assigned', 'accepted', 'production', c)).toBe(true);
    expect(canMove('accepted', 'in_production', 'production', c)).toBe(true);
    expect(canMove('in_production', 'ready', 'production', c)).toBe(true);
    expect(canMove('assigned', 'in_production', 'production', c)).toBe(false);
    expect(canMove('ready', 'out_for_delivery', 'production', c)).toBe(false);
    expect(canMove('ready', 'out_for_delivery', 'courier', c)).toBe(true);
    expect(canMove('ready', 'delivered', 'courier', c)).toBe(true);
    expect(canMove('out_for_delivery', 'delivered', 'courier', c)).toBe(true);
  });
  it('provider delivery: only the delivery role dispatches and delivers', () => {
    const c = { method: 'provider' as const };
    expect(canMove('ready', 'out_for_delivery', 'delivery', c)).toBe(true);
    expect(canMove('ready', 'out_for_delivery', 'courier', c)).toBe(false);
    expect(canMove('out_for_delivery', 'delivered', 'delivery', c)).toBe(true);
    expect(canMove('out_for_delivery', 'delivered', 'production', c)).toBe(false);
  });
  it('pickup at the provider shop is handed over by the producer; at a Digital Sarv point only by an admin', () => {
    const shop = { method: 'customer_pickup' as const, pickupAt: 'provider' as const };
    const point = { method: 'customer_pickup' as const, pickupAt: 'point' as const };
    expect(canMove('ready', 'ready_for_pickup', 'production', shop)).toBe(true);
    expect(canMove('ready_for_pickup', 'delivered', 'production', shop)).toBe(true);
    expect(canMove('ready', 'ready_for_pickup', 'production', point)).toBe(false);
    expect(canMove('ready', 'ready_for_pickup', 'admin', point)).toBe(true);
    expect(canMove('ready_for_pickup', 'delivered', 'admin', point)).toBe(true);
    expect(canMove('ready', 'out_for_delivery', 'admin', point)).toBe(false);
  });
  it('admin may make every listed move but no unlisted one; completion by admin or system only', () => {
    const c = { method: 'courier' as const };
    expect(canMove('assigned', 'accepted', 'admin', c)).toBe(true);
    expect(canMove('in_production', 'failed', 'admin', c)).toBe(true);
    expect(canMove('delivered', 'completed', 'admin', c)).toBe(true);
    expect(canMove('delivered', 'completed', 'system', c)).toBe(true);
    expect(canMove('delivered', 'completed', 'production', c)).toBe(false);
    expect(canMove('unassigned', 'ready', 'admin', c)).toBe(false);
    expect(canMove('ready', 'in_production', 'admin', c)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('order mirroring (D10)', () => {
  it('is forward only along the happy path', () => {
    expect(mirrorSources('confirmed')).toEqual(['registered']);
    expect(mirrorSources('packing')).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['registered', 'picked_up', 'preparing', 'qc']));
    expect(mirrorSources('packing')).not.toContain('out_for_delivery');
    expect(mirrorSources('packing')).not.toContain('awaiting_approval');
    expect(mirrorSources('delivered')).not.toContain('cancelled');
    expect(mirrorSources('registered')).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('production blocked before home pickup', () => {
    expect(productionBlocked({ status: 'courier_assigned', homePickup: true })).toBe('اقلام سفارش هنوز از مشتری تحویل گرفته نشده است');
    expect(productionBlocked({ status: 'awaiting_approval', homePickup: true })).not.toBeNull();
    expect(productionBlocked({ status: 'picked_up', homePickup: true })).toBeNull();
    expect(productionBlocked({ status: 'registered', homePickup: false })).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('stages, legacy jobs, deadlines, labels', () => {
  it('cancel stage (M4 compensation basis)', () => {
    for (const s of ['unassigned', 'assigned', 'accepted'] as const) expect(cancelStage(s)).toBe('before_production');
    expect(cancelStage('in_production')).toBe('in_production');
    for (const s of ['ready', 'out_for_delivery', 'ready_for_pickup'] as const) expect(cancelStage(s)).toBe('after_production');
  });
  it('a v1 centre assignment becomes a job at the matching stage', () => {
    expect(legacyJobStatus('registered')).toBe('accepted');
    expect(legacyJobStatus('awaiting_approval')).toBe('accepted');
    expect(legacyJobStatus('binding')).toBe('in_production');
    expect(legacyJobStatus('packing')).toBe('ready');
    expect(legacyJobStatus('out_for_delivery')).toBe('out_for_delivery');
  });
  it('deadline from lead hours; late only while not finished in time', () => {
    const d = deadlineFor(t(0), false, { leadHours: 48, urgentLeadHours: 24 });
    expect(d.getTime() - t(0).getTime()).toBe(48 * 3_600_000);
    expect(deadlineFor(t(0), true, { leadHours: 48, urgentLeadHours: 24 }).getTime() - t(0).getTime()).toBe(24 * 3_600_000);
    expect(isLate({ status: 'in_production', deadline: t(10) }, t(11))).toBe(true);
    expect(isLate({ status: 'in_production', deadline: t(10) }, t(9))).toBe(false);
    expect(isLate({ status: 'delivered', deadline: t(10), readyAt: t(9) }, t(30))).toBe(false);
    expect(isLate({ status: 'delivered', deadline: t(10), readyAt: t(12) }, t(30))).toBe(true);
    expect(isLate({ status: 'cancelled', deadline: t(10) }, t(30))).toBe(false);
  });
  it('customer labels never name a provider', () => {
    expect(customerFulfillmentLabel('assigned')).toBe('در صف تولید');
    expect(customerFulfillmentLabel('ready_for_pickup')).toBe('آماده تحویل حضوری');
    expect(customerFulfillmentLabel('completed')).toBe('تحویل شد');
  });
  it('dispatch events have an audience; order statuses go to the customer', () => {
    expect(audienceOf('job_assigned')).toBe('provider');
    expect(audienceOf('job_timeout')).toBe('admin');
    expect(audienceOf('ready_for_pickup')).toBe('customer');
    expect(audienceOf('packing')).toBe('customer');
  });
});

describe('assignment history (§60, §75)', () => {
  const A = new Types.ObjectId();
  const B = new Types.ObjectId();
  const admin = new Types.ObjectId();
  it('keeps the previous provider, the rejection, its reason, the new provider, the admin and the times', () => {
    const h: JobHistoryEntry[] = [
      { at: t(0), action: 'created', status: 'unassigned', byRole: 'system' },
      { at: t(1), action: 'assigned', status: 'assigned', providerId: A, by: admin, byRole: 'admin' },
      { at: t(5), action: 'rejected', status: 'rejected', providerId: A, byRole: 'provider', reasonCode: 'equipment_unavailable', reason: 'تجهیزات در دسترس نیست' },
      { at: t(6), action: 'assigned', status: 'assigned', providerId: B, by: admin, byRole: 'admin' },
      { at: t(7), action: 'accepted', status: 'accepted', providerId: B, byRole: 'provider' },
      { at: t(50), action: 'completed', status: 'completed', providerId: B, byRole: 'system' },
    ];
    const a = assignmentHistory(h);
    expect(a).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(a[0]).toMatchObject({ providerId: String(A), assignedBy: String(admin), assignedAt: t(1), rejectedAt: t(5), endedAt: t(5), outcome: 'rejected', reasonCode: 'equipment_unavailable' });
    expect(a[1]).toMatchObject({ providerId: String(B), assignedAt: t(6), acceptedAt: t(7), completedAt: t(50), outcome: 'completed' });
  });
  it('a reassignment closes the previous assignment as withdrawn with the reason; timeout and failure end it too', () => {
    const a = assignmentHistory([
      { at: t(1), action: 'assigned', status: 'assigned', providerId: A, byRole: 'admin' },
      { at: t(2), action: 'reassigned', status: 'assigned', providerId: B, previousProviderId: A, byRole: 'admin', reason: 'فوری‌تر' },
      { at: t(3), action: 'timeout', status: 'unassigned', providerId: B, byRole: 'system' },
    ]);
    expect(a.map((r) => r.outcome)).toEqual(['withdrawn', 'timeout']);
    expect(a[0]).toMatchObject({ reason: 'فوری‌تر', endedAt: t(2) });
  });
});

describe('tabs (§55, §57)', () => {
  const P = new Types.ObjectId();
  const Q = new Types.ObjectId();
  const job = (o: Record<string, unknown>) => ({ _id: new Types.ObjectId(), attempts: 0, ...o });
  it('«سفارش‌های جدید» = never assigned; «تخصیص‌نیافته» includes returned jobs', () => {
    const fresh = job({ status: 'unassigned' });
    const returned = job({ status: 'unassigned', attempts: 2 });
    expect(matches(fresh, dispatchTabFilter('new'))).toBe(true);
    expect(matches(returned, dispatchTabFilter('new'))).toBe(false);
    expect(matches(returned, dispatchTabFilter('unassigned'))).toBe(true);
    expect(matches(job({ status: 'ready_for_pickup' }), dispatchTabFilter('delivery'))).toBe(true);
    expect(matches(job({ status: 'in_production' }), dispatchTabFilter('in_progress'))).toBe(true);
    expect(matches(job({ status: 'ready_for_pickup' }), deliveryStatusFilter('on_the_way'))).toBe(true);
  });
  it('provider tabs are scoped to the provider; a delivery provider sees ready jobs under «تحویل»', () => {
    const unseen = job({ status: 'assigned', providerId: P, assignment: { assignedAt: t(0) } });
    const seen = job({ status: 'assigned', providerId: P, assignment: { assignedAt: t(0), seenAt: t(1) } });
    expect(matches(unseen, providerTabFilter('new', P))).toBe(true);
    expect(matches(seen, providerTabFilter('pending', P))).toBe(true);
    expect(matches(unseen, providerTabFilter('new', Q))).toBe(false);
    const delivering = job({ status: 'ready', providerId: P, deliveryProviderId: Q });
    expect(matches(delivering, providerTabFilter('delivery', Q))).toBe(true);
    expect(matches(delivering, providerTabFilter('ready', Q))).toBe(false);
    expect(matches(delivering, providerTabFilter('ready', P))).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

```ts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------- v4.2 dispatch & fulfillment (M3)

/** Job status (contract v4.2). `rejected` / `failed` are history statuses: the job returns to `unassigned` at once (D21). */
export const JOB_STATUSES = [
  'unassigned', 'assigned', 'accepted', 'in_production', 'ready', 'out_for_delivery', 'ready_for_pickup',
  'delivered', 'completed', 'rejected', 'cancelled', 'failed',
] as const;
export type JobStatus = (typeof JOB_STATUSES)[number];
export const JOB_STATUS_LABELS: Record<JobStatus, string> = {
  unassigned: 'تخصیص‌نیافته', assigned: 'تخصیص‌یافته (در انتظار پاسخ)', accepted: 'پذیرفته‌شده', in_production: 'در حال تولید',
  ready: 'آماده', out_for_delivery: 'در مسیر تحویل', ready_for_pickup: 'آماده تحویل حضوری', delivered: 'تحویل شد',
  completed: 'تکمیل‌شده', rejected: 'ردشده', cancelled: 'لغوشده', failed: 'ناموفق',
};
export const DELIVERY_METHODS = ['courier', 'provider', 'customer_pickup'] as const;
export type DeliveryMethod = (typeof DELIVERY_METHODS)[number];
export const DELIVERY_METHOD_LABELS: Record<DeliveryMethod, string> = { courier: 'ارسال با پیک', provider: 'ارسال توسط مجری', customer_pickup: 'تحویل حضوری' };
export const PICKUP_AT = ['point', 'provider'] as const;
export type PickupAt = (typeof PICKUP_AT)[number];
export const JOB_PRIORITIES = ['normal', 'high', 'urgent'] as const;
export type JobPriority = (typeof JOB_PRIORITIES)[number];
/** §59 rejection reasons. */
export const REJECT_REASONS = ['equipment_unavailable', 'paper_unavailable', 'capacity_full', 'machine_problem', 'outside_hours', 'other'] as const;
export type RejectReason = (typeof REJECT_REASONS)[number];
export const REJECT_REASON_LABELS: Record<RejectReason, string> = {
  equipment_unavailable: 'تجهیزات در دسترس نیست', paper_unavailable: 'کاغذ موجود نیست', capacity_full: 'ظرفیت تکمیل است',
  machine_problem: 'دستگاه خراب است', outside_hours: 'خارج از ساعت کاری', other: 'سایر',
};

/** Notification events of the dispatch flow (§68); each has a `push` template seeded by `seed:base`. */
export const DISPATCH_EVENTS = [
  'job_assigned', 'job_delivery_assigned', 'job_withdrawn', 'job_cancelled', 'job_changed', 'job_deadline',
  'job_rejected', 'job_timeout', 'job_failed', 'job_late', 'provider_unavailable',
  'assigned', 'ready_for_pickup',
] as const;
export type DispatchEvent = (typeof DISPATCH_EVENTS)[number];
export const NOTIFICATION_EVENTS = [...ORDER_STATUSES, ...DISPATCH_EVENTS] as const;
export type NotificationEvent = (typeof NOTIFICATION_EVENTS)[number];
export const NOTIFICATION_AUDIENCES = ['customer', 'provider', 'admin'] as const;
export type NotificationAudience = (typeof NOTIFICATION_AUDIENCES)[number];
const EVENT_AUDIENCE: Record<DispatchEvent, NotificationAudience> = {
  job_assigned: 'provider', job_delivery_assigned: 'provider', job_withdrawn: 'provider', job_cancelled: 'provider',
  job_changed: 'provider', job_deadline: 'provider',
  job_rejected: 'admin', job_timeout: 'admin', job_failed: 'admin', job_late: 'admin', provider_unavailable: 'admin',
  assigned: 'customer', ready_for_pickup: 'customer',
};
/** Who receives an event: order statuses go to the order's customer. */
export function audienceOf(event: NotificationEvent): NotificationAudience {
  return (EVENT_AUDIENCE as Record<string, NotificationAudience>)[event] ?? 'customer';
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `apps/api/src/modules/dispatch/job-model.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * v4.2 fulfilment model (spec §57–63, §72, §75; D10, D11, D21) — pure: the job flow, forward-only order mirroring,
 * dispatch / provider tabs, cancel stage, assignment history and the shared job types. No Mongo here.
 */
import type { Types } from 'mongoose';
import {
  STATUS_FLOW,
  type DeliveryMethod,
  type JobStatus,
  type OrderStatus,
  type PickupAt,
  type RejectReason,
} from '../../common/constants.js';

/** Who moves a job: the production provider, the delivering provider, the courier, an admin, or the scheduler. */
export type Actor = 'production' | 'delivery' | 'courier' | 'admin' | 'system';
export type ByRole = 'admin' | 'provider' | 'courier' | 'customer' | 'system';

/** A provider holds the job (it can be reassigned or taken back). */
export const HELD: JobStatus[] = ['assigned', 'accepted', 'in_production'];
/** Not finished yet. */
export const LIVE: JobStatus[] = ['unassigned', 'assigned', 'accepted', 'in_production', 'ready', 'out_for_delivery', 'ready_for_pickup'];
export const AFTER_READY: JobStatus[] = ['ready', 'out_for_delivery', 'ready_for_pickup', 'delivered', 'completed'];
/** A separate delivery provider sees the job from `ready` on. */
export const DELIVERY_VISIBLE: JobStatus[] = ['ready', 'out_for_delivery', 'delivered', 'completed'];
/** The production provider may download the order's files in these statuses (§65). */
export const FILE_STATUSES: JobStatus[] = ['assigned', 'accepted', 'in_production', 'ready', 'ready_for_pickup'];

export interface FlowCtx { method: DeliveryMethod; pickupAt?: PickupAt }
interface Step { from: JobStatus; to: JobStatus; who: Actor[]; when?: (c: FlowCtx) => boolean }
const byCourier = (c: FlowCtx) => c.method === 'courier';
const byProvider = (c: FlowCtx) => c.method === 'provider';
const atShop = (c: FlowCtx) => c.method === 'customer_pickup' && c.pickupAt === 'provider';
const atPickup = (c: FlowCtx) => c.method === 'customer_pickup';

/** Contract v4.2 «Job flow»; an `admin` may make every listed move whose condition holds (on behalf of anyone). */
const FLOW: Step[] = [
  { from: 'assigned', to: 'accepted', who: ['production'] },
  { from: 'accepted', to: 'in_production', who: ['production'] },
  { from: 'in_production', to: 'ready', who: ['production'] },
  { from: 'accepted', to: 'failed', who: ['production'] },
  { from: 'in_production', to: 'failed', who: ['production'] },
  { from: 'ready', to: 'out_for_delivery', who: ['delivery'], when: byProvider },
  { from: 'ready', to: 'out_for_delivery', who: ['courier'], when: byCourier },
  { from: 'ready', to: 'delivered', who: ['courier'], when: byCourier },
  { from: 'ready', to: 'ready_for_pickup', who: ['production'], when: atShop },
  { from: 'ready', to: 'ready_for_pickup', who: [], when: atPickup },
  { from: 'out_for_delivery', to: 'delivered', who: ['delivery'], when: byProvider },
  { from: 'out_for_delivery', to: 'delivered', who: ['courier'], when: byCourier },
  { from: 'ready_for_pickup', to: 'delivered', who: ['production'], when: atShop },
  { from: 'ready_for_pickup', to: 'delivered', who: [], when: atPickup },
  { from: 'delivered', to: 'completed', who: ['system'] },
];

export function canMove(from: JobStatus, to: JobStatus, who: Actor, ctx: FlowCtx): boolean {
  return FLOW.some((s) => s.from === from && s.to === to && (who === 'admin' || s.who.includes(who)) && (!s.when || s.when(ctx)));
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- order mirroring (D10)

/** The order status a job status maps to one-to-one (contract «Order status mapping»). */
export const ORDER_MIRROR: Partial<Record<JobStatus, OrderStatus>> = {
  accepted: 'confirmed', in_production: 'preparing', ready: 'packing', out_for_delivery: 'out_for_delivery', delivered: 'delivered',
};

/** Order statuses that may move forward to `target`: the ones before it on the happy path (never back, never out of an exception). */
export function mirrorSources(target: OrderStatus): OrderStatus[] {
  const i = STATUS_FLOW.indexOf(target);
  return i <= 0 ? [] : STATUS_FLOW.slice(0, i);
}

const BEFORE_PICKUP: OrderStatus[] = ['registered', 'confirmed', 'courier_assigned', 'awaiting_approval'];

/** A home-pickup order (school books, cartridge, device) enters production only after the courier collected it. */
export function productionBlocked(order: { status: OrderStatus; homePickup: boolean }): string | null {
  return order.homePickup && BEFORE_PICKUP.includes(order.status) ? 'اقلام سفارش هنوز از مشتری تحویل گرفته نشده است' : null;
}

export type CancelStage = 'before_production' | 'in_production' | 'after_production';

/** Where the work was when the order was cancelled or the job taken away — the M4 compensation basis. */
export function cancelStage(s: JobStatus): CancelStage {
  if (s === 'in_production') return 'in_production';
  if (AFTER_READY.includes(s)) return 'after_production';
  return 'before_production';
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- tabs

export const DISPATCH_TABS = ['new', 'unassigned', 'assigned', 'in_progress', 'ready', 'delivery', 'completed', 'cancelled'] as const;
export type DispatchTab = (typeof DISPATCH_TABS)[number];

/** §55 «مرکز تخصیص سفارش» sections. */
export function dispatchTabFilter(tab: DispatchTab): Record<string, unknown> {
  switch (tab) {
    case 'new': return { status: 'unassigned', attempts: 0 };
    case 'unassigned': return { status: 'unassigned' };
    case 'assigned': return { status: 'assigned' };
    case 'in_progress': return { status: { $in: ['accepted', 'in_production'] } };
    case 'ready': return { status: 'ready' };
    case 'delivery': return { status: { $in: ['out_for_delivery', 'ready_for_pickup', 'delivered'] } };
    case 'completed': return { status: 'completed' };
    case 'cancelled': return { status: 'cancelled' };
  }
}

export const DELIVERY_STATUSES = ['pending', 'ready', 'on_the_way', 'done'] as const;
export type DeliveryStatus = (typeof DELIVERY_STATUSES)[number];

/** «وضعیت تحویل» filter. */
export function deliveryStatusFilter(s: DeliveryStatus): Record<string, unknown> {
  switch (s) {
    case 'pending': return { status: { $in: ['unassigned', 'assigned', 'accepted', 'in_production'] } };
    case 'ready': return { status: 'ready' };
    case 'on_the_way': return { status: { $in: ['out_for_delivery', 'ready_for_pickup'] } };
    case 'done': return { status: { $in: ['delivered', 'completed'] } };
  }
}

export const PROVIDER_TABS = ['new', 'pending', 'accepted', 'in_production', 'ready', 'delivery', 'completed', 'cancelled'] as const;
export type ProviderTab = (typeof PROVIDER_TABS)[number];

/** §57 provider sections — always scoped to the caller. `cancelled` is built by the service (own cancelled jobs + withdrawn stubs). */
export function providerTabFilter(tab: Exclude<ProviderTab, 'cancelled'>, providerId: Types.ObjectId): Record<string, unknown> {
  const own = { providerId };
  const delivering = { deliveryProviderId: providerId, providerId: { $ne: providerId } };
  switch (tab) {
    case 'new': return { ...own, status: 'assigned', 'assignment.seenAt': { $exists: false } };
    case 'pending': return { ...own, status: 'assigned', 'assignment.seenAt': { $exists: true } };
    case 'accepted': return { ...own, status: 'accepted' };
    case 'in_production': return { ...own, status: 'in_production' };
    case 'ready': return { ...own, status: { $in: ['ready', 'ready_for_pickup'] } };
    case 'delivery': return { $or: [{ ...own, status: 'out_for_delivery' }, { ...delivering, status: { $in: ['ready', 'out_for_delivery'] } }] };
    case 'completed': return { $or: [{ ...own, status: { $in: ['delivered', 'completed'] } }, { ...delivering, status: { $in: ['delivered', 'completed'] } }] };
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- history (§75)

export const JOB_ACTIONS = [
  'created', 'migrated', 'assigned', 'reassigned', 'accepted', 'rejected', 'timeout', 'unassigned', 'status',
  'failed', 'delivery_changed', 'updated', 'cancelled', 'completed',
] as const;
export type JobAction = (typeof JOB_ACTIONS)[number];

/** One append-only history entry; `providerId` = the provider holding the job at that moment. */
export interface JobHistoryEntry {
  at: Date;
  action: JobAction;
  status: JobStatus;
  providerId?: Types.ObjectId;
  deliveryProviderId?: Types.ObjectId;
  previousProviderId?: Types.ObjectId;
  by?: Types.ObjectId;
  byRole: ByRole;
  reason?: string;
  reasonCode?: RejectReason;
  override?: boolean;
  /** The previous provider's stage when the job was taken away / cancelled (M4). */
  stage?: CancelStage;
}

export interface AssignmentRecord {
  providerId: string;
  assignedBy?: string;
  assignedAt: Date;
  acceptedAt?: Date;
  rejectedAt?: Date;
  completedAt?: Date;
  endedAt?: Date;
  reason?: string;
  reasonCode?: RejectReason;
  override?: boolean;
  outcome: 'pending' | 'active' | 'rejected' | 'timeout' | 'withdrawn' | 'failed' | 'cancelled' | 'completed';
}

/** §75 view: one record per assignment (provider, assigned by/at, accepted/rejected/completed at, reason, outcome). */
export function assignmentHistory(history: JobHistoryEntry[]): AssignmentRecord[] {
  const out: AssignmentRecord[] = [];
  let open: AssignmentRecord | undefined;
  const end = (outcome: AssignmentRecord['outcome'], e: JobHistoryEntry) => {
    if (!open) return;
    open.outcome = outcome;
    open.endedAt = e.at;
    if (e.reason) open.reason = e.reason;
    if (e.reasonCode) open.reasonCode = e.reasonCode;
    open = undefined;
  };
  for (const e of history) {
    if (e.action === 'assigned' || e.action === 'reassigned' || e.action === 'migrated') {
      end('withdrawn', e);
      open = {
        providerId: String(e.providerId),
        ...(e.by ? { assignedBy: String(e.by) } : {}),
        assignedAt: e.at,
        outcome: e.action === 'migrated' ? 'active' : 'pending',
        ...(e.override ? { override: true } : {}),
      };
      out.push(open);
    } else if (e.action === 'accepted' && open) {
      open.acceptedAt = e.at;
      open.outcome = 'active';
    } else if (e.action === 'rejected' && open) {
      open.rejectedAt = e.at;
      end('rejected', e);
    } else if (e.action === 'timeout') end('timeout', e);
    else if (e.action === 'unassigned') end('withdrawn', e);
    else if (e.action === 'failed') end('failed', e);
    else if (e.action === 'cancelled') end('cancelled', e);
    else if (e.action === 'completed' && open) {
      open.completedAt = e.at;
      end('completed', e);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- legacy, deadline, labels

/** A v1 `centerId` assignment (no acceptance existed) becomes a job at the order's stage. */
export function legacyJobStatus(s: OrderStatus): JobStatus {
  if (s === 'preparing' || s === 'binding' || s === 'extras' || s === 'qc') return 'in_production';
  if (s === 'packing') return 'ready';
  if (s === 'out_for_delivery') return 'out_for_delivery';
  return 'accepted';
}

export function deadlineFor(createdAt: Date, urgent: boolean, s: { leadHours: number; urgentLeadHours: number }): Date {
  return new Date(createdAt.getTime() + (urgent ? s.urgentLeadHours : s.leadHours) * 3_600_000);
}

const BEFORE_READY: JobStatus[] = ['unassigned', 'assigned', 'accepted', 'in_production'];

/** Ready after the deadline, or still not ready while the deadline passed (cancelled jobs are never late). */
export function isLate(job: { status: JobStatus; deadline: Date; readyAt?: Date }, now: Date): boolean {
  if (job.status === 'cancelled') return false;
  const t = job.readyAt ?? (BEFORE_READY.includes(job.status) ? now : undefined);
  return !!t && t.getTime() > new Date(job.deadline).getTime();
}

const CUSTOMER_LABEL: Record<JobStatus, string> = {
  unassigned: 'در صف تولید', assigned: 'در صف تولید', rejected: 'در صف تولید', failed: 'در صف تولید',
  accepted: 'تأیید شد', in_production: 'در حال تولید', ready: 'آماده شد', ready_for_pickup: 'آماده تحویل حضوری',
  out_for_delivery: 'در مسیر تحویل', delivered: 'تحویل شد', completed: 'تحویل شد', cancelled: 'لغو شد',
};
/** The customer-facing fulfilment label (never names a provider, never shows a reassignment). */
export function customerFulfillmentLabel(s: JobStatus): string {
  return CUSTOMER_LABEL[s];
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- provider price snapshot (§40, §53)

export interface ProviderPricingLine { serviceIndex: number; comboKey: string; label: string; versionId: string; version: number; unitPrice: number; quantity: number; subtotal: number }
export interface ProviderPricingMissing { serviceIndex?: number; childIndex?: number; comboKey: string | null; label: string }
export interface ProviderQuote { lines: ProviderPricingLine[]; missing: ProviderPricingMissing[]; subtotal: number | null }
export interface ProviderPricingSnapshot {
  providerId: string;
  snapshotAt: string;
  currency: 'toman';
  lines: ProviderPricingLine[];
  missing: ProviderPricingMissing[];
  providerSubtotal: number | null;
  delivery: { providerId: string; fee: number } | null;
  /** provider_payout_basis (M4): providerSubtotal + the delivery fee when the production provider delivers. */
  payoutBasis: number | null;
  confirmedMissing: boolean;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Extend `apps/api/test/fake-model.ts`**

Replace the `updateOne` helper and the `findOneAndUpdate` / `create` members inside `fakeModel`:

```ts
  /** An upsert inserts the filter's equality fields + `$setOnInsert` + the rest of the update. */
  const insertFromUpsert = (filter: Doc, update: Doc) => {
    const now = new Date();
    const d: Doc = { _id: new Types.ObjectId(), createdAt: now, updatedAt: now, ...clone(equalityFields(filter)) };
    docs.push(d);
    applyUpdate(d, { ...update, $set: { ...((update.$setOnInsert as Doc) ?? {}), ...((update.$set as Doc) ?? {}) } });
    return d;
  };
  const updateOne = async (filter: Doc, update: Doc, options?: { upsert?: boolean }) => {
    await Promise.resolve();
    const d = first(filter);
    if (!d && options?.upsert) {
      insertFromUpsert(filter, update);
      return { matchedCount: 0, modifiedCount: 0, upsertedCount: 1 };
    }
    if (!d) return { matchedCount: 0, modifiedCount: 0, upsertedCount: 0 };
    applyUpdate(d, update);
    return { matchedCount: 1, modifiedCount: 1, upsertedCount: 0 };
  };
  const create = async (doc: Doc) => {
    const now = new Date();
    const d = { _id: new Types.ObjectId(), createdAt: now, updatedAt: now, ...clone(doc) };
    docs.push(d);
    return out(d);
  };
```

and in the returned object:

```ts
      if (!d) return null;
      applyUpdate(d, update);
      return out(d);
    }),
    // …
    create: vi.fn(create),
    insertMany: vi.fn(async (list: Doc[]) => Promise.all(list.map(create))),
```

```ts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------- v4.2 dispatch (M3)

export interface DispatchSettings {
  /** Minutes a provider has to accept/reject before the job returns to «تخصیص‌نیافته» (D21). */
  responseTimeoutMinutes: number;
  leadHours: number;
  urgentLeadHours: number;
  /** 0 = no deadline reminder. */
  deadlineReminderMinutes: number;
  /** 0 = admins complete delivered jobs by hand. */
  autoCompleteHours: number;
}
export const DEFAULT_DISPATCH: DispatchSettings = { responseTimeoutMinutes: 120, leadHours: 48, urgentLeadHours: 24, deadlineReminderMinutes: 120, autoCompleteHours: 24 };
export const defaultDispatch = (): DispatchSettings => ({ ...DEFAULT_DISPATCH });

/** «محل‌های تحویل حضوری» of Digital Sarv (D17). `phone` in ASCII digits. */
export interface PickupPoint { id: string; name: string; address: string; phone: string; hours: string; on: boolean }
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `Job` schema** — `apps/api/src/modules/dispatch/job.schema.ts`

```ts
import { Prop, Schema, SchemaFactory } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
import { HydratedDocument, Schema as MongooseSchema, Types } from 'mongoose';
import {
  DELIVERY_METHODS, JOB_PRIORITIES, JOB_STATUSES, PICKUP_AT,
  type DeliveryMethod, type JobPriority, type JobStatus, type PickupAt,
} from '../../common/constants.js';
import type { ProviderService } from '../centers/provider-model.js';
import type { ByRole, CancelStage, JobHistoryEntry, ProviderPricingSnapshot } from './job-model.js';

/** The current assignment (replaced on every assign/reassign, removed when the job returns to the queue). */
@Schema({ _id: false })
export class JobAssignment {
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId, required: true }) id: Types.ObjectId;
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId, required: true }) providerId: Types.ObjectId;
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId }) assignedBy?: Types.ObjectId;
  @Prop({ type: Date, required: true }) assignedAt: Date;
  /** Unanswered after this → back to «تخصیص‌نیافته» (D21). */
  @Prop({ type: Date, required: true }) respondBy: Date;
  @Prop({ type: Date }) seenAt?: Date;
  @Prop({ type: Date }) acceptedAt?: Date;
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.Mixed }) override?: { reason: string; problems: string[] };
}
export const JobAssignmentSchema = SchemaFactory.createForClass(JobAssignment);

/** v4.2 fulfilment of (a part of) an order — V1: exactly one per order, every line (D11, D18). */
@Schema({ collection: 'jobs', timestamps: true })
export class Job {
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId, required: true }) orderId: Types.ObjectId;
  /** Job number within the order (V1: 1). Splitting later adds 2, 3, … without a migration. */
  @Prop({ type: Number, default: 1 }) seq: number;
  @Prop({ type: String, required: true }) orderCode: string;
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId, required: true }) customerId: Types.ObjectId;
  @Prop({ type: String, default: '' }) customerName: string;
  @Prop({ type: String, default: '' }) customerPhone: string;
  @Prop({ type: String }) zone?: string;
  @Prop({ type: Boolean, default: true }) homePickup: boolean;
  /** Provider services the job needs (filters, capability). */
  @Prop({ type: [String], default: [] }) services: ProviderService[];
  @Prop({ type: [Number], default: [] }) childIndexes: number[];
  @Prop({ type: [Number], default: [] }) lineIndexes: number[];
  @Prop({ type: String, enum: JOB_STATUSES, default: 'unassigned' }) status: JobStatus;
  @Prop({ type: String, enum: JOB_PRIORITIES, default: 'normal' }) priority: JobPriority;
  @Prop({ type: Date, required: true }) deadline: Date;
  /** The production provider holding the job. */
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId }) providerId?: Types.ObjectId;
  /** §61: who delivers when `deliveryMethod = 'provider'`. */
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId }) deliveryProviderId?: Types.ObjectId;
  @Prop({ type: String, enum: DELIVERY_METHODS, default: 'courier' }) deliveryMethod: DeliveryMethod;
  @Prop({ type: String, enum: PICKUP_AT }) pickupAt?: PickupAt;
  @Prop({ type: JobAssignmentSchema }) assignment?: JobAssignment;
  @Prop({ type: Number, default: 0 }) attempts: number;
  /** Providers that held the job and lost it (rejected, timed out, taken away) — their «لغوشده» stubs. */
  @Prop({ type: [MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId], default: [] }) releasedProviderIds: Types.ObjectId[];
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.Mixed }) providerPricing?: ProviderPricingSnapshot;
  @Prop({ type: [MongooseSchema.Types.Mixed], default: [] }) pricingHistory: ProviderPricingSnapshot[];
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.Mixed, default: () => ({}) }) units: Partial<Record<ProviderService, number>>;
  /** B/W + colour sheets × copies (capacity unit). */
  @Prop({ type: Number, default: 0 }) sheets: number;
  @Prop({ type: [MongooseSchema.Types.Mixed], default: [] }) history: JobHistoryEntry[];
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.Mixed }) cancel?: { at: Date; stage: CancelStage; providerId?: Types.ObjectId; byRole: ByRole };
  @Prop({ type: Date }) readyAt?: Date;
  @Prop({ type: Date }) deliveredAt?: Date;
  @Prop({ type: Date }) completedAt?: Date;
  @Prop({ type: Date }) deadlineNotifiedAt?: Date;
  @Prop({ type: Date }) lateAlertedAt?: Date;
  @Prop({ type: Date }) unavailableAlertedAt?: Date;
  @Prop({ type: Date, required: true }) orderCreatedAt: Date;

  createdAt: Date;
  updatedAt: Date;
}

export type JobDocument = HydratedDocument<Job>;
export const JobSchema = SchemaFactory.createForClass(Job);
JobSchema.index({ orderId: 1, seq: 1 }, { unique: true });
JobSchema.index({ status: 1, deadline: 1 });
JobSchema.index({ providerId: 1, status: 1 });
JobSchema.index({ deliveryProviderId: 1, status: 1 }, { sparse: true });
JobSchema.index({ 'assignment.respondBy': 1 }, { sparse: true });
JobSchema.index({ releasedProviderIds: 1 });
JobSchema.index({ orderCreatedAt: -1 });
```

Register it in `apps/api/src/database/database.module.ts`: `import { Job, JobSchema } from '../modules/dispatch/job.schema.js';` and `{ name: Job.name, schema: JobSchema },` in `forFeature`.

```ts
}
```

- Produces: `OrderLines`, `LineRequirement`, `jobRequirements(order): LineRequirement[]`, `capabilityProblems(reqs, caps, paperNames?): string[]`, `JobUnits`, `jobUnits(order): JobUnits`, `providerServicesOf(order): ProviderService[]`, `openAt(hours, now): boolean | null`, `ProviderFacts`, `Usage`, `CheckKey`, `ProviderCheck`, `CapacityLeft`, `CheckResult`, `capacityLeft(p, usage)`, `checkProvider(p, reqs, job, usage, ctx): CheckResult`, `SERVICE_LABEL`.
