```ts
import { capabilityProblems, checkProvider, jobRequirements, jobUnits, openAt, providerServicesOf, type ProviderFacts } from './eligibility.js';

const NOW = new Date('2026-10-17T07:30:00Z'); // Saturday 11:00 Tehran
const bw = (sizes: string[], sides = ['single', 'double'], addons: string[] = []) => ({ service: 'black_white_printing', sizes, colorTypes: [], papers: [], sides, addons });
const color = (sizes: string[], papers: string[], addons: string[] = []) => ({ service: 'color_printing', sizes, colorTypes: ['text', 'full'], papers, sides: ['single', 'double'], addons });
const provider = (id: string, capabilities: unknown[], o: Partial<ProviderFacts> = {}): ProviderFacts => ({
  id, status: 'active', availableForOrders: true, workingHours: [], serviceZoneIds: [], capacity: { perService: [] }, capacityPerDay: 0,
  capabilities: capabilities as ProviderFacts['capabilities'], canDeliver: false, ...o,
});
// §86 test providers
const A = provider('A', [color(['A3'], ['glossy200', 'tahrir80'])]);
const B = provider('B', [bw(['A4'])]);
const C = provider('C', [color(['A4'], ['glossy200'])]);
const D = provider('D', [{ service: 'school', sizes: [], colorTypes: [], papers: [], sides: [], addons: [] }, { service: 'docs', sizes: [], colorTypes: [], papers: [], sides: [], addons: [] }]);
const E = provider('E', [color(['A3', 'A4'], ['tahrir80'])]);
const F = provider('F', [], { canDeliver: true });
const ALL = [A, B, C, D, E, F];
const empty = { sheets: 0, jobs: 0, units: {} };

const glossyOrder = {
  children: [],
  services: [{ kind: 'color_printing', label: 'چاپ رنگی', spec: { size: 'A4', sides: 'single', colorType: 'full', paper: 'glossy200', copies: 1, binding: 'none', staple: false, laminate: 'none' },
    pricing: { lines: [{ comboKey: 'color|A4|full|glossy200|single', quantity: 200 }] } }],
};
const capable = (order: Parameters<typeof jobRequirements>[0]) => {
  const reqs = jobRequirements(order);
  return ALL.filter((p) => checkProvider(p, reqs, jobUnits(order), empty, { now: NOW, orderZoneId: null }).assignable).map((p) => p.id);
};

describe('eligibility — §86 six providers: a provider cannot receive an order it cannot fulfil', () => {
  it('§88 order (A4 تمام‌رنگی گلاسه ۲۰۰ گرم, 200 sheets): only C', () => {
    expect(capable(glossyOrder)).toEqual(['C']);
  });
  it('A3 colour on plain paper: A and E; A4 B/W: B; school binding: D; nothing ever goes to the delivery-only F', () => {
    expect(capable({ services: [{ kind: 'color_printing', spec: { size: 'A3', sides: 'double', colorType: 'text', paper: 'tahrir80' } }] })).toEqual(['A', 'E']);
    expect(capable({ services: [{ kind: 'black_white_printing', spec: { size: 'A4', sides: 'double' } }] })).toEqual(['B']);
    expect(capable({ children: [{ name: 'سارا', books: 9 }] })).toEqual(['D']);
    expect(capable({ services: [{ kind: 'flyer', spec: { qty: 500 } }] })).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('add-ons must be capabilities: B/W with spiral is not B', () => {
    expect(capable({ services: [{ kind: 'black_white_printing', spec: { size: 'A4', sides: 'single', binding: 'spiral' } }] })).toEqual([]);
    const problems = capabilityProblems(jobRequirements({ services: [{ kind: 'black_white_printing', label: 'چاپ سیاه‌وسفید', spec: { size: 'A4', sides: 'single', binding: 'spiral', laminate: 'all' } }] }), B.capabilities);
    expect(problems).toEqual(['چاپ سیاه‌وسفید: فنری پشتیبانی نمی‌شود', 'چاپ سیاه‌وسفید: لمینت هر برگ پشتیبانی نمی‌شود']);
  });
  it('every line must match: school + glossy colour needs one provider doing both', () => {
    expect(capable({ children: [{ name: 'سارا', books: 9 }], services: glossyOrder.services })).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('a legacy «چاپ اسناد» line is read as its v4 kind (paper unknown → not checked)', () => {
    expect(capable({ services: [{ kind: 'print', spec: { ink: 'bw', size: 'A4', sides: 'single', binding: 'none' } }] })).toEqual(['B']);
  });
});

describe('eligibility — soft and hard checks', () => {
  const reqs = jobRequirements(glossyOrder);
  const units = jobUnits(glossyOrder);
  it('inactive/suspended are hard; busy, not taking orders, hours, zone and capacity are soft', () => {
    expect(checkProvider({ ...C, status: 'suspended' }, reqs, units, empty, { now: NOW, orderZoneId: null }).assignable).toBe(false);
    const busy = checkProvider({ ...C, status: 'busy', availableForOrders: false }, reqs, units, empty, { now: NOW, orderZoneId: null });
    expect(busy).toMatchObject({ eligible: false, assignable: true });
    expect(busy.problems).toEqual(['مجری مشغول است', 'مجری فعلاً سفارش جدید نمی‌پذیرد']);
  });
  it('working hours by Tehran weekday and time; none configured = ok', () => {
    expect(openAt([], NOW)).toBeNull();
    expect(openAt([{ weekday: 6, from: '08:00', to: '20:00' }], NOW)).toBe(true);
    expect(openAt([{ weekday: 6, from: '12:00', to: '20:00' }], NOW)).toBe(false);
    expect(openAt([{ weekday: 0, from: '08:00', to: '20:00' }], NOW)).toBe(false);
  });
  it('zone: no zones = all; unknown order zone = ok; otherwise must be served', () => {
    const z = { ...C, serviceZoneIds: ['z1'] };
    expect(checkProvider(z, reqs, units, empty, { now: NOW, orderZoneId: 'z2' }).checks.find((c) => c.key === 'zone')).toMatchObject({ ok: false, hard: false });
    expect(checkProvider(z, reqs, units, empty, { now: NOW, orderZoneId: null }).eligible).toBe(true);
    expect(checkProvider(z, reqs, units, empty, { now: NOW, orderZoneId: 'z1' }).eligible).toBe(true);
  });
  it('capacity: daily sheets, legacy jobs per day and per-service units; remaining capacity is reported', () => {
    const p = { ...C, capacity: { dailySheets: 1000, perService: [{ service: 'color_printing' as const, perDay: 500 }] }, capacityPerDay: 3 };
    const ok = checkProvider(p, reqs, units, { sheets: 700, jobs: 2, units: { color_printing: 250 } }, { now: NOW, orderZoneId: null });
    expect(ok.eligible).toBe(true);
    expect(ok.capacityLeft).toEqual({ sheets: 300, jobs: 1, perService: [{ service: 'color_printing', left: 250 }] });
    const full = checkProvider(p, reqs, units, { sheets: 900, jobs: 3, units: { color_printing: 400 } }, { now: NOW, orderZoneId: null });
    expect(full).toMatchObject({ eligible: false, assignable: true });
    expect(full.checks.find((c) => c.key === 'capacity')!.message).toContain('۱۰۰ برگ باقی مانده');
  });
  it('provider delivery needs canDeliver (hard) only when asked for', () => {
    expect(checkProvider(C, reqs, units, empty, { now: NOW, orderZoneId: null, needsDelivery: true }).assignable).toBe(false);
    expect(checkProvider({ ...C, canDeliver: true }, reqs, units, empty, { now: NOW, orderZoneId: null, needsDelivery: true }).eligible).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('job units', () => {

});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `apps/api/src/modules/dispatch/eligibility.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * v4.2 capability engine (spec §51–54, §86): "who can fulfil this?" — pure. Capability is always a hard check
 * (a provider never receives what it cannot do); availability, hours, zone and capacity are soft (admin override, §71).
 */
import { tehranMinutes, tehranWeekday, tehranYmd } from '../../common/utils/dates.js';
import { fa } from '../../common/utils/fa.js';
import type { Capability, CapabilityAddon, Capacity, ProviderService, ProviderStatus, WorkingHours } from '../centers/provider-model.js';
import type { ColorType, PrintSize, Side } from '../price-book/combo.js';
import { legacyPrintToV4 } from '../pricing/print-v4.js';

export interface OrderLines {
  children?: { name?: string; books?: number }[];
  services?: { kind: string; label?: string; spec?: unknown; pricing?: { lines: { comboKey: string; quantity: number }[] } }[];
}
export interface LineRequirement { line: string; service: ProviderService; size?: PrintSize; side?: Side; colorType?: ColorType; paper?: string; addons: CapabilityAddon[] }

export const SERVICE_LABEL: Record<ProviderService, string> = {
  black_white_printing: 'چاپ سیاه‌وسفید', color_printing: 'چاپ رنگی', docs: 'پایان‌نامه و صحافی', school: 'فنری کتاب مدرسه',
  flyer: 'تراکت', cart: 'شارژ کارتریج', repair: 'تعمیر پرینتر',
};
const ADDON_LABEL: Record<CapabilityAddon, string> = { mixed: 'ترکیبی', spiral: 'فنری', glue: 'ته‌چسب', hardcover: 'گالینگور', staple: 'منگنه', lam_cover: 'لمینت جلد', lam_sheet: 'لمینت هر برگ' };
const OTHER: ProviderService[] = ['docs', 'flyer', 'cart', 'repair'];
const obj = (v: unknown): Record<string, unknown> => (v && typeof v === 'object' ? (v as Record<string, unknown>) : {});

/** A legacy v3 «چاپ اسناد» line is read as its v4 kind. */
function v4Line(s: { kind: string; spec?: unknown }): { kind: string; spec: Record<string, unknown> } {
  if (s.kind !== 'print') return { kind: s.kind, spec: obj(s.spec) };
  const d = legacyPrintToV4(obj(s.spec) as never);
  return { kind: d.kind, spec: obj(d.spec) };
}

function printAddons(spec: Record<string, unknown>): CapabilityAddon[] {
  const out: CapabilityAddon[] = [];
  const binding = spec.binding;
  if (binding === 'spiral' || binding === 'glue' || binding === 'hardcover') out.push(binding);
  if (spec.staple === true) out.push('staple');
  if (spec.laminate === 'cover') out.push('lam_cover');
  if (spec.laminate === 'all') out.push('lam_sheet');
  if (spec.mixed === true) out.push('mixed');
  return out;
}

/** What every line of the order needs from a provider. */
export function jobRequirements(order: OrderLines): LineRequirement[] {
  const reqs: LineRequirement[] = (order.children ?? []).map((c, i) => ({ line: `فنری کتاب مدرسه — ${c.name || `فرزند ${fa(i + 1)}`}`, service: 'school' as const, addons: [] }));
  for (const s of order.services ?? []) {
    const { kind, spec } = v4Line(s);
    if (kind === 'black_white_printing' || kind === 'color_printing') {
      reqs.push({
        line: s.label || SERVICE_LABEL[kind], service: kind, size: spec.size as PrintSize, side: spec.sides as Side,
        ...(kind === 'color_printing' ? { colorType: spec.colorType as ColorType, paper: typeof spec.paper === 'string' ? spec.paper : '' } : {}),
        addons: printAddons(spec),
      });
    } else if ((OTHER as string[]).includes(kind)) {
      reqs.push({ line: s.label || SERVICE_LABEL[kind as ProviderService], service: kind as ProviderService, addons: [] });
    }
  }
  return reqs;
}

export function providerServicesOf(order: OrderLines): ProviderService[] {
  return [...new Set(jobRequirements(order).map((r) => r.service))];
}

/** Persian problems, one per unmet need (empty = capable). */
export function capabilityProblems(reqs: LineRequirement[], caps: Capability[], paperNames?: Map<string, string>): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const r of reqs) {
    const c = caps.find((x) => x.service === r.service);
    if (!c) {
      out.push(`${r.line}: «${SERVICE_LABEL[r.service]}» در توانمندی‌های مجری نیست`);
      continue;
    }
    if (r.service !== 'black_white_printing' && r.service !== 'color_printing') continue;
    if (r.size && !c.sizes.includes(r.size)) out.push(`${r.line}: قطع ${r.size} پشتیبانی نمی‌شود`);
    if (r.side && !c.sides.includes(r.side)) out.push(`${r.line}: چاپ ${r.side === 'double' ? 'دورو' : 'یک‌رو'} پشتیبانی نمی‌شود`);
    if (r.colorType && !c.colorTypes.includes(r.colorType)) out.push(`${r.line}: ${r.colorType === 'text' ? 'متن رنگی' : 'تمام‌رنگی'} پشتیبانی نمی‌شود`);
    if (r.paper && !c.papers.includes(r.paper)) out.push(`${r.line}: کاغذ ${paperNames?.get(r.paper) ?? r.paper} پشتیبانی نمی‌شود`);
    for (const a of r.addons) if (!c.addons.includes(a)) out.push(`${r.line}: ${ADDON_LABEL[a]} پشتیبانی نمی‌شود`);
  }
  return [...new Set(out)];
}

export interface JobUnits { sheets: number; units: Partial<Record<ProviderService, number>> }

/** Capacity units of the order (contract «Eligibility»: print = sheets × copies, school = books, docs = copies, …). */
export function jobUnits(order: OrderLines): JobUnits {
  const units: Partial<Record<ProviderService, number>> = {};
  const add = (s: ProviderService, n: number) => {
    if (n > 0) units[s] = (units[s] ?? 0) + n;
  };
  let sheets = 0;
  for (const c of order.children ?? []) add('school', c.books ?? 0);
  for (const s of order.services ?? []) {
    const spec = obj(s.spec);
    if (s.kind === 'black_white_printing' || s.kind === 'color_printing') {
      const n = (s.pricing?.lines ?? []).filter((l) => /^(bw|color)\|/.test(l.comboKey)).reduce((t, l) => t + l.quantity, 0);
      sheets += n;
      add(s.kind, n);
    } else if (s.kind === 'docs') add('docs', Math.max(1, Number(spec.copies) || 1));
    else if (s.kind === 'flyer') add('flyer', Math.max(0, Number(spec.qty) || 0));
    else if (s.kind === 'cart') add('cart', Math.max(1, Number(spec.count) || 1));
    else if (s.kind === 'repair') add('repair', 1);
  }
  return { sheets, units };
}

const hm = (t: string) => Number(t.slice(0, 2)) * 60 + Number(t.slice(3));

/** Open at `now` (Tehran weekday and time)? `null` = no working hours configured. */
export function openAt(hours: WorkingHours[], now: Date): boolean | null {
  if (!hours.length) return null;
  const day = tehranWeekday(tehranYmd(now));
  const m = tehranMinutes(now);
  return hours.some((h) => h.weekday === day && hm(h.from) <= m && m < hm(h.to));
}

export interface ProviderFacts {
  id: string; status: ProviderStatus; availableForOrders: boolean; workingHours: WorkingHours[]; serviceZoneIds: string[];
  capacity: Capacity; capacityPerDay: number; capabilities: Capability[]; canDeliver: boolean;
}
/** Today's assigned load of a provider (Tehran day, by `assignment.assignedAt`). */
export interface Usage { sheets: number; jobs: number; units: Partial<Record<ProviderService, number>> }
export type CheckKey = 'capability' | 'status' | 'available' | 'hours' | 'zone' | 'capacity' | 'delivery';
export interface ProviderCheck { key: CheckKey; ok: boolean; hard: boolean; message: string }
export interface CapacityLeft { sheets: number | null; jobs: number | null; perService: { service: ProviderService; left: number }[] }
export interface CheckResult { checks: ProviderCheck[]; eligible: boolean; assignable: boolean; problems: string[]; hardProblems: string[]; capacityLeft: CapacityLeft; openNow: boolean | null }

const STATUS_MSG: Record<ProviderStatus, string> = { active: 'فعال', busy: 'مجری مشغول است', inactive: 'مجری غیرفعال است', suspended: 'مجری تعلیق شده است' };

export function capacityLeft(p: Pick<ProviderFacts, 'capacity' | 'capacityPerDay'>, usage: Usage): CapacityLeft {
  const daily = p.capacity?.dailySheets;
  return {
    sheets: typeof daily === 'number' ? Math.max(0, daily - usage.sheets) : null,
    jobs: p.capacityPerDay > 0 ? Math.max(0, p.capacityPerDay - usage.jobs) : null,
    perService: (p.capacity?.perService ?? []).map((s) => ({ service: s.service, left: Math.max(0, s.perDay - (usage.units[s.service] ?? 0)) })),
  };
}

/** Every check of one provider for one job (contract v4.2 «Eligibility»). */
export function checkProvider(
  p: ProviderFacts, reqs: LineRequirement[], job: JobUnits, usage: Usage,
  ctx: { now: Date; orderZoneId: string | null; needsDelivery?: boolean; paperNames?: Map<string, string> },
): CheckResult {
  const checks: ProviderCheck[] = [];
  const cap = capabilityProblems(reqs, p.capabilities ?? [], ctx.paperNames);
  checks.push({ key: 'capability', hard: true, ok: cap.length === 0, message: cap.length ? cap.join(' · ') : 'توانمندی‌ها با سفارش مطابقت دارد' });
  checks.push({ key: 'status', hard: p.status === 'inactive' || p.status === 'suspended', ok: p.status === 'active', message: STATUS_MSG[p.status] ?? STATUS_MSG.inactive });
  checks.push({ key: 'available', hard: false, ok: p.availableForOrders !== false, message: p.availableForOrders !== false ? 'سفارش جدید می‌پذیرد' : 'مجری فعلاً سفارش جدید نمی‌پذیرد' });
  const open = openAt(p.workingHours ?? [], ctx.now);
  checks.push({ key: 'hours', hard: false, ok: open !== false, message: open === null ? 'ساعت کاری ثبت نشده است' : open ? 'در ساعت کاری' : 'خارج از ساعت کاری' });
  const zones = p.serviceZoneIds ?? [];
  const zoneOk = !zones.length || !ctx.orderZoneId || zones.includes(ctx.orderZoneId);
  checks.push({ key: 'zone', hard: false, ok: zoneOk, message: !zones.length ? 'همه مناطق' : !ctx.orderZoneId ? 'منطقه سفارش مشخص نیست' : zoneOk ? 'در منطقه خدمت' : 'خارج از منطقه خدمت مجری' });
  const left = capacityLeft(p, usage);
  const over: string[] = [];
  if (left.sheets !== null && job.sheets > left.sheets) over.push(`ظرفیت امروز: ${fa(left.sheets)} برگ باقی مانده، این سفارش ${fa(job.sheets)} برگ است`);
  if (left.jobs !== null && left.jobs < 1) over.push('ظرفیت روزانه سفارش تکمیل است');
  for (const s of left.perService) {
    const need = job.units[s.service] ?? 0;
    if (need > s.left) over.push(`ظرفیت ${SERVICE_LABEL[s.service]}: ${fa(s.left)} باقی مانده، نیاز ${fa(need)}`);
  }
  checks.push({ key: 'capacity', hard: false, ok: over.length === 0, message: over.length ? over.join(' · ') : 'ظرفیت کافی' });
  if (ctx.needsDelivery) {
    const ok = !!p.canDeliver && p.status === 'active';
    checks.push({ key: 'delivery', hard: true, ok, message: ok ? 'امکان ارسال دارد' : 'مجری امکان ارسال ندارد' });
  }
  const failed = checks.filter((c) => !c.ok);
  return {
    checks, eligible: failed.length === 0, assignable: !failed.some((c) => c.hard),
    problems: failed.map((c) => c.message), hardProblems: failed.filter((c) => c.hard).map((c) => c.message),
    capacityLeft: left, openNow: open,
  };
}
```

```ts
import { pricingSnapshot, providerLineLabel, providerQuote } from './provider-pricing.js';

const names = { papers: new Map([['glossy200', 'گلاسه ۲۰۰ گرم']]), ranges: new Map([['6650f0000000000000000001', { minPages: 10, maxPages: 12 }]]) };
const labelOf = (k: string) => providerLineLabel(k, names);
const bwLine = (comboKey: string, quantity: number) => ({ comboKey, versionId: 'c1', version: 1, unitPrice: 3800, quantity, subtotal: 3800 * quantity });

describe('provider price of a job (§52–53)', () => {
  it('sums the provider’s own effective unit prices × the customer line quantities', () => {
    const order = { services: [{ kind: 'black_white_printing', label: 'چاپ سیاه‌وسفید', pricing: { lines: [bwLine('bw|A4|double', 10), bwLine('addon|staple', 2)] } }] };
    const q = providerQuote(order, { 'bw|A4|double': { price: 3000, versionId: 'p1', version: 4 }, 'addon|staple': { price: 1500, versionId: 'p2', version: 1 } }, labelOf);
    expect(q.subtotal).toBe(10 * 3000 + 2 * 1500);
    expect(q.missing).toEqual([]);
    expect(q.lines[0]).toEqual({ serviceIndex: 0, comboKey: 'bw|A4|double', label: 'چاپ سیاه‌وسفید · A4 · دورو', versionId: 'p1', version: 4, unitPrice: 3000, quantity: 10, subtotal: 30000 });
  });
  it('a combo without a provider price is «missing» and the subtotal is null — never 0, never a fallback', () => {
    const order = { services: [{ kind: 'color_printing', label: 'چاپ رنگی', pricing: { lines: [bwLine('color|A4|full|glossy200|single', 200)] } }] };
    const q = providerQuote(order, { 'bw|A4|single': { price: 3500, versionId: 'x', version: 1 } }, labelOf);
    expect(q).toMatchObject({ subtotal: null, lines: [] });
    expect(q.missing).toEqual([{ serviceIndex: 0, comboKey: 'color|A4|full|glossy200|single', label: 'چاپ رنگی: چاپ رنگی · A4 · تمام‌رنگی · گلاسه ۲۰۰ گرم · یک‌رو' }]);
  });
  it('school binding, services without price-book lines and non-price-book lines are missing', () => {
    const order = {
      children: [{ name: 'سارا' }],
      services: [
        { kind: 'flyer', label: 'تراکت' },
        { kind: 'black_white_printing', label: 'چاپ سیاه‌وسفید', pricing: { lines: [bwLine('bw|A4|single', 4), { ...bwLine('extra|trim', 1), versionId: '' }] } },
      ],
    };
    const q = providerQuote(order, { 'bw|A4|single': { price: 3000, versionId: 'p', version: 1 } }, labelOf);
    expect(q.subtotal).toBeNull();
    expect(q.missing.map((m) => m.label)).toEqual(['فنری کتاب مدرسه — سارا', 'تراکت', 'چاپ سیاه‌وسفید: خدمات اضافی']);
    expect(q.lines).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it('snapshot: payout basis adds the delivery fee only when the production provider delivers', () => {
    const quote = { lines: [], missing: [], subtotal: 50000 };
    const at = new Date('2026-10-16T08:00:00Z');
    expect(pricingSnapshot({ providerId: 'P', quote, at, delivery: { providerId: 'P', fee: 30000 }, confirmedMissing: false }))
      .toMatchObject({ providerId: 'P', snapshotAt: at.toISOString(), currency: 'toman', providerSubtotal: 50000, payoutBasis: 80000, delivery: { providerId: 'P', fee: 30000 } });
    expect(pricingSnapshot({ providerId: 'P', quote, at, delivery: { providerId: 'F', fee: 30000 }, confirmedMissing: false }).payoutBasis).toBe(50000);
    expect(pricingSnapshot({ providerId: 'P', quote: { ...quote, subtotal: null }, at, delivery: null, confirmedMissing: true })).toMatchObject({ payoutBasis: null, confirmedMissing: true });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `snapshot.ts`** — replace `effectiveProviderPrices` with:

```ts
export interface ProviderEntry { price: number; versionId: string; version: number }

/** Effective provider-layer entries at `at` (price + the version in force), by provider then combo. */
export function effectiveProviderEntries(rows: SnapshotRow[], at: Date): Record<string, Record<string, ProviderEntry>> {
  const groups = new Map<string, { pid: string; key: string; list: (VersionLike & { comboKey: string })[] }>();
  for (const r of rows) {
    if (r.layer !== 'provider' || !r.providerId) continue;
    const pid = String(r.providerId);
    const g = groups.get(`${pid}|${r.comboKey}`) ?? { pid, key: r.comboKey, list: [] };
    g.list.push(toVersionLike(r));
    groups.set(`${pid}|${r.comboKey}`, g);
  }
  const out: Record<string, Record<string, ProviderEntry>> = {};
  for (const { pid, key, list } of groups.values()) {
    const e = pickEffective(list, at);
    const price = e ? unitPrice(e, {}) : null;
    if (e && price !== null) (out[pid] ??= {})[key] = { price, versionId: e.id, version: e.version };
  }
  return out;
}

/** Effective provider-layer prices at `at`, by provider then combo (unavailable combos are absent). */
export function effectiveProviderPrices(rows: SnapshotRow[], at: Date): Record<string, Record<string, number>> {
  const out: Record<string, Record<string, number>> = {};
  for (const [pid, combos] of Object.entries(effectiveProviderEntries(rows, at))) {
    out[pid] = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(combos).map(([k, e]) => [k, e.price]));
  }
  return out;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `apps/api/src/modules/dispatch/provider-pricing.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * v4.2 provider price of a job (spec §40, §52–53, §66) — pure. Only customer price-book lines can carry a provider
 * price; everything else is «قیمت مجری تنظیم نشده» (missing), never 0 and never another provider's price.
 */
import { parseCombo } from '../price-book/combo.js';
import { comboLabel, type LabelNames } from '../price-book/labels.js';
import type { ProviderEntry } from '../price-book/snapshot.js';
import type { ProviderPricingLine, ProviderPricingMissing, ProviderPricingSnapshot, ProviderQuote } from './job-model.js';

const PRICE_BOOK = /^(bw|color|spiral|addon)\|/;

export interface PricedOrder {
  children?: { name?: string }[];
  services?: { kind: string; label?: string; pricing?: { lines: { comboKey: string; quantity: number }[] } }[];
}

/** Persian label of a snapshot line key (price-book combos via `comboLabel`). */
export function providerLineLabel(key: string, names: LabelNames): string {
  const o = parseCombo(key);
  if (o) return comboLabel(o, names);
  if (key.startsWith('extra|')) return 'خدمات اضافی';
  if (key.startsWith('spiral_color|')) return 'هزینه رنگ فنری';
  if (key === 'docs_print') return 'چاپ صفحات';
  return key;
}

/** The provider's price of every line of the order from that provider's own effective entries. */
export function providerQuote(order: PricedOrder, entries: Record<string, ProviderEntry>, labelOf: (comboKey: string) => string): ProviderQuote {
  const lines: ProviderPricingLine[] = [];
  const missing: ProviderPricingMissing[] = [];
  (order.children ?? []).forEach((c, i) => missing.push({ childIndex: i, comboKey: null, label: `فنری کتاب مدرسه — ${c.name || 'فرزند'}` }));
  (order.services ?? []).forEach((s, i) => {
    const priced = s.pricing?.lines ?? [];
    if (!priced.length) {
      missing.push({ serviceIndex: i, comboKey: null, label: s.label || 'سرویس' });
      return;
    }
    for (const l of priced) {
      const e = PRICE_BOOK.test(l.comboKey) ? entries[l.comboKey] : undefined;
      if (!e) {
        missing.push({ serviceIndex: i, comboKey: l.comboKey, label: `${s.label || 'سرویس'}: ${labelOf(l.comboKey)}` });
        continue;
      }
      lines.push({ serviceIndex: i, comboKey: l.comboKey, label: labelOf(l.comboKey), versionId: e.versionId, version: e.version, unitPrice: e.price, quantity: l.quantity, subtotal: e.price * l.quantity });
    }
  });
  return { lines, missing, subtotal: missing.length ? null : lines.reduce((t, l) => t + l.subtotal, 0) };
}

/** The assignment snapshot (contract v4.2 `ProviderPricingSnapshot`). */
export function pricingSnapshot(input: {
  providerId: string; quote: ProviderQuote; at: Date; delivery: { providerId: string; fee: number } | null; confirmedMissing: boolean;
}): ProviderPricingSnapshot {
  const { providerId, quote, at, delivery, confirmedMissing } = input;
  const ownDelivery = delivery && delivery.providerId === providerId ? delivery.fee : 0;
  return {
    providerId, snapshotAt: at.toISOString(), currency: 'toman', lines: quote.lines, missing: quote.missing,
    providerSubtotal: quote.subtotal, delivery, payoutBasis: quote.subtotal === null ? null : quote.subtotal + ownDelivery, confirmedMissing,
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `apps/api/src/modules/orders/delivery-rules.ts`**

```ts
/** v4.2 delivery rules (D12, D15, D17) — pure. */
import type { PickupPoint } from '../catalog/catalog.defaults.js';
import type { OrderDelivery, PlaceSnapshot } from './order.schema.js';

/** Services whose items the courier collects at the customer's home (besides school books). */
export const HOME_PICKUP_KINDS = ['cart', 'repair'] as const;

export function needsHomePickup(d: { children?: unknown[]; services?: { kind: string }[] }): boolean {
  return (d.children?.length ?? 0) > 0 || (d.services ?? []).some((s) => (HOME_PICKUP_KINDS as readonly string[]).includes(s.kind));
}

/** The order's delivery; orders saved before v4.2 read as courier delivery with home pickup. */
export function deliveryOf(order: { delivery?: Partial<OrderDelivery> | null }): OrderDelivery {
  const d = order.delivery ?? {};
  return {
    method: d.method ?? 'courier',
    homePickup: d.homePickup ?? true,
    note: d.note ?? '',
    ...(d.pickupAt ? { pickupAt: d.pickupAt } : {}),
    ...(d.pickupPoint ? { pickupPoint: d.pickupPoint } : {}),
    ...(d.providerShop ? { providerShop: d.providerShop } : {}),
  };
}

/** Where a customer-pickup order is handed over (the provider shop wins once the admin moved it there). */
export function placeOf(d: OrderDelivery): PlaceSnapshot | undefined {
  if (d.method !== 'customer_pickup') return undefined;
  return d.pickupAt === 'provider' ? (d.providerShop ?? d.pickupPoint) : d.pickupPoint;
}

export interface DeliveryInput { method?: 'courier' | 'customer_pickup'; pickupPointId?: string; note?: string }
export interface PickupInput { address?: string; phone?: string; date?: string; slot?: string }

/** The checkout's delivery choice → what the order stores, or the first Persian problem (contract v4.2 order). */
export function resolveDelivery(homePickup: boolean, input: DeliveryInput | undefined, pickup: PickupInput, points: PickupPoint[]): { delivery: OrderDelivery } | { problem: string } {
  const method = input?.method ?? 'courier';
  if (homePickup && (!pickup.date || !pickup.slot)) return { problem: 'زمان تحویل‌گیری را انتخاب کنید' };
  if ((homePickup || method === 'courier') && !pickup.address?.trim()) {
    return { problem: homePickup ? 'آدرس تحویل‌گیری را وارد کنید' : 'آدرس ارسال را وارد کنید' };
  }
  const note = (input?.note ?? '').trim().slice(0, 300);
  if (method === 'customer_pickup') {
    const p = points.find((x) => x.on && x.id === input?.pickupPointId);
    if (!p) return { problem: 'محل تحویل حضوری معتبر نیست' };
    return { delivery: { method, homePickup, note, pickupAt: 'point', pickupPoint: { id: p.id, name: p.name, address: p.address, phone: p.phone, hours: p.hours, kind: 'point' } } };
  }
  return { delivery: { method: 'courier', homePickup, note } };
}
```

```ts
import type { JobStatus } from '../../common/constants.js';
import { deliveryOf } from './delivery-rules.js';
import type { PlaceSnapshot } from './order.schema.js';

export interface CustomerFulfillment { status: JobStatus; label: string; place?: PlaceSnapshot }

/** The customer's view of an order (contract v4.2): never the provider id or provider prices. */
export function customerOrderJson(order: unknown, fulfillment?: CustomerFulfillment): Record<string, unknown> {
  const o = order as { toJSON?: () => Record<string, unknown> };
  const json = typeof o.toJSON === 'function' ? o.toJSON() : { ...(order as Record<string, unknown>) };
  const { centerId: _center, providerPricing: _pricing, ...rest } = json;
  return { ...rest, delivery: deliveryOf(rest as { delivery?: never }), ...(fulfillment ? { fulfillment } : {}) };
}
```

`user-notification.schema.ts`:

```ts
import { NOTIFICATION_AUDIENCES, NOTIFICATION_EVENTS, type NotificationAudience, type NotificationEvent } from '../../common/constants.js';

/** In-app notification of an order / dispatch event (v3.3 customers; v4.2 providers and admins too — never SMS). */
@Schema({ collection: 'userNotifications', timestamps: { createdAt: true, updatedAt: false } })
export class UserNotification {
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId, required: true }) userId: Types.ObjectId;
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId, required: true }) orderId: Types.ObjectId;
  @Prop({ type: String, required: true }) orderCode: string;
  @Prop({ type: String, enum: NOTIFICATION_EVENTS, required: true }) event: NotificationEvent;
  /** Which app's inbox shows it (absent on v3.3 rows = customer). */
  @Prop({ type: String, enum: NOTIFICATION_AUDIENCES, default: 'customer' }) audience: NotificationAudience;
  @Prop({ type: String }) url?: string;
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId }) jobId?: Types.ObjectId;
  @Prop({ type: String, required: true }) text: string;
  @Prop({ type: Boolean, default: false }) read: boolean;
  createdAt: Date;
}

export type UserNotificationDocument = HydratedDocument<UserNotification>;
export const UserNotificationSchema = SchemaFactory.createForClass(UserNotification);
UserNotificationSchema.index({ userId: 1, createdAt: -1 });
UserNotificationSchema.index({ userId: 1, audience: 1, createdAt: -1 });

/** Public shape (contract v3.3 `UserNotification` + v4.2 `audience`, `url`, `jobId`). */
export function userNotificationJson(d: {
  _id: unknown; orderId: unknown; orderCode: string; event: NotificationEvent; text: string; read?: boolean; createdAt?: Date;
  audience?: NotificationAudience; url?: string; jobId?: unknown;
}) {
  return {
    id: String(d._id), orderId: String(d.orderId), orderCode: d.orderCode, event: d.event, text: d.text, read: !!d.read, createdAt: d.createdAt,
    audience: d.audience ?? 'customer', url: d.url ?? `/app/track/${String(d.orderId)}`, ...(d.jobId ? { jobId: String(d.jobId) } : {}),
  };
}
```

`notification-template.schema.ts`: `@Prop({ type: String, enum: NOTIFICATION_EVENTS, required: true }) event: NotificationEvent;` (import from constants).

- [ ] **Step 4: `NotificationsService`**

```ts
import { audienceOf, type NotificationAudience, type NotificationEvent } from '../../common/constants.js';

/** Reference of a provider/admin notification (contract v4.2). */
export interface NotifyRef { orderId: unknown; orderCode: string; jobId?: unknown; url: string; tag: string }

const PUSH_TITLE: Record<NotificationAudience, string> = { customer: 'دیجیتال سرو', provider: 'سرو مجری', admin: 'دیجیتال سرو · مدیریت' };
/** v3.3 rows have no audience: they are the customer's. */
const audienceFilter = (a: NotificationAudience) => (a === 'customer' ? { audience: { $nin: ['provider', 'admin'] } } : { audience: a });
```

`create(dto: { event: NotificationEvent; text: string; on?: boolean })`. Replace `dispatch`:

```ts
  async dispatch(event: NotificationEvent, order: DispatchOrder, suffix = ''): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const template = await this.templates.findOne({ event, channel: 'push', on: true });
      if (!template) return;
      const userId = new Types.ObjectId(String(order.customerId));
      const orderId = String(order._id);
      const text = template.text + suffix;
      const url = `/app/track/${orderId}`;
      await this.inbox.create({ userId, orderId: new Types.ObjectId(orderId), orderCode: order.code, event, audience: 'customer', url, text, read: false });
      void this.pushToUser(userId, { title: PUSH_TITLE.customer, body: text, url, tag: orderId });
    } catch (err) {
      this.logger.warn(`notification ${event} for order ${order.code} failed: ${(err as Error).message}`);
    }
  }

  /**
   * v4.2: provider / admin events (§68) — one inbox row per user and a Web Push titled by audience. Never throws:
   * notifications must not slow down or break the dispatch flow.
   */
  async notify(event: NotificationEvent, userIds: unknown[], ref: NotifyRef, suffix = ''): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const ids = [...new Set(userIds.filter(Boolean).map(String))];
      if (!ids.length) return;
      const template = await this.templates.findOne({ event, channel: 'push', on: true });
      if (!template) return;
      const audience = audienceOf(event);
      const text = template.text + suffix;
      for (const id of ids) {
        const userId = new Types.ObjectId(id);
        await this.inbox.create({
          userId, orderId: new Types.ObjectId(String(ref.orderId)), orderCode: ref.orderCode, event, audience, url: ref.url, text, read: false,
          ...(ref.jobId ? { jobId: new Types.ObjectId(String(ref.jobId)) } : {}),
        });
        void this.pushToUser(userId, { title: PUSH_TITLE[audience], body: text, url: ref.url, tag: ref.tag });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      this.logger.warn(`notification ${event} for order ${ref.orderCode} failed: ${(err as Error).message}`);
    }
  }
```

`mine(userId, q, audience: NotificationAudience = 'customer')` and `markRead(userId, ids?, audience: NotificationAudience = 'customer')`: the `owner` filter becomes `{ userId: new Types.ObjectId(userId), ...audienceFilter(audience) }` (both the list/count queries and the `updateMany`).

```ts
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { fakeModel, type Doc } from '../../../test/fake-model.js';
import { DEFAULT_DISPATCH } from '../catalog/catalog.defaults.js';
import { JobsService } from './jobs.service.js';

const P = new Types.ObjectId();
const asP = { actor: 'production' as const, byRole: 'provider' as const, providerId: String(P) };

function setup(orderOver: Doc = {}) {
  const order: Doc = {
    _id: new Types.ObjectId(), code: '10400', customerId: new Types.ObjectId(), customerName: 'مریم', customerPhone: '09123456789', status: 'registered',
    children: [], services: [{ kind: 'black_white_printing', label: 'چاپ سیاه‌وسفید', spec: { size: 'A4', sides: 'single' }, pricing: { lines: [{ comboKey: 'bw|A4|single', quantity: 20 }] } }],
    delivery: { method: 'courier', homePickup: false, note: '' }, urgent: false, payMethod: 'cod', paid: false, quote: { total: 90000 },
    qcLabels: ['کیفیت چاپ'], qc: [false], timeline: [], createdAt: new Date('2026-10-16T06:00:00Z'), __v: 0, ...orderOver,
  };
  const orders = fakeModel([order]);
  const jobs = fakeModel([]);
  const notifier = { provider: vi.fn(async () => undefined), admins: vi.fn(async () => undefined), customer: vi.fn(async () => undefined) };
  const catalog = { getSettings: vi.fn(async () => ({ dispatch: { ...DEFAULT_DISPATCH } })) };
  const svc = new JobsService(jobs as never, orders as never, catalog as never, notifier as never);
  return { svc, orders, jobs, notifier, order };
}

/** The job as DispatchService.assign leaves it: held by P in `status`. */
async function heldBy(s: ReturnType<typeof setup>, status = 'assigned') {
  const job = (await s.svc.ensureJob(s.order as never))!;
  Object.assign(s.jobs.docs[0], {
    status, providerId: P, attempts: 1,
    assignment: { id: new Types.ObjectId(), providerId: P, assignedAt: new Date(), respondBy: new Date(Date.now() + 3_600_000) },
  });
  return String(job._id);
}

describe('JobsService.ensureJob (D11)', () => {
  it('creates exactly one unassigned job per order, also under concurrent calls', async () => {
    const s = setup({ urgent: true });
    await Promise.all([s.svc.ensureJob(s.order as never), s.svc.ensureJob(s.order as never), s.svc.ensureJob(s.order as never)]);
    expect(s.jobs.docs).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0]).toMatchObject({
      seq: 1, status: 'unassigned', attempts: 0, priority: 'urgent', homePickup: false, services: ['black_white_printing'],
      sheets: 20, units: { black_white_printing: 20 }, deliveryMethod: 'courier', orderCode: '10400', lineIndexes: [0], childIndexes: [],
    });
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].deadline.getTime() - (s.order.createdAt as Date).getTime()).toBe(24 * 3_600_000);
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].history).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ action: 'created', status: 'unassigned', byRole: 'system' })]);
  });
  it('unpaid, cancelled and delivered orders get no job', async () => {
    for (const status of ['pending_payment', 'cancelled', 'delivered']) {
      const s = setup({ status });
      await expect(s.svc.ensureJob(s.order as never)).resolves.toBeNull();
      expect(s.jobs.docs).toHaveLength(0);
    }
  });
  it('an order with a v1 centre becomes that provider’s job at the order’s stage, without a price snapshot', async () => {
    const s = setup({ status: 'qc', centerId: P });
    const job = await s.svc.ensureJob(s.order as never);
    expect(job).toMatchObject({ status: 'in_production', attempts: 1 });
    expect(String(job!.providerId)).toBe(String(P));
    expect(job!.history[0]).toMatchObject({ action: 'migrated', status: 'in_production' });
    expect(job!.providerPricing).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe('JobsService.move — the one transition path', () => {
  it('accept mirrors registered → confirmed and tells the customer «assigned»', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await heldBy(s);
    await s.svc.move(id, 'accepted', asP);
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].status).toBe('accepted');
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].assignment.acceptedAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].history.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ action: 'accepted', status: 'accepted', byRole: 'provider' });
    expect(s.orders.docs[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'confirmed', __v: 1 });
    expect(s.notifier.customer).toHaveBeenCalledWith('assigned', expect.anything());
  });
  it('another provider cannot move the job (404, nothing changes)', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await heldBy(s);
    await expect(s.svc.move(id, 'accepted', { ...asP, providerId: String(new Types.ObjectId()) })).rejects.toThrow('سفارش یافت نشد');
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].status).toBe('assigned');
  });
  it('production blocked before home pickup', async () => {
    const s = setup({ children: [{ name: 'سارا', books: 9 }], services: [], delivery: { method: 'courier', homePickup: true, note: '' }, status: 'courier_assigned' });
    const id = await heldBy(s, 'accepted');
    await expect(s.svc.move(id, 'in_production', asP)).rejects.toThrow('اقلام سفارش هنوز از مشتری تحویل گرفته نشده است');
    s.orders.docs[0].status = 'picked_up';
    await s.svc.move(id, 'in_production', asP);
    expect(s.orders.docs[0].status).toBe('preparing');
  });
  it('ready needs the order’s QC checklist and mirrors packing', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await heldBy(s, 'in_production');
    await expect(s.svc.move(id, 'ready', asP)).rejects.toThrow('کنترل کیفیت کامل نشده است');
    s.orders.docs[0].qc = [true];
    await s.svc.move(id, 'ready', asP);
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].readyAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
    expect(s.orders.docs[0].status).toBe('packing');
  });
  it('an unlisted move is a Persian 400', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await heldBy(s, 'accepted');
    await expect(s.svc.move(id, 'ready', asP)).rejects.toThrow('تغییر وضعیت از «پذیرفته‌شده» به «آماده» مجاز نیست');
  });
  it('provider delivery: the order moves first and records the COD payment atomically', async () => {
    const s = setup({ status: 'packing' });
    const id = await heldBy(s, 'out_for_delivery');
    Object.assign(s.jobs.docs[0], { deliveryMethod: 'provider', deliveryProviderId: P });
    await s.svc.move(id, 'delivered', { actor: 'delivery', byRole: 'provider', providerId: String(P) });
    expect(s.orders.docs[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'delivered', paid: true, chargedAmount: 90000, paidVia: 'cod' });
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].status).toBe('delivered');
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].deliveredAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
    expect(s.orders.findOneAndUpdate.mock.invocationCallOrder[0]).toBeLessThan(s.jobs.findOneAndUpdate.mock.invocationCallOrder[0]);
  });
  it('two concurrent accepts: one wins, the other gets 409; the customer is told once', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await heldBy(s);
    const r = await Promise.allSettled([s.svc.move(id, 'accepted', asP), s.svc.move(id, 'accepted', asP)]);
    expect(r.filter((x) => x.status === 'fulfilled')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect((r.find((x) => x.status === 'rejected') as PromiseRejectedResult).reason.status).toBe(409);
    expect(s.notifier.customer.mock.calls.filter(([e]) => e === 'assigned')).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});

describe('JobsService.giveBack (D21) and cancellation', () => {
  it('a rejection returns the job to the queue, releases the provider, clears the order’s provider and alerts the admins', async () => {
    const s = setup({ centerId: P });
    const id = await heldBy(s);
    await s.svc.giveBack(await s.svc.byId(id), 'rejected', { byRole: 'provider', providerId: String(P), reasonCode: 'equipment_unavailable', reason: 'تجهیزات در دسترس نیست' });
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].status).toBe('unassigned');
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].providerId).toBeUndefined();
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].assignment).toBeUndefined();
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].releasedProviderIds.map(String)).toEqual([String(P)]);
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].history.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ action: 'rejected', status: 'rejected', reasonCode: 'equipment_unavailable', stage: 'before_production', providerId: P });
    expect(s.orders.docs[0].centerId).toBeUndefined();
    expect(s.notifier.admins).toHaveBeenCalledWith('job_rejected', expect.anything());
    expect(s.notifier.provider).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it('rejecting a job that is no longer waiting for an answer is 400', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await heldBy(s, 'accepted');
    await expect(s.svc.giveBack(await s.svc.byId(id), 'rejected', { byRole: 'provider', providerId: String(P), reasonCode: 'other', reason: 'x' })).rejects.toThrow('این سفارش منتظر پاسخ شما نیست');
  });
  it('cancel during production records the stage and tells the provider once; nothing moves afterwards', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await heldBy(s, 'in_production');
    s.orders.docs[0].status = 'cancelled';
    await s.svc.cancelForOrder(s.orders.docs[0]);
    await s.svc.cancelForOrder(s.orders.docs[0]);
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'cancelled', cancel: { stage: 'in_production', byRole: 'admin' } });
    expect(s.notifier.provider).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(s.notifier.provider).toHaveBeenCalledWith('job_cancelled', P, expect.anything());
    await expect(s.svc.move(id, 'ready', asP)).rejects.toThrow('این سفارش لغو شده است');
  });
});

describe('JobsService.reconcile and fulfilment', () => {
  it('creates missing jobs and makes jobs follow cancelled / delivered orders', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.orders.docs.push({ ...s.order, _id: new Types.ObjectId(), code: '10401', status: 'cancelled' });
    expect((await s.svc.reconcile()).created).toBe(1);
    s.orders.docs[0].status = 'delivered';
    expect((await s.svc.reconcile()).delivered).toBe(1);
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].status).toBe('delivered');
    expect(s.jobs.docs).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it('the customer sees a provider-free label and the pickup place', async () => {
    const place = { id: 'pp1', name: 'دفتر مرکزی', address: 'ونک', phone: '0', hours: '', kind: 'point' };
    const s = setup({ delivery: { method: 'customer_pickup', homePickup: false, note: '', pickupAt: 'point', pickupPoint: place } });
    await s.svc.ensureJob(s.order as never);
    const f = await s.svc.fulfillmentFor([s.order as never]);
    expect(f.get(String(s.order._id))).toEqual({ status: 'unassigned', label: 'در صف تولید', place });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `apps/api/src/modules/dispatch/job-seed.ts`**

```ts
/** v4.2: the insert document of an order's job (pure). V1 = one job per order, every line (D11, D18). */
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import type { JobStatus, OrderStatus } from '../../common/constants.js';
import type { DispatchSettings } from '../catalog/catalog.defaults.js';
import { deliveryOf } from '../orders/delivery-rules.js';
import type { Order } from '../orders/order.schema.js';
import { jobUnits, providerServicesOf } from './eligibility.js';
import { AFTER_READY, deadlineFor, legacyJobStatus, type JobHistoryEntry } from './job-model.js';

/** What a job needs of its order. */
export type JobSource = Pick<Order, 'code' | 'status' | 'children' | 'services' | 'urgent' | 'customerName' | 'customerPhone' | 'zone' | 'delivery' | 'createdAt'> & {
  _id: Types.ObjectId; customerId: Types.ObjectId; centerId?: Types.ObjectId | null;
};

const NO_JOB: OrderStatus[] = ['pending_payment', 'cancelled', 'delivered'];
/** Placed and not finished. */
export const orderNeedsJob = (status: OrderStatus) => !NO_JOB.includes(status);

export function jobSeed(order: JobSource, s: Pick<DispatchSettings, 'leadHours' | 'urgentLeadHours'>, now: Date) {
  const delivery = deliveryOf(order);
  const createdAt = order.createdAt ? new Date(order.createdAt) : now;
  const { units, sheets } = jobUnits(order);
  const base = {
    orderCode: order.code, customerId: order.customerId, customerName: order.customerName ?? '', customerPhone: order.customerPhone ?? '',
    ...(order.zone ? { zone: order.zone } : {}),
    homePickup: delivery.homePickup, services: providerServicesOf(order),
    childIndexes: (order.children ?? []).map((_, i) => i), lineIndexes: (order.services ?? []).map((_, i) => i),
    priority: order.urgent ? ('urgent' as const) : ('normal' as const), deadline: deadlineFor(createdAt, !!order.urgent, s),
    deliveryMethod: delivery.method, ...(delivery.pickupAt ? { pickupAt: delivery.pickupAt } : {}),
    releasedProviderIds: [], pricingHistory: [], units, sheets, orderCreatedAt: createdAt,
  };
  if (!order.centerId) {
    const history: JobHistoryEntry[] = [{ at: now, action: 'created', status: 'unassigned', byRole: 'system' }];
    return { ...base, status: 'unassigned' as JobStatus, attempts: 0, history };
  }
  // a v1 centre assignment (no acceptance existed): the job starts held by that provider at the order's stage
  const status = legacyJobStatus(order.status);
  const providerId = new Types.ObjectId(String(order.centerId));
  const history: JobHistoryEntry[] = [{ at: now, action: 'migrated', status, providerId, byRole: 'system', reason: 'تخصیص پیش از نسخه ۴.۲ (بدون قیمت مجری)' }];
  return {
    ...base, status, attempts: 1, providerId, history,
    assignment: { id: new Types.ObjectId(), providerId, assignedAt: createdAt, respondBy: now, seenAt: now, acceptedAt: now },
    ...(AFTER_READY.includes(status) ? { readyAt: now } : {}),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `apps/api/src/modules/dispatch/dispatch-notifier.ts`**

```ts
import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
import { Model } from 'mongoose';
import type { DispatchEvent, NotificationEvent } from '../../common/constants.js';
import { Center } from '../centers/center.schema.js';
import { NotificationsService, type DispatchOrder, type NotifyRef } from '../notifications/notifications.service.js';
import { User } from '../users/user.schema.js';

export interface JobRef { _id: unknown; orderId: unknown; orderCode: string }

/** §68 recipients: the provider's linked login, every admin allowed to dispatch, the order's customer. Never throws. */
@Injectable()
export class DispatchNotifier {
  private readonly logger = new Logger('DispatchNotifier');

  constructor(
    private readonly notifications: NotificationsService,
    @InjectModel(Center.name) private readonly centers: Model<Center>,
    @InjectModel(User.name) private readonly users: Model<User>,
  ) {}

  private ref(job: JobRef, url: string): NotifyRef {
    return { orderId: job.orderId, orderCode: job.orderCode, jobId: job._id, url, tag: `job-${String(job._id)}` };
  }

  /** D19: in-app inbox + web push in «سرو مجری». */
  async provider(event: DispatchEvent, providerId: unknown, job: JobRef): Promise<void> {
    try {
      if (!providerId) return;
      const c = await this.centers.findById(providerId, { userId: 1 }).lean();
      if (c?.userId) await this.notifications.notify(event, [c.userId], this.ref(job, `/provider/jobs/${String(job._id)}`));
    } catch (err) {
      this.logger.warn(`provider ${event} for order ${job.orderCode} failed: ${(err as Error).message}`);
    }
  }

  /** Super-admins and admins with `dispatch_view`. */
  async admins(event: DispatchEvent, job: JobRef): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const admins = await this.users.find({ role: 'admin' }, { permissions: 1 }).lean();
      const ids = admins.filter((a) => !a.permissions || a.permissions.includes('dispatch_view')).map((a) => a._id);
      await this.notifications.notify(event, ids, this.ref(job, `/admin/dispatch?job=${String(job._id)}`));
    } catch (err) {
      this.logger.warn(`admin ${event} for order ${job.orderCode} failed: ${(err as Error).message}`);
    }
  }

  async customer(event: NotificationEvent, order: DispatchOrder, suffix = ''): Promise<void> {
    await this.notifications.dispatch(event, order, suffix);
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `apps/api/src/modules/dispatch/jobs.service.ts`**

```ts
import { BadRequestException, ConflictException, Injectable, Logger, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
import { Model, Types } from 'mongoose';
import { JOB_STATUS_LABELS, STATUS_LABELS, type JobStatus, type OrderStatus, type RejectReason } from '../../common/constants.js';
import { assertObjectId } from '../../common/utils/object-id.js';
import { CatalogService } from '../catalog/catalog.service.js';
import { deliveryOf, placeOf } from '../orders/delivery-rules.js';
import { qcComplete, timelineEntry, type CustomerFulfillment } from '../orders/order-helpers.js';
import { Order, type OrderDelivery, type OrderDocument } from '../orders/order.schema.js';
import { DispatchNotifier } from './dispatch-notifier.js';
import {
  DELIVERY_VISIBLE, HELD, LIVE, ORDER_MIRROR, canMove, cancelStage, customerFulfillmentLabel, mirrorSources, productionBlocked,
  type Actor, type ByRole, type FlowCtx, type JobAction, type JobHistoryEntry,
} from './job-model.js';
import { Job, JobDocument } from './job.schema.js';
import { jobSeed, orderNeedsJob, type JobSource } from './job-seed.js';

export interface MoveOptions { actor: Actor; byRole: ByRole; by?: string; reason?: string; reasonCode?: RejectReason; providerId?: string }
export interface GiveBackOptions { byRole: ByRole; by?: string; reason?: string; reasonCode?: RejectReason; providerId?: string; assignmentId?: string }
type GiveBackKind = 'rejected' | 'timeout' | 'failed' | 'unassigned';

const NOT_FOUND = 'سفارش یافت نشد';
export const raced = () => new ConflictException('وضعیت سفارش هم‌زمان تغییر کرد؛ دوباره تلاش کنید');
const badMove = (from: JobStatus, to: JobStatus) => new BadRequestException(`تغییر وضعیت از «${JOB_STATUS_LABELS[from]}» به «${JOB_STATUS_LABELS[to]}» مجاز نیست`);
const flowOf = (job: Pick<Job, 'deliveryMethod' | 'pickupAt'>): FlowCtx => ({ method: job.deliveryMethod, pickupAt: job.pickupAt });

function stampsFor(to: JobStatus, now: Date): Record<string, Date> {
  if (to === 'accepted') return { 'assignment.acceptedAt': now };
  if (to === 'ready') return { readyAt: now };
  if (to === 'delivered') return { deliveredAt: now };
  if (to === 'completed') return { completedAt: now };
  return {};
}

/**
 * v4.2 fulfilment engine: one job per order (D11), every status change through `move`/`giveBack` (compare-and-set on
 * the status and the holder), the order mirrored forward only (D10), notifications to provider/admin/customer (§68).
 */
@Injectable()
export class JobsService {
  private readonly logger = new Logger('Jobs');

  constructor(
    @InjectModel(Job.name) private readonly jobs: Model<Job>,
    @InjectModel(Order.name) private readonly orders: Model<Order>,
    private readonly catalog: CatalogService,
    private readonly notifier: DispatchNotifier,
  ) {}

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ creation and reads

  /** Creates the order's job once — an idempotent, race-safe upsert on `{orderId, seq: 1}`. Never throws. */
  async ensureJob(order: JobSource): Promise<JobDocument | null> {
    if (!orderNeedsJob(order.status)) return null;
    const key = { orderId: order._id, seq: 1 };
    try {
      const { dispatch } = await this.catalog.getSettings();
      await this.jobs.updateOne(key, { $setOnInsert: jobSeed(order, dispatch, new Date()) }, { upsert: true });
    } catch (err) {
      if ((err as { code?: number }).code !== 11000) {
        this.logger.warn(`job for order ${order.code} not created (reconcile retries): ${(err as Error).message}`);
        return null;
      }
    }
    return this.jobs.findOne(key);
  }

  /** By job id, or by the order id (the dispatch center links orders by their id). */
  async byId(id: string): Promise<JobDocument> {
    assertObjectId(id, 'سفارش');
    const job = (await this.jobs.findById(id)) ?? (await this.jobs.findOne({ orderId: new Types.ObjectId(id), seq: 1 }));
    if (!job) throw new NotFoundException(NOT_FOUND);
    return job;
  }

  forOrder(orderId: unknown) {
    return this.jobs.findOne({ orderId, seq: 1 });
  }

  /** Contract «Customer order view»: `fulfillment` by order id (orders without a job are absent). */
  async fulfillmentFor(orders: { _id: unknown; delivery?: Partial<OrderDelivery> | null }[]): Promise<Map<string, CustomerFulfillment>> {
    const out = new Map<string, CustomerFulfillment>();
    if (!orders.length) return out;
    const jobs = await this.jobs.find({ orderId: { $in: orders.map((o) => o._id) }, seq: 1 }, { orderId: 1, status: 1 }).lean();
    const byOrder = new Map(jobs.map((j) => [String(j.orderId), j.status]));
    for (const o of orders) {
      const status = byOrder.get(String(o._id));
      if (!status) continue;
      const place = placeOf(deliveryOf(o));
      out.set(String(o._id), { status, label: customerFulfillmentLabel(status), ...(place ? { place } : {}) });
    }
    return out;
  }

  /** Is `providerId` acting on its own job in that role (§64)? */
  holds(job: Pick<Job, 'providerId' | 'deliveryProviderId' | 'status'>, providerId: string, actor: Actor): boolean {
    if (actor === 'production') return !!job.providerId && String(job.providerId) === providerId;
    if (actor === 'delivery') return !!job.deliveryProviderId && String(job.deliveryProviderId) === providerId && DELIVERY_VISIBLE.includes(job.status);
    return true;
  }

  entry(job: Pick<Job, 'providerId' | 'deliveryProviderId'>, action: JobAction, status: JobStatus, o: { byRole: ByRole; by?: string; reason?: string; reasonCode?: RejectReason }, at: Date, extra: Partial<JobHistoryEntry> = {}): JobHistoryEntry {
    return {
      at, action, status,
      ...(job.providerId ? { providerId: job.providerId } : {}),
      ...(job.deliveryProviderId ? { deliveryProviderId: job.deliveryProviderId } : {}),
      ...(o.by ? { by: new Types.ObjectId(o.by) } : {}),
      byRole: o.byRole,
      ...(o.reason?.trim() ? { reason: o.reason.trim() } : {}),
      ...(o.reasonCode ? { reasonCode: o.reasonCode } : {}),
      ...extra,
    };
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ transitions

  /**
   * The single job transition path (contract «Job flow»): flow + preconditions, compare-and-set on the status and the
   * holder, the order mirrored forward, notifications. `delivered` moves the order first (COD is money); the job follows.
   */
  async move(jobId: string, to: JobStatus, o: MoveOptions): Promise<JobDocument> {
    const job = await this.byId(jobId);
    if (o.providerId && !this.holds(job, o.providerId, o.actor)) throw new NotFoundException(NOT_FOUND);
    const order = await this.orders.findById(job.orderId);
    if (!order) throw new NotFoundException(NOT_FOUND);
    if (job.status === 'cancelled' || order.status === 'cancelled') throw new BadRequestException('این سفارش لغو شده است');
    if (!canMove(job.status, to, o.actor, flowOf(job))) throw badMove(job.status, to);
    if (to === 'failed') {
      if (!o.reasonCode && !o.reason?.trim()) throw new BadRequestException('دلیل را بنویسید');
      return this.giveBack(job, 'failed', { ...o, assignmentId: job.assignment ? String(job.assignment.id) : undefined });
    }
    if (to === 'in_production') {
      const blocked = productionBlocked({ status: order.status, homePickup: deliveryOf(order).homePickup });
      if (blocked) throw new BadRequestException(blocked);
    }
    if (to === 'ready' && !qcComplete(order)) throw new BadRequestException('کنترل کیفیت کامل نشده است');
    const now = new Date();
    if (to === 'delivered') await this.mirrorOrder(order, 'delivered', true);
    const action: JobAction = to === 'accepted' ? 'accepted' : to === 'completed' ? 'completed' : 'status';
    const updated = await this.jobs.findOneAndUpdate(
      { _id: job._id, status: job.status, ...(job.providerId ? { providerId: job.providerId } : {}) },
      { $set: { status: to, ...stampsFor(to, now) }, $push: { history: this.entry(job, action, to, o, now) } },
      { returnDocument: 'after' },
    );
    if (!updated) throw raced();
    if (to !== 'delivered') await this.mirrorOrder(order, ORDER_MIRROR[to], false);
    if (to === 'accepted') await this.notifier.customer('assigned', order);
    if (to === 'ready_for_pickup') {
      const place = placeOf(deliveryOf(order));
      await this.notifier.customer('ready_for_pickup', order, place ? ` — ${place.name}، ${place.address}` : '');
    }
    return updated;
  }

  /** Forward-only order mirror (D10). `strict` (delivered): a mirror that cannot happen is an error; otherwise it is skipped. */
  private async mirrorOrder(order: OrderDocument, target: OrderStatus | undefined, strict: boolean): Promise<void> {
    if (!target || order.status === target) return;
    if (!mirrorSources(target).includes(order.status)) {
      if (strict) throw new BadRequestException(`سفارش در وضعیت «${STATUS_LABELS[order.status]}» است و به «${STATUS_LABELS[target]}» نمی‌رود`);
      return;
    }
    const codPaid = target === 'delivered' && order.payMethod === 'cod' && !order.paid
      ? { paid: true, chargedAmount: order.quote?.total ?? 0, paidVia: 'cod' as const }
      : {};
    const updated = await this.orders.findOneAndUpdate(
      { _id: order._id, status: order.status },
      { $set: { status: target, ...codPaid }, $push: { timeline: timelineEntry(target) }, $inc: { __v: 1 } },
      { returnDocument: 'after' },
    );
    if (!updated) {
      if (strict) throw raced();
      this.logger.warn(`order ${order.code}: mirror to ${target} skipped (the order changed meanwhile)`);
      return;
    }
    await this.notifier.customer(target, updated);
  }

  /**
   * The job returns to «تخصیص‌نیافته» (D21): rejected / timed out (from `assigned`), failed (from accepted/in production)
   * or taken back by an admin (any held status). The provider joins `releasedProviderIds`; provider delivery and a shop
   * pickup fall back to the courier / the Digital Sarv point.
   */
  async giveBack(job: JobDocument, kind: GiveBackKind, o: GiveBackOptions): Promise<JobDocument> {
    if (o.providerId && String(job.providerId) !== o.providerId) throw new NotFoundException(NOT_FOUND);
    const from: JobStatus[] = kind === 'rejected' || kind === 'timeout' ? ['assigned'] : kind === 'failed' ? ['accepted', 'in_production'] : HELD;
    if (!from.includes(job.status) || !job.providerId) {
      throw kind === 'rejected' ? new BadRequestException('این سفارش منتظر پاسخ شما نیست') : badMove(job.status, 'unassigned');
    }
    const providerId = job.providerId;
    const now = new Date();
    const status: JobStatus = kind === 'rejected' ? 'rejected' : kind === 'failed' ? 'failed' : 'unassigned';
    const reset = { ...(job.deliveryMethod === 'provider' ? { deliveryMethod: 'courier' } : {}), ...(job.pickupAt === 'provider' ? { pickupAt: 'point' } : {}) };
    const updated = await this.jobs.findOneAndUpdate(
      { _id: job._id, status: job.status, providerId, ...(o.assignmentId ? { 'assignment.id': new Types.ObjectId(o.assignmentId) } : {}) },
      {
        $set: { status: 'unassigned', ...reset },
        $unset: { providerId: 1, assignment: 1, deliveryProviderId: 1, providerPricing: 1 },
        $addToSet: { releasedProviderIds: providerId },
        $push: { history: this.entry(job, kind, status, o, now, { stage: cancelStage(job.status) }) },
      },
      { returnDocument: 'after' },
    );
    if (!updated) throw raced();
    const orderSet = { ...(reset.deliveryMethod ? { 'delivery.method': 'courier' } : {}), ...(reset.pickupAt ? { 'delivery.pickupAt': 'point' } : {}) };
    await this.orders.updateOne(
      { _id: job.orderId },
      { $unset: { centerId: 1, providerPricing: 1, ...(reset.pickupAt ? { 'delivery.providerShop': 1 } : {}) }, ...(Object.keys(orderSet).length ? { $set: orderSet } : {}), $inc: { __v: 1 } },
    );
    if (kind === 'timeout' || kind === 'unassigned') await this.notifier.provider('job_withdrawn', providerId, updated);
    if (job.deliveryProviderId && String(job.deliveryProviderId) !== String(providerId)) await this.notifier.provider('job_withdrawn', job.deliveryProviderId, updated);
    if (kind === 'rejected') await this.notifier.admins('job_rejected', updated);
    if (kind === 'timeout') await this.notifier.admins('job_timeout', updated);
    if (kind === 'failed') await this.notifier.admins('job_failed', updated);
    return updated;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ order events

  /** The order was cancelled: its live job becomes `cancelled` with the stage (M4) and the holder is told. Never throws. */
  async cancelForOrder(order: { _id: unknown }, byRole: ByRole = 'admin'): Promise<void> {
    try {
      for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {
        const job = await this.forOrder(order._id);
        if (!job || !LIVE.includes(job.status)) return;
        const now = new Date();
        const stage = cancelStage(job.status);
        const updated = await this.jobs.findOneAndUpdate(
          { _id: job._id, status: job.status },
          {
            $set: { status: 'cancelled', cancel: { at: now, stage, ...(job.providerId ? { providerId: job.providerId } : {}), byRole } },
            $push: { history: this.entry(job, 'cancelled', 'cancelled', { byRole }, now, { stage }) },
          },
          { returnDocument: 'after' },
        );
        if (!updated) continue;
        if (job.providerId) await this.notifier.provider('job_cancelled', job.providerId, updated);
        if (job.deliveryProviderId && String(job.deliveryProviderId) !== String(job.providerId)) await this.notifier.provider('job_cancelled', job.deliveryProviderId, updated);
        return;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      this.logger.warn(`cancel job of order ${String(order._id)} failed (reconcile retries): ${(err as Error).message}`);
    }
  }

  /** The order was delivered (courier or admin): the job follows from whatever live stage. Never throws. */
  async onOrderDelivered(orderId: unknown, byRole: ByRole): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const job = await this.forOrder(orderId);
      if (!job || !LIVE.includes(job.status)) return;
      const now = new Date();
      await this.jobs.updateOne(
        { _id: job._id, status: job.status },
        { $set: { status: 'delivered', deliveredAt: now, ...(job.readyAt ? {} : { readyAt: now }) }, $push: { history: this.entry(job, 'status', 'delivered', { byRole }, now) } },
      );
    } catch (err) {
      this.logger.warn(`delivered job of order ${String(orderId)} failed (reconcile retries): ${(err as Error).message}`);
    }
  }

  /** An admin set the order `out_for_delivery`: a courier job that is `ready` follows. Never throws. */
  async onOrderOutForDelivery(orderId: unknown): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const job = await this.forOrder(orderId);
      if (!job || job.status !== 'ready' || job.deliveryMethod !== 'courier') return;
      const now = new Date();
      await this.jobs.updateOne({ _id: job._id, status: 'ready' }, { $set: { status: 'out_for_delivery' }, $push: { history: this.entry(job, 'status', 'out_for_delivery', { byRole: 'admin' }, now) } });
    } catch (err) {
      this.logger.warn(`out_for_delivery job of order ${String(orderId)} failed: ${(err as Error).message}`);
    }
  }

  /** The courier took the parcel at the provider (`POST /courier/orders/:id/collected`). */
  async courierCollected(orderId: unknown): Promise<void> {
    const job = await this.forOrder(orderId);
    if (!job || job.status !== 'ready' || job.deliveryMethod !== 'courier') throw new BadRequestException('این سفارش هنوز آماده تحویل نیست');
    await this.move(String(job._id), 'out_for_delivery', { actor: 'courier', byRole: 'courier' });
  }

  /** Safety net (boot + every minute): missing jobs are created; jobs follow cancelled / delivered orders. */
  async reconcile(limit = 500): Promise<{ created: number; cancelled: number; delivered: number }> {
    const res = { created: 0, cancelled: 0, delivered: 0 };
    try {
      const open = await this.orders.find({ status: { $nin: ['pending_payment', 'cancelled', 'delivered'] } }).sort({ createdAt: 1 }).limit(limit);
      if (open.length) {
        const have = new Set((await this.jobs.find({ orderId: { $in: open.map((o) => o._id) } }, { orderId: 1 }).lean()).map((j) => String(j.orderId)));
        for (const o of open) if (!have.has(String(o._id)) && (await this.ensureJob(o))) res.created++;
      }
      const live = await this.jobs.find({ status: { $in: LIVE } }, { orderId: 1 }).limit(limit).lean();
      if (live.length) {
        const done = await this.orders.find({ _id: { $in: live.map((j) => j.orderId) }, status: { $in: ['cancelled', 'delivered'] } }, { status: 1 });
        for (const o of done) {
          if (o.status === 'cancelled') {
            await this.cancelForOrder(o, 'system');
            res.cancelled++;
          } else {
            await this.onOrderDelivered(o._id, 'system');
            res.delivered++;
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      this.logger.warn(`reconcile failed: ${(err as Error).message}`);
    }
    return res;
  }
}
```

```ts
import { IsIn, IsMongoId, IsOptional, Matches } from 'class-validator';
import { DELIVERY_METHODS, JOB_PRIORITIES, JOB_STATUSES, type DeliveryMethod, type JobPriority, type JobStatus } from '../../../common/constants.js';
import { PageQueryDto } from '../../../common/utils/pagination.js';
import { PROVIDER_SERVICES, type ProviderService } from '../../centers/provider-model.js';
import { DELIVERY_STATUSES, DISPATCH_TABS, PROVIDER_TABS, type DeliveryStatus, type DispatchTab, type ProviderTab } from '../job-model.js';

const ymd = { message: 'تاریخ باید به‌صورت yyyy-mm-dd باشد' };

export class DispatchQueryDto extends PageQueryDto {
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(DISPATCH_TABS, { message: 'بخش معتبر نیست' }) tab?: DispatchTab;
  @IsOptional() @IsMongoId({ message: 'شناسه مجری معتبر نیست' }) providerId?: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(PROVIDER_SERVICES, { message: 'سرویس معتبر نیست' }) service?: ProviderService;
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(JOB_STATUSES, { message: 'وضعیت معتبر نیست' }) status?: JobStatus;
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(JOB_PRIORITIES, { message: 'اولویت معتبر نیست' }) priority?: JobPriority;
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(DELIVERY_METHODS, { message: 'روش تحویل معتبر نیست' }) deliveryMethod?: DeliveryMethod;
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(DELIVERY_STATUSES, { message: 'وضعیت تحویل معتبر نیست' }) deliveryStatus?: DeliveryStatus;
  @IsOptional() @Matches(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/, ymd) from?: string;
  @IsOptional() @Matches(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/, ymd) to?: string;
}

export class EligibilityQueryDto {
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(DELIVERY_METHODS, { message: 'روش تحویل معتبر نیست' }) deliveryMethod?: DeliveryMethod;
}

export class ProviderJobsQueryDto extends PageQueryDto {
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(PROVIDER_TABS, { message: 'بخش معتبر نیست' }) tab?: ProviderTab;
}
```
