- [ ] **Step 4: Serializers** — `apps/api/src/modules/dispatch/job-json.ts`

```ts
/** v4.2 job serializers. Admin views here; provider views (Task 11) are added below them — isolation lives in this file. */
import type { Types } from 'mongoose';
import {
  JOB_STATUS_LABELS, type DeliveryMethod, type JobPriority, type JobStatus, type OrderStatus, type PayMethod, type PickupAt, type RejectReason,
} from '../../common/constants.js';
import type { ProviderService } from '../centers/provider-model.js';
import { deliveryOf } from '../orders/delivery-rules.js';
import { qcComplete } from '../orders/order-helpers.js';
import type { Order, OrderDelivery } from '../orders/order.schema.js';
import { assignmentHistory, isLate, type ByRole, type CancelStage, type JobAction, type JobHistoryEntry, type ProviderPricingSnapshot } from './job-model.js';
import type { Job } from './job.schema.js';

export type JobRow = Job & { _id: Types.ObjectId };
/** Provider and user names by id (ids are unique across both collections). */
export type Names = Map<string, string>;

const iso = (d?: Date | string | null) => (d ? new Date(d).toISOString() : undefined);
const nameOf = (names: Names, id?: unknown) => (id ? (names.get(String(id)) ?? '—') : undefined);

export interface DispatchJobRow {
  id: string; orderId: string; orderCode: string; customerName: string; zone?: string; services: ProviderService[]; summary: string;
  status: JobStatus; orderStatus: OrderStatus; priority: JobPriority; deadline: string; late: boolean; attempts: number;
  provider: { id: string; name: string } | null; deliveryProvider: { id: string; name: string } | null;
  deliveryMethod: DeliveryMethod; pickupAt?: PickupAt; respondBy?: string;
  lastException?: { kind: 'rejected' | 'timeout' | 'failed' | 'unassigned'; reason: string; providerName: string; at: string };
  createdAt: string;
}
export interface JobHistoryView {
  at: string; action: JobAction; status: JobStatus; label: string; providerName?: string; deliveryProviderName?: string;
  byName?: string; byRole: ByRole; reason?: string; reasonCode?: RejectReason; override?: boolean; stage?: CancelStage;
}
export interface AssignmentView {
  providerId: string; providerName: string; assignedByName?: string; assignedAt: string; acceptedAt?: string; rejectedAt?: string;
  completedAt?: string; endedAt?: string; reason?: string; reasonCode?: RejectReason; override?: boolean;
  outcome: 'pending' | 'active' | 'rejected' | 'timeout' | 'withdrawn' | 'failed' | 'cancelled' | 'completed';
}
export interface DispatchJobDetail extends DispatchJobRow {
  homePickup: boolean; sheets: number; units: Partial<Record<ProviderService, number>>; requirements: string[];
  order: { status: OrderStatus; urgent: boolean; payMethod: PayMethod; paid: boolean; total: number; delivery: OrderDelivery;
    pickup: { address: string; phone: string; date?: string; slot?: string }; qcDone: boolean; courierId?: string };
  providerPricing: ProviderPricingSnapshot | null; history: JobHistoryView[]; assignments: AssignmentView[];
  cancel?: { at: string; stage: CancelStage; providerName?: string };
}

const EXCEPTIONS = ['rejected', 'timeout', 'failed', 'unassigned'] as const;
type ExceptionKind = (typeof EXCEPTIONS)[number];

/** Why the job is back in the queue (the latest give-back after the latest assignment). */
export function lastException(history: JobHistoryEntry[], names: Names): DispatchJobRow['lastException'] {
  for (let i = history.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const e = history[i];
    if (e.action === 'assigned' || e.action === 'reassigned') return undefined;
    if ((EXCEPTIONS as readonly string[]).includes(e.action)) {
      return { kind: e.action as ExceptionKind, reason: e.reason ?? (e.action === 'timeout' ? 'بدون پاسخ در مهلت' : ''), providerName: nameOf(names, e.providerId) ?? '—', at: iso(e.at)! };
    }
  }
  return undefined;
}

export function dispatchRow(job: JobRow, names: Names, orderStatus: OrderStatus, summary: string, now: Date): DispatchJobRow {
  const exc = job.status === 'unassigned' ? lastException(job.history ?? [], names) : undefined;
  return {
    id: String(job._id), orderId: String(job.orderId), orderCode: job.orderCode, customerName: job.customerName, ...(job.zone ? { zone: job.zone } : {}),
    services: job.services ?? [], summary, status: job.status, orderStatus, priority: job.priority, deadline: iso(job.deadline)!,
    late: isLate(job, now), attempts: job.attempts ?? 0,
    provider: job.providerId ? { id: String(job.providerId), name: nameOf(names, job.providerId)! } : null,
    deliveryProvider: job.deliveryProviderId ? { id: String(job.deliveryProviderId), name: nameOf(names, job.deliveryProviderId)! } : null,
    deliveryMethod: job.deliveryMethod, ...(job.pickupAt ? { pickupAt: job.pickupAt } : {}),
    ...(job.status === 'assigned' && job.assignment ? { respondBy: iso(job.assignment.respondBy) } : {}),
    ...(exc ? { lastException: exc } : {}),
    createdAt: iso(job.orderCreatedAt)!,
  };
}

export function historyView(history: JobHistoryEntry[], names: Names): JobHistoryView[] {
  return history.map((e) => ({
    at: iso(e.at)!, action: e.action, status: e.status, label: JOB_STATUS_LABELS[e.status], byRole: e.byRole,
    ...(e.providerId ? { providerName: nameOf(names, e.providerId) } : {}),
    ...(e.deliveryProviderId ? { deliveryProviderName: nameOf(names, e.deliveryProviderId) } : {}),
    ...(e.by ? { byName: nameOf(names, e.by) } : {}),
    ...(e.reason ? { reason: e.reason } : {}), ...(e.reasonCode ? { reasonCode: e.reasonCode } : {}),
    ...(e.override ? { override: true } : {}), ...(e.stage ? { stage: e.stage } : {}),
  }));
}

type DetailOrder = Pick<Order, 'status' | 'urgent' | 'payMethod' | 'paid' | 'quote' | 'pickup' | 'qc' | 'qcLabels' | 'delivery' | 'courierId' | 'children' | 'services'>;

export function dispatchDetail(job: JobRow, order: DetailOrder, names: Names, requirements: string[], summary: string, now: Date): DispatchJobDetail {
  return {
    ...dispatchRow(job, names, order.status, summary, now),
    homePickup: job.homePickup, sheets: job.sheets ?? 0, units: job.units ?? {}, requirements,
    order: {
      status: order.status, urgent: !!order.urgent, payMethod: order.payMethod, paid: !!order.paid, total: order.quote?.total ?? 0,
      delivery: deliveryOf(order),
      pickup: { address: order.pickup?.address ?? '', phone: order.pickup?.phone ?? '', ...(order.pickup?.date ? { date: order.pickup.date } : {}), ...(order.pickup?.slot ? { slot: order.pickup.slot } : {}) },
      qcDone: qcComplete(order), ...(order.courierId ? { courierId: String(order.courierId) } : {}),
    },
    providerPricing: job.providerPricing ?? null,
    history: historyView(job.history ?? [], names),
    assignments: assignmentHistory(job.history ?? []).map((a) => ({
      providerId: a.providerId, providerName: nameOf(names, a.providerId) ?? '—', ...(a.assignedBy ? { assignedByName: nameOf(names, a.assignedBy) } : {}),
      assignedAt: iso(a.assignedAt)!, ...(a.acceptedAt ? { acceptedAt: iso(a.acceptedAt) } : {}), ...(a.rejectedAt ? { rejectedAt: iso(a.rejectedAt) } : {}),
      ...(a.completedAt ? { completedAt: iso(a.completedAt) } : {}), ...(a.endedAt ? { endedAt: iso(a.endedAt) } : {}),
      ...(a.reason ? { reason: a.reason } : {}), ...(a.reasonCode ? { reasonCode: a.reasonCode } : {}), ...(a.override ? { override: true } : {}), outcome: a.outcome,
    })),
    ...(job.cancel ? { cancel: { at: iso(job.cancel.at)!, stage: job.cancel.stage, ...(job.cancel.providerId ? { providerName: nameOf(names, job.cancel.providerId) } : {}) } } : {}),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `DispatchService` (reads)** — `apps/api/src/modules/dispatch/dispatch.service.ts`

```ts
import { Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
import { Model, Types, type QueryFilter } from 'mongoose';
import type { DeliveryMethod } from '../../common/constants.js';
import { addDays, tehranDayStart, tehranYmd } from '../../common/utils/dates.js';
import { escapeRegex, pageParams } from '../../common/utils/pagination.js';
import { normalizePhone, toAsciiDigits } from '../../common/utils/phone.js';
import { CatalogService } from '../catalog/catalog.service.js';
import { Paper } from '../catalog/schemas/paper.schema.js';
import { Center } from '../centers/center.schema.js';
import type { ProviderService, ProviderStatus } from '../centers/provider-model.js';
import { orderSummary } from '../orders/order-helpers.js';
import { Order } from '../orders/order.schema.js';
import { BindingRange } from '../price-book/schemas/binding-range.schema.js';
import { PriceVersion } from '../price-book/schemas/price-version.schema.js';
import { effectiveProviderEntries, type ProviderEntry, type SnapshotRow } from '../price-book/snapshot.js';
import { User } from '../users/user.schema.js';
import { Zone } from '../zones/zone.schema.js';
import { DispatchNotifier } from './dispatch-notifier.js';
import type { DispatchQueryDto } from './dto/dispatch.dto.js';
import { checkProvider, jobRequirements, type CheckResult, type ProviderCheck, type ProviderFacts, type Usage } from './eligibility.js';
import { dispatchDetail, dispatchRow, type DispatchJobDetail, type DispatchJobRow, type JobRow, type Names } from './job-json.js';
import { DISPATCH_TABS, HELD, deliveryStatusFilter, dispatchTabFilter, type DispatchTab, type ProviderQuote } from './job-model.js';
import { Job, type JobDocument } from './job.schema.js';
import { JobsService } from './jobs.service.js';
import { providerLineLabel, providerQuote } from './provider-pricing.js';

export interface EligibilityRow {
  providerId: string; name: string; businessName: string; status: ProviderStatus; current: boolean;
  eligible: boolean; assignable: boolean; checks: ProviderCheck[];
  price: { subtotal: number | null; missing: string[]; lines: { label: string; unitPrice: number; quantity: number; subtotal: number }[] };
  capacityLeft: CheckResult['capacityLeft']; hourlySheets?: number; openNow: boolean | null; activeJobs: number; hasLogin: boolean;
  canDeliver: boolean; deliveryFee: number; allowCustomerPickup: boolean;
}
export interface Eligibility {
  jobId: string; requirements: string[]; eligible: EligibilityRow[]; others: EligibilityRow[];
  deliveryProviders: { id: string; name: string; deliveryFee: number; zoneOk: boolean }[];
}
export type CenterRow = Center & { _id: Types.ObjectId };
type OrderRow = Order & { _id: Types.ObjectId };

const EMPTY_USAGE: Usage = { sheets: 0, jobs: 0, units: {} };
const ACTIVE = [...HELD, 'ready', 'ready_for_pickup', 'out_for_delivery'];

/** Provider facts with the v4.1 defaults (centres saved before v4.1 lack them). */
export function factsOf(p: CenterRow): ProviderFacts {
  return {
    id: String(p._id), status: p.status ?? 'active', availableForOrders: p.availableForOrders ?? true, workingHours: p.workingHours ?? [],
    serviceZoneIds: (p.serviceZoneIds ?? []).map(String), capacity: p.capacity ?? { perService: [] }, capacityPerDay: p.capacityPerDay ?? 0,
    capabilities: p.capabilities ?? [], canDeliver: !!p.canDeliver,
  };
}

/** «مرکز تخصیص سفارش» (§55): the admin's view of every job, eligibility (§51–54) and — Task 10 — assignment. */
@Injectable()
export class DispatchService {
  constructor(
    @InjectModel(Job.name) private readonly jobModel: Model<Job>,
    @InjectModel(Order.name) private readonly orders: Model<Order>,
    @InjectModel(Center.name) private readonly centers: Model<Center>,
    @InjectModel(User.name) private readonly users: Model<User>,
    @InjectModel(Zone.name) private readonly zones: Model<Zone>,
    @InjectModel(PriceVersion.name) private readonly versions: Model<PriceVersion>,
    @InjectModel(BindingRange.name) private readonly ranges: Model<BindingRange>,
    @InjectModel(Paper.name) private readonly papers: Model<Paper>,
    private readonly jobs: JobsService,
    private readonly catalog: CatalogService,
    private readonly notifier: DispatchNotifier,
  ) {}

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ list

  private conditions(q: DispatchQueryDto): QueryFilter<Job>[] {
    const c: QueryFilter<Job>[] = [];
    if (q.providerId) {
      const id = new Types.ObjectId(q.providerId);
      c.push({ $or: [{ providerId: id }, { deliveryProviderId: id }] });
    }
    if (q.service) c.push({ services: q.service });
    if (q.priority) c.push({ priority: q.priority });
    if (q.deliveryMethod) c.push({ deliveryMethod: q.deliveryMethod });
    if (q.deliveryStatus) c.push(deliveryStatusFilter(q.deliveryStatus) as QueryFilter<Job>);
    if (q.from) c.push({ orderCreatedAt: { $gte: tehranDayStart(q.from) } });
    if (q.to) c.push({ orderCreatedAt: { $lt: addDays(tehranDayStart(q.to), 1) } });
    if (q.q?.trim()) {
      const term = toAsciiDigits(q.q.trim());
      const rx = new RegExp(escapeRegex(term), 'i');
      c.push({ $or: [{ orderCode: rx }, { customerName: rx }, { customerPhone: normalizePhone(term) ?? rx }] });
    }
    return c;
  }

  async list(q: DispatchQueryDto): Promise<{ items: DispatchJobRow[]; total: number; page: number; limit: number; counts: Record<DispatchTab, number> }> {
    const { page, limit, skip } = pageParams(q);
    const base = this.conditions(q);
    const tab = q.tab ?? 'unassigned';
    const scope = q.status ? { status: q.status } : dispatchTabFilter(tab);
    const filter = { $and: [...base, scope] } as QueryFilter<Job>;
    const sort = tab === 'completed' || tab === 'cancelled' ? { updatedAt: -1 as const } : { deadline: 1 as const, _id: 1 as const };
    const [items, total, ...counts] = await Promise.all([
      this.jobModel.find(filter).sort(sort).skip(skip).limit(limit).lean(),
      this.jobModel.countDocuments(filter),
      ...DISPATCH_TABS.map((t) => this.jobModel.countDocuments({ $and: [...base, dispatchTabFilter(t)] } as QueryFilter<Job>)),
    ]);
    const rows = items as JobRow[];
    const [names, orders] = await Promise.all([this.namesFor(rows), this.orderFacts(rows)]);
    const now = new Date();
    return {
      items: rows.map((j) => {
        const o = orders.get(String(j.orderId));
        return dispatchRow(j, names, o?.status ?? 'registered', o ? orderSummary(o) : '', now);
      }),
      total, page, limit,
      counts: Object.fromEntries(DISPATCH_TABS.map((t, i) => [t, counts[i]])) as Record<DispatchTab, number>,
    };
  }

  private async orderFacts(jobs: JobRow[]) {
    const ids = jobs.map((j) => j.orderId);
    const list = ids.length ? ((await this.orders.find({ _id: { $in: ids } }, { status: 1, children: 1, services: 1 }).lean()) as OrderRow[]) : [];
    return new Map(list.map((o) => [String(o._id), o]));
  }

  /** Names of every provider and admin a job mentions. */
  async namesFor(jobs: Pick<JobRow, 'providerId' | 'deliveryProviderId' | 'history' | 'cancel' | 'assignment'>[]): Promise<Names> {
    const providerIds = new Set<string>();
    const userIds = new Set<string>();
    for (const j of jobs) {
      for (const id of [j.providerId, j.deliveryProviderId, j.cancel?.providerId]) if (id) providerIds.add(String(id));
      if (j.assignment?.assignedBy) userIds.add(String(j.assignment.assignedBy));
      for (const e of j.history ?? []) {
        for (const id of [e.providerId, e.deliveryProviderId, e.previousProviderId]) if (id) providerIds.add(String(id));
        if (e.by) userIds.add(String(e.by));
      }
    }
    const [providers, users] = await Promise.all([
      providerIds.size ? this.centers.find({ _id: { $in: [...providerIds].map((id) => new Types.ObjectId(id)) } }, { name: 1 }).lean() : [],
      userIds.size ? this.users.find({ _id: { $in: [...userIds].map((id) => new Types.ObjectId(id)) } }, { name: 1, phone: 1 }).lean() : [],
    ]);
    const names: Names = new Map();
    for (const p of providers) names.set(String(p._id), p.name);
    for (const u of users) names.set(String(u._id), u.name || u.phone);
    return names;
  }

  async detail(id: string): Promise<DispatchJobDetail> {
    const job = (await this.jobs.byId(id)) as unknown as JobRow;
    const order = (await this.orders.findById(job.orderId).lean()) as OrderRow | null;
    if (!order) throw new NotFoundException('سفارش یافت نشد');
    const names = await this.namesFor([job]);
    return dispatchDetail(job, order, names, jobRequirements(order).map((r) => r.line), orderSummary(order), new Date());
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ eligibility (§51–54)

  /** Today's assigned load per provider (Tehran day, by `assignment.assignedAt`), without `exclude`. */
  private async usageToday(now: Date, exclude: unknown): Promise<Map<string, Usage>> {
    const since = tehranDayStart(tehranYmd(now));
    const rows = await this.jobModel.find({ 'assignment.assignedAt': { $gte: since }, status: { $nin: ['unassigned', 'cancelled'] }, _id: { $ne: exclude } }, { providerId: 1, sheets: 1, units: 1 }).lean();
    const out = new Map<string, Usage>();
    for (const j of rows) {
      if (!j.providerId) continue;
      const u = out.get(String(j.providerId)) ?? { sheets: 0, jobs: 0, units: {} };
      u.sheets += j.sheets ?? 0;
      u.jobs += 1;
      for (const [s, n] of Object.entries(j.units ?? {})) u.units[s as ProviderService] = (u.units[s as ProviderService] ?? 0) + (n ?? 0);
      out.set(String(j.providerId), u);
    }
    return out;
  }

  private async activeJobs(): Promise<Map<string, number>> {
    const rows = await this.jobModel.find({ status: { $in: ACTIVE }, providerId: { $exists: true } }, { providerId: 1 }).lean();
    const out = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const j of rows) out.set(String(j.providerId), (out.get(String(j.providerId)) ?? 0) + 1);
    return out;
  }

  private async zoneIdOf(name?: string): Promise<string | null> {
    if (!name) return null;
    const z = await this.zones.findOne({ name }).lean();
    return z ? String(z._id) : null;
  }

  /** Every provider's effective provider-layer entries now (provider_price_version included). */
  private async providerEntries(at: Date): Promise<Record<string, Record<string, ProviderEntry>>> {
    const rows = (await this.versions.find({ layer: 'provider', state: { $in: ['published', 'disabled'] } }).lean()) as SnapshotRow[];
    return effectiveProviderEntries(rows, at);
  }

  async labelOf(): Promise<{ labelOf: (k: string) => string; paperNames: Map<string, string> }> {
    const [papers, ranges] = await Promise.all([this.papers.find().lean(), this.ranges.find().lean()]);
    const paperNames = new Map(papers.map((p) => [p.key, p.name]));
    const names = { papers: paperNames, ranges: new Map(ranges.map((r) => [String(r._id), { minPages: r.minPages, maxPages: r.maxPages }])) };
    return { labelOf: (k) => providerLineLabel(k, names), paperNames };
  }

  /** One provider against one job: every check and the provider's own price (Task 10's assign uses it too). */
  async evaluate(job: JobRow | JobDocument, order: OrderRow, p: CenterRow, opts: { needsDelivery: boolean; now?: Date }): Promise<{ check: CheckResult; quote: ProviderQuote }> {
    const now = opts.now ?? new Date();
    const [usage, zoneId, entries, labels] = await Promise.all([this.usageToday(now, job._id), this.zoneIdOf(order.zone), this.providerEntries(now), this.labelOf()]);
    const check = checkProvider(factsOf(p), jobRequirements(order), { sheets: job.sheets ?? 0, units: job.units ?? {} }, usage.get(String(p._id)) ?? EMPTY_USAGE,
      { now, orderZoneId: zoneId, needsDelivery: opts.needsDelivery, paperNames: labels.paperNames });
    return { check, quote: providerQuote(order, entries[String(p._id)] ?? {}, labels.labelOf) };
  }

  async eligibility(id: string, deliveryMethod?: DeliveryMethod): Promise<Eligibility> {
    const job = (await this.jobs.byId(id)) as unknown as JobRow;
    const order = (await this.orders.findById(job.orderId).lean()) as OrderRow | null;
    if (!order) throw new NotFoundException('سفارش یافت نشد');
    const now = new Date();
    const [providers, usage, zoneId, entries, labels, active] = await Promise.all([
      this.centers.find().sort({ name: 1 }).lean() as unknown as Promise<CenterRow[]>,
      this.usageToday(now, job._id), this.zoneIdOf(order.zone), this.providerEntries(now), this.labelOf(), this.activeJobs(),
    ]);
    const reqs = jobRequirements(order);
    const method = deliveryMethod ?? job.deliveryMethod;
    const rows: EligibilityRow[] = providers.map((p) => {
      const id = String(p._id);
      const facts = factsOf(p);
      const check = checkProvider(facts, reqs, { sheets: job.sheets ?? 0, units: job.units ?? {} }, usage.get(id) ?? EMPTY_USAGE,
        { now, orderZoneId: zoneId, needsDelivery: method === 'provider', paperNames: labels.paperNames });
      const q = providerQuote(order, entries[id] ?? {}, labels.labelOf);
      return {
        providerId: id, name: p.name, businessName: p.businessName ?? '', status: facts.status, current: !!job.providerId && String(job.providerId) === id,
        eligible: check.eligible, assignable: check.assignable, checks: check.checks,
        price: { subtotal: q.subtotal, missing: q.missing.map((m) => m.label), lines: q.lines.map((l) => ({ label: l.label, unitPrice: l.unitPrice, quantity: l.quantity, subtotal: l.subtotal })) },
        capacityLeft: check.capacityLeft, ...(typeof p.capacity?.hourlySheets === 'number' ? { hourlySheets: p.capacity.hourlySheets } : {}),
        openNow: check.openNow, activeJobs: active.get(id) ?? 0, hasLogin: !!p.userId,
        canDeliver: !!p.canDeliver, deliveryFee: p.deliveryFee ?? 0, allowCustomerPickup: !!p.allowCustomerPickup,
      };
    });
    return {
      jobId: String(job._id), requirements: reqs.map((r) => r.line),
      eligible: rows.filter((r) => r.eligible),
      others: rows.filter((r) => !r.eligible).sort((a, b) => Number(b.assignable) - Number(a.assignable)),
      deliveryProviders: providers.filter((p) => p.canDeliver && (p.status ?? 'active') === 'active').map((p) => {
        const zones = (p.serviceZoneIds ?? []).map(String);
        return { id: String(p._id), name: p.name, deliveryFee: p.deliveryFee ?? 0, zoneOk: !zones.length || !zoneId || zones.includes(zoneId) };
      }),
    };
  }
}
```

(`Array.prototype.sort` is stable, so `others` keeps the name order within «assignable» / «not capable».)

```ts
import { Controller, Get, Param, Query, UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import { RequirePermissions, Roles } from '../../common/auth/decorators.js';
import { JwtAuthGuard, RolesGuard } from '../../common/auth/guards.js';
import { PermissionsGuard } from '../../common/auth/permissions.guard.js';
import { DispatchService } from './dispatch.service.js';
import { DispatchQueryDto, EligibilityQueryDto } from './dto/dispatch.dto.js';

/** «مرکز تخصیص سفارش» (contract v4.2 «Admin endpoints»). */
@Controller('admin/dispatch')
@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, RolesGuard, PermissionsGuard)
@Roles('admin')
export class AdminDispatchController {
  constructor(private readonly dispatch: DispatchService) {}

  @Get('jobs') @RequirePermissions('dispatch_view')
  list(@Query() q: DispatchQueryDto) { return this.dispatch.list(q); }

  @Get('jobs/:id') @RequirePermissions('dispatch_view')
  get(@Param('id') id: string) { return this.dispatch.detail(id); }

  @Get('jobs/:id/eligibility') @RequirePermissions('dispatch_view')
  eligibility(@Param('id') id: string, @Query() q: EligibilityQueryDto) { return this.dispatch.eligibility(id, q.deliveryMethod); }
}
```

```ts
describe('DispatchService.assign (§54, §88)', () => {
  it('assigns the capable provider: snapshot of its prices on the order, response deadline, history, notification', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await withJob(s);
    const before = Date.now();
    const d = await s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C) } as never, String(ADMIN));
    expect(d).toMatchObject({ status: 'assigned', attempts: 1, provider: { id: String(C), name: 'آزمون C' } });
    const job = s.jobsModel.docs[0];
    expect(job.assignment.respondBy.getTime() - before).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(120 * 60_000 - 1000);
    expect(job.providerPricing).toMatchObject({ providerId: String(C), providerSubtotal: 1_600_000, payoutBasis: 1_600_000, confirmedMissing: false });
    expect(job.providerPricing.lines[0]).toMatchObject({ comboKey: 'color|A4|full|glossy200|single', unitPrice: 8000, quantity: 200, version: 1 });
    const order = s.orders.docs[0];
    expect(String(order.centerId)).toBe(String(C));
    expect(order.providerPricing).toEqual(job.providerPricing);
    expect(order.services[0].pricing.lines[0].unitPrice).toBe(9500); // the customer snapshot is never touched
    expect(d.assignments[0]).toMatchObject({ providerName: 'آزمون C', assignedByName: 'مدیر سیستم', outcome: 'pending' });
    expect(s.notifier.provider).toHaveBeenCalledWith('job_assigned', C, expect.anything());
  });
  it('a provider that cannot fulfil the order is refused even with override (§86)', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await withJob(s);
    await expect(s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(A), override: true, reason: 'فوری' } as never, String(ADMIN))).rejects.toMatchObject({
      status: 400, response: expect.objectContaining({ code: 'NOT_CAPABLE' }),
    });
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0].status).toBe('unassigned');
  });
  it('a soft failure needs override + a reason; the override is recorded', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await withJob(s);
    s.centers.docs[2].status = 'busy';
    await expect(s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C) } as never, String(ADMIN))).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 409, response: expect.objectContaining({ code: 'PROVIDER_NOT_ELIGIBLE', problems: ['مجری مشغول است'] }) });
    await expect(s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C), override: true } as never, String(ADMIN))).rejects.toThrow('دلیل نادیده‌گرفتن شرایط را بنویسید');
    await s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C), override: true, reason: 'هماهنگ شد' } as never, String(ADMIN));
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0].assignment.override).toEqual({ reason: 'هماهنگ شد', problems: ['مجری مشغول است'] });
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0].history.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ action: 'assigned', override: true, reason: 'هماهنگ شد' });
  });
  it('a missing provider price needs explicit confirmation; the snapshot then has no subtotal (§53)', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await withJob(s);
    s.versions.docs.length = 0;
    await expect(s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C) } as never, String(ADMIN))).rejects.toMatchObject({
      status: 409, response: expect.objectContaining({ code: 'PROVIDER_PRICE_MISSING', message: 'قیمت مجری تنظیم نشده', missing: [expect.stringContaining('چاپ رنگی')] }),
    });
    await s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C), confirmMissingPrice: true } as never, String(ADMIN));
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0].providerPricing).toMatchObject({ providerSubtotal: null, payoutBasis: null, confirmedMissing: true });
  });
  it('concurrent assigns: one wins, the other gets 409, one notification', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await withJob(s);
    const r = await Promise.allSettled([1, 2].map(() => s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C) } as never, String(ADMIN))));
    expect(r.filter((x) => x.status === 'fulfilled')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect((r.find((x) => x.status === 'rejected') as PromiseRejectedResult).reason.status).toBe(409);
    expect(s.notifier.provider.mock.calls.filter(([e]) => e === 'job_assigned')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0].attempts).toBe(1);
  });
  it('assigning an assigned job is 409; a cancelled order is 400', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await withJob(s);
    await s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C) } as never, String(ADMIN));
    await expect(s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C) } as never, String(ADMIN))).rejects.toThrow(/قبلاً تخصیص داده شده/);
    const t = setup();
    const tid = await withJob(t);
    t.orders.docs[0].status = 'cancelled';
    await expect(t.svc.assign(tid, { providerId: String(C) } as never, String(ADMIN))).rejects.toThrow('این سفارش لغو یا تحویل شده است');
  });
});

describe('DispatchService reassign / unassign / delivery (§60, §61, §71)', () => {
  const G = new Types.ObjectId();
  const withG = (s: ReturnType<typeof setup>) => s.centers.docs.push({ ...s.centers.docs[2], _id: G, name: 'آزمون G', userId: new Types.ObjectId(), allowCustomerPickup: false, canDeliver: false });
  it('reassign needs a reason, keeps the previous provider in the history with its stage, and tells both providers', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    withG(s);
    const id = await withJob(s);
    await s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C) } as never, String(ADMIN));
    await expect(s.svc.reassign(id, { providerId: String(G) } as never, String(ADMIN))).rejects.toThrow('دلیل را بنویسید');
    await expect(s.svc.reassign(id, { providerId: String(C), reason: 'x' } as never, String(ADMIN))).rejects.toThrow('هم‌اکنون به همین مجری');
    const d = await s.svc.reassign(id, { providerId: String(G), reason: 'ظرفیت C پر شد', confirmMissingPrice: true } as never, String(ADMIN));
    expect(d).toMatchObject({ status: 'assigned', attempts: 2, provider: { name: 'آزمون G' } });
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0].history.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ action: 'reassigned', previousProviderId: C, stage: 'before_production', reason: 'ظرفیت C پر شد' });
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0].releasedProviderIds.map(String)).toEqual([String(C)]);
    expect(d.assignments.map((a) => [a.providerName, a.outcome])).toEqual([['آزمون C', 'withdrawn'], ['آزمون G', 'pending']]);
    expect(s.notifier.provider).toHaveBeenCalledWith('job_withdrawn', C, expect.anything());
    expect(s.notifier.provider).toHaveBeenCalledWith('job_assigned', G, expect.anything());
    expect(String(s.orders.docs[0].centerId)).toBe(String(G));
  });
  it('unassign returns the job to the queue and tells the provider', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await withJob(s);
    await s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C) } as never, String(ADMIN));
    const d = await s.svc.unassign(id, 'مشتری درخواست تغییر داد', String(ADMIN));
    expect(d).toMatchObject({ status: 'unassigned', provider: null, lastException: { kind: 'unassigned', reason: 'مشتری درخواست تغییر داد' } });
    expect(s.notifier.provider).toHaveBeenCalledWith('job_withdrawn', C, expect.anything());
    expect(s.orders.docs[0].centerId).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it('provider delivery by a separate delivery-only provider (§61): fee on the snapshot, not in the producer’s payout basis', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await withJob(s);
    await s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C), deliveryMethod: 'provider', deliveryProviderId: String(F) } as never, String(ADMIN));
    const job = s.jobsModel.docs[0];
    expect(job).toMatchObject({ deliveryMethod: 'provider' });
    expect(String(job.deliveryProviderId)).toBe(String(F));
    expect(job.providerPricing).toMatchObject({ delivery: { providerId: String(F), fee: 20000 }, payoutBasis: 1_600_000 });
    expect(s.orders.docs[0].delivery.method).toBe('provider');
    expect(s.notifier.provider).toHaveBeenCalledWith('job_delivery_assigned', F, expect.anything());
    await expect(s.svc.updateDelivery(id, { deliveryMethod: 'provider', deliveryProviderId: String(B) } as never, String(ADMIN))).rejects.toThrow('مجری تحویل باید فعال باشد و امکان ارسال داشته باشد');
    const back = await s.svc.updateDelivery(id, { deliveryMethod: 'courier' } as never, String(ADMIN));
    expect(back).toMatchObject({ deliveryMethod: 'courier', deliveryProvider: null });
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0].history.at(-1).action).toBe('delivery_changed');
  });
  it('customer pickup: may move to the provider shop only when allowed; the customer’s choice never flips', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    withG(s);
    s.orders.docs[0].delivery = { method: 'customer_pickup', homePickup: false, note: '', pickupAt: 'point', pickupPoint: { id: 'pp1', name: 'دفتر مرکزی', address: 'ونک', phone: '0', hours: '', kind: 'point' } };
    const id = await withJob(s);
    await expect(s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C), deliveryMethod: 'courier' } as never, String(ADMIN))).rejects.toThrow('روش تحویل انتخابی مشتری');
    await expect(s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(G), pickupAt: 'provider', confirmMissingPrice: true } as never, String(ADMIN))).rejects.toThrow('تحویل حضوری در محل این مجری مجاز نیست');
    await s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C), pickupAt: 'provider' } as never, String(ADMIN));
    expect(s.orders.docs[0].delivery).toMatchObject({ method: 'customer_pickup', pickupAt: 'provider', providerShop: { name: 'آزمون C', address: 'تهران، ونک', kind: 'provider' }, pickupPoint: { id: 'pp1' } });
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0].pickupAt).toBe('provider');
  });
  it('priority and deadline: a deadline change re-arms the alerts and tells the provider', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await withJob(s);
    await s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C) } as never, String(ADMIN));
    Object.assign(s.jobsModel.docs[0], { deadlineNotifiedAt: new Date(), lateAlertedAt: new Date() });
    const deadline = new Date(Date.now() + 5 * 3_600_000).toISOString();
    const d = await s.svc.update(id, { priority: 'high', deadline } as never, String(ADMIN));
    expect(d).toMatchObject({ priority: 'high', deadline });
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0].deadlineNotifiedAt).toBeUndefined();
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0].lateAlertedAt).toBeUndefined();
    expect(s.notifier.provider).toHaveBeenCalledWith('job_changed', C, expect.anything());
  });
});

describe('DispatchService on behalf of the provider and the v1 order-sheet path', () => {
  it('respond: accept or reject for the provider, with the admin’s reason in the history', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const id = await withJob(s);
    await s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C) } as never, String(ADMIN));
    const d = await s.svc.respond(id, { accept: false, reasonCode: 'capacity_full', reason: 'تلفنی اعلام شد' } as never, String(ADMIN));
    expect(d).toMatchObject({ status: 'unassigned', lastException: { kind: 'rejected', reason: 'تلفنی اعلام شد' } });
    expect(s.notifier.admins).toHaveBeenCalledWith('job_rejected', expect.anything());
    await s.svc.assign(id, { providerId: String(C) } as never, String(ADMIN));
    expect((await s.svc.respond(id, { accept: true, reason: 'تلفنی تأیید شد' } as never, String(ADMIN))).status).toBe('accepted');
    expect(s.orders.docs[0].status).toBe('confirmed');
  });
  it('the v1 centre select delegates with the dispatch permissions', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    await withJob(s);
    const staff = new Types.ObjectId();
    (s.svc as unknown as { users: { docs: Doc[] } }).users.docs.push({ _id: staff, role: 'admin', permissions: ['dispatch_view'] });
    await expect(s.svc.assignFromOrder(String(s.order._id), String(C), String(staff))).rejects.toThrow('به این بخش دسترسی ندارید');
    await s.svc.assignFromOrder(String(s.order._id), String(C), String(ADMIN));
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0].status).toBe('assigned');
    await s.svc.assignFromOrder(String(s.order._id), null, String(ADMIN));
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'unassigned' });
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0].history.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ action: 'unassigned', reason: 'لغو از صفحه سفارش' });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: DTOs** — append to `dto/dispatch.dto.ts` (add `IsBoolean, IsISO8601, IsNotEmpty, IsString, MaxLength` to the class-validator import and `PICKUP_AT, REJECT_REASONS, type PickupAt, type RejectReason` to the constants import):

```ts
const reasonMsg = { message: 'دلیل را بنویسید' };

export class AssignJobDto {
  @IsMongoId({ message: 'مجری را انتخاب کنید' }) providerId: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(DELIVERY_METHODS, { message: 'روش تحویل معتبر نیست' }) deliveryMethod?: DeliveryMethod;
  @IsOptional() @IsMongoId({ message: 'شناسه مجری تحویل معتبر نیست' }) deliveryProviderId?: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(PICKUP_AT, { message: 'محل تحویل حضوری معتبر نیست' }) pickupAt?: PickupAt;
  @IsOptional() @IsBoolean() override?: boolean;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() @MaxLength(300, { message: 'دلیل حداکثر ۳۰۰ کاراکتر است' }) reason?: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsBoolean() confirmMissingPrice?: boolean;
}

export class ReasonDto {
  @IsString(reasonMsg) @IsNotEmpty(reasonMsg) @MaxLength(300, { message: 'دلیل حداکثر ۳۰۰ کاراکتر است' }) reason: string;
}

export class DeliveryChangeDto {
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(DELIVERY_METHODS, { message: 'روش تحویل معتبر نیست' }) deliveryMethod?: DeliveryMethod;
  @IsOptional() @IsMongoId({ message: 'شناسه مجری تحویل معتبر نیست' }) deliveryProviderId?: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(PICKUP_AT, { message: 'محل تحویل حضوری معتبر نیست' }) pickupAt?: PickupAt;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() @MaxLength(300) reason?: string;
}

export class UpdateJobDto {
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(JOB_PRIORITIES, { message: 'اولویت معتبر نیست' }) priority?: JobPriority;
  @IsOptional() @IsISO8601({}, { message: 'مهلت معتبر نیست' }) deadline?: string;
}

export class RespondDto {
  @IsBoolean({ message: 'پاسخ را مشخص کنید' }) accept: boolean;
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(REJECT_REASONS, { message: 'دلیل رد معتبر نیست' }) reasonCode?: RejectReason;
  @IsString(reasonMsg) @IsNotEmpty(reasonMsg) @MaxLength(300) reason: string;
}

export const ADMIN_JOB_TARGETS = ['in_production', 'ready', 'out_for_delivery', 'ready_for_pickup', 'delivered', 'completed', 'failed'] as const;
export class AdminJobStatusDto {
  @IsIn(ADMIN_JOB_TARGETS, { message: 'وضعیت معتبر نیست' }) status: (typeof ADMIN_JOB_TARGETS)[number];
  @IsString(reasonMsg) @IsNotEmpty(reasonMsg) @MaxLength(300) reason: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(REJECT_REASONS, { message: 'دلیل معتبر نیست' }) reasonCode?: RejectReason;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `DispatchService` writes** — add to `dispatch.service.ts` (imports: `BadRequestException, ConflictException, ForbiddenException` from `@nestjs/common`; `PickupAt`, `JobStatus` types; `Permission` from `../../common/permissions.js`; `faDigits` from `../../common/utils/fa.js`; `assertObjectId`; `deliveryOf`; `OrderDelivery`; `LIVE`, `cancelStage` from `job-model.js`; `raced` from `jobs.service.js`; `pricingSnapshot` from `provider-pricing.js`; the new DTO types):

```ts
interface ResolvedJobDelivery { method: DeliveryMethod; deliveryProviderId?: Types.ObjectId; pickupAt?: PickupAt; fee: number; orderDelivery: OrderDelivery }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ writes (§54, §60, §61, §71)

  async requirePermission(userId: string, permission: Permission): Promise<void> {
    const u = await this.users.findById(userId, { role: 1, permissions: 1 }).lean();
    if (!u || u.role !== 'admin' || (u.permissions && !u.permissions.includes(permission))) throw new ForbiddenException('به این بخش دسترسی ندارید');
  }

  private async liveOrder(orderId: unknown): Promise<OrderRow> {
    const order = (await this.orders.findById(orderId).lean()) as OrderRow | null;
    if (!order) throw new NotFoundException('سفارش یافت نشد');
    if (order.status === 'cancelled' || order.status === 'delivered') throw new BadRequestException('این سفارش لغو یا تحویل شده است');
    if (order.status === 'pending_payment') throw new BadRequestException('این سفارش هنوز پرداخت نشده است');
    return order;
  }

  private async providerById(id: string): Promise<CenterRow> {
    assertObjectId(id, 'مجری');
    const p = (await this.centers.findById(id).lean()) as CenterRow | null;
    if (!p) throw new NotFoundException('مجری یافت نشد');
    return p;
  }

  /** Contract «Delivery»: courier ↔ provider for a home delivery, point ↔ provider shop for a pickup; never flips the customer's choice. */
  private async resolveJobDelivery(order: OrderRow, p: CenterRow | null, dto: { deliveryMethod?: DeliveryMethod; deliveryProviderId?: string; pickupAt?: PickupAt }): Promise<ResolvedJobDelivery> {
    const current = deliveryOf(order);
    const pickup = current.method === 'customer_pickup';
    const method: DeliveryMethod = dto.deliveryMethod ?? (pickup ? 'customer_pickup' : 'courier');
    if ((method === 'customer_pickup') !== pickup) throw new BadRequestException('روش تحویل انتخابی مشتری (ارسال یا تحویل حضوری) تغییر نمی‌کند');
    if (method === 'provider') {
      const dp = dto.deliveryProviderId ? await this.providerById(dto.deliveryProviderId) : p;
      if (!dp || !dp.canDeliver || (dp.status ?? 'active') !== 'active') throw new BadRequestException('مجری تحویل باید فعال باشد و امکان ارسال داشته باشد');
      return { method, deliveryProviderId: dp._id, fee: dp.deliveryFee ?? 0, orderDelivery: { ...current, method } };
    }
    const { providerShop: _shop, pickupAt: _at, ...rest } = current;
    if (method === 'customer_pickup') {
      const pickupAt: PickupAt = dto.pickupAt ?? 'point';
      if (pickupAt === 'provider') {
        if (!p) throw new BadRequestException('ابتدا مجری سفارش را تعیین کنید');
        if (!p.allowCustomerPickup) throw new BadRequestException('تحویل حضوری در محل این مجری مجاز نیست');
        const h = p.workingHours?.[0];
        const providerShop = { id: String(p._id), name: p.businessName || p.name, address: p.address ?? '', phone: p.phone ?? '', hours: h ? faDigits(`${h.from} تا ${h.to}`) : '', kind: 'provider' as const };
        return { method, pickupAt, fee: 0, orderDelivery: { ...rest, method, pickupAt, providerShop } };
      }
      return { method, pickupAt, fee: 0, orderDelivery: { ...rest, method, pickupAt } };
    }
    return { method: 'courier', fee: 0, orderDelivery: { ...rest, method: 'courier' } };
  }

  async assign(id: string, dto: AssignJobDto, by: string): Promise<DispatchJobDetail> {
    const job = await this.jobs.byId(id);
    if (job.status !== 'unassigned') throw new ConflictException('این سفارش قبلاً تخصیص داده شده است؛ برای تغییر مجری از «تغییر مجری» استفاده کنید');
    return this.give(job, dto, by, 'assigned');
  }

  async reassign(id: string, dto: AssignJobDto, by: string): Promise<DispatchJobDetail> {
    const job = await this.jobs.byId(id);
    if (!HELD.includes(job.status)) throw new BadRequestException('این سفارش در وضعیتی نیست که بتوان مجری آن را تغییر داد');
    if (!dto.reason?.trim()) throw new BadRequestException('دلیل را بنویسید');
    if (String(job.providerId) === dto.providerId) throw new BadRequestException('این سفارش هم‌اکنون به همین مجری سپرده شده است');
    return this.give(job, dto, by, 'reassigned');
  }

  /** Assign / reassign: hard checks never pass (§86), soft ones need override + reason (§71), a missing price needs confirmation (§53). */
  private async give(job: JobDocument, dto: AssignJobDto, by: string, action: 'assigned' | 'reassigned'): Promise<DispatchJobDetail> {
    const order = await this.liveOrder(job.orderId);
    const p = await this.providerById(dto.providerId);
    const delivery = await this.resolveJobDelivery(order, p, dto);
    const { check, quote } = await this.evaluate(job, order, p, { needsDelivery: false });
    if (!check.assignable) throw new BadRequestException({ statusCode: 400, code: 'NOT_CAPABLE', message: 'این مجری توانایی انجام این سفارش را ندارد', problems: check.hardProblems });
    const overriding = !check.eligible;
    if (overriding && !dto.override) {
      throw new ConflictException({ statusCode: 409, code: 'PROVIDER_NOT_ELIGIBLE', message: 'این مجری شرایط دریافت سفارش را ندارد؛ برای تخصیص با وجود این شرایط دلیل را بنویسید', problems: check.problems });
    }
    if (overriding && !dto.reason?.trim()) throw new BadRequestException('دلیل نادیده‌گرفتن شرایط را بنویسید');
    if (quote.subtotal === null && !dto.confirmMissingPrice) {
      throw new ConflictException({ statusCode: 409, code: 'PROVIDER_PRICE_MISSING', message: 'قیمت مجری تنظیم نشده', missing: quote.missing.map((m) => m.label) });
    }
    const now = new Date();
    const { dispatch } = await this.catalog.getSettings();
    const snapshot = pricingSnapshot({
      providerId: String(p._id), quote, at: now, confirmedMissing: quote.subtotal === null,
      delivery: delivery.deliveryProviderId ? { providerId: String(delivery.deliveryProviderId), fee: delivery.fee } : null,
    });
    const assignment = {
      id: new Types.ObjectId(), providerId: p._id, assignedBy: new Types.ObjectId(by), assignedAt: now,
      respondBy: new Date(now.getTime() + dispatch.responseTimeoutMinutes * 60_000),
      ...(overriding ? { override: { reason: dto.reason!.trim(), problems: check.problems } } : {}),
    };
    const prev = job.providerId;
    const history = this.jobs.entry({ providerId: p._id, deliveryProviderId: delivery.deliveryProviderId }, action, 'assigned', { byRole: 'admin', by, reason: dto.reason }, now, {
      ...(prev ? { previousProviderId: prev, stage: cancelStage(job.status) } : {}), ...(overriding ? { override: true } : {}),
    });
    const updated = await this.jobModel.findOneAndUpdate(
      { _id: job._id, status: job.status, ...(prev ? { providerId: prev } : { providerId: { $exists: false } }) },
      {
        $set: {
          status: 'assigned', providerId: p._id, assignment, providerPricing: snapshot, deliveryMethod: delivery.method,
          ...(delivery.deliveryProviderId ? { deliveryProviderId: delivery.deliveryProviderId } : {}), ...(delivery.pickupAt ? { pickupAt: delivery.pickupAt } : {}),
        },
        ...(delivery.deliveryProviderId ? {} : { $unset: { deliveryProviderId: 1 } }),
        $inc: { attempts: 1 },
        $push: { history, pricingHistory: snapshot },
        ...(prev ? { $addToSet: { releasedProviderIds: prev } } : {}),
      },
      { returnDocument: 'after' },
    );
    if (!updated) throw raced();
    await this.orders.updateOne({ _id: order._id }, { $set: { centerId: p._id, providerPricing: snapshot, delivery: delivery.orderDelivery }, $inc: { __v: 1 } });
    if (prev) await this.notifier.provider('job_withdrawn', prev, updated);
    const oldDp = job.deliveryProviderId;
    if (oldDp && String(oldDp) !== String(prev) && String(oldDp) !== String(delivery.deliveryProviderId)) await this.notifier.provider('job_withdrawn', oldDp, updated);
    await this.notifier.provider('job_assigned', p._id, updated);
    if (delivery.deliveryProviderId && String(delivery.deliveryProviderId) !== String(p._id)) await this.notifier.provider('job_delivery_assigned', delivery.deliveryProviderId, updated);
    return this.detail(String(updated._id));
  }

  async unassign(id: string, reason: string, by: string): Promise<DispatchJobDetail> {
    const job = await this.jobs.byId(id);
    if (!HELD.includes(job.status)) throw new BadRequestException('این سفارش در وضعیتی نیست که بتوان مجری آن را تغییر داد');
    await this.jobs.giveBack(job, 'unassigned', { byRole: 'admin', by, reason });
    return this.detail(String(job._id));
  }

  async updateDelivery(id: string, dto: DeliveryChangeDto, by: string): Promise<DispatchJobDetail> {
    const job = await this.jobs.byId(id);
    if (!(['unassigned', ...HELD, 'ready'] as JobStatus[]).includes(job.status)) throw new BadRequestException('روش تحویل این سفارش دیگر قابل تغییر نیست');
    const order = await this.liveOrder(job.orderId);
    const p = job.providerId ? await this.providerById(String(job.providerId)) : null;
    const d = await this.resolveJobDelivery(order, p, dto);
    const now = new Date();
    const pp = job.providerPricing;
    const pricing = pp
      ? { ...pp, delivery: d.deliveryProviderId ? { providerId: String(d.deliveryProviderId), fee: d.fee } : null,
          payoutBasis: pp.providerSubtotal === null ? null : pp.providerSubtotal + (d.deliveryProviderId && String(d.deliveryProviderId) === pp.providerId ? d.fee : 0) }
      : undefined;
    const updated = await this.jobModel.findOneAndUpdate(
      { _id: job._id, status: job.status },
      {
        $set: { deliveryMethod: d.method, ...(d.pickupAt ? { pickupAt: d.pickupAt } : {}), ...(d.deliveryProviderId ? { deliveryProviderId: d.deliveryProviderId } : {}), ...(pricing ? { providerPricing: pricing } : {}) },
        ...(d.deliveryProviderId ? {} : { $unset: { deliveryProviderId: 1 } }),
        $push: { history: this.jobs.entry({ providerId: job.providerId, deliveryProviderId: d.deliveryProviderId }, 'delivery_changed', job.status, { byRole: 'admin', by, reason: dto.reason }, now) },
      },
      { returnDocument: 'after' },
    );
    if (!updated) throw raced();
    await this.orders.updateOne({ _id: order._id }, { $set: { delivery: d.orderDelivery, ...(pricing ? { providerPricing: pricing } : {}) }, $inc: { __v: 1 } });
    if (job.providerId) await this.notifier.provider('job_changed', job.providerId, updated);
    const oldDp = job.deliveryProviderId;
    if (oldDp && String(oldDp) !== String(job.providerId) && String(oldDp) !== String(d.deliveryProviderId)) await this.notifier.provider('job_withdrawn', oldDp, updated);
    if (d.deliveryProviderId && String(d.deliveryProviderId) !== String(job.providerId) && String(d.deliveryProviderId) !== String(oldDp)) await this.notifier.provider('job_delivery_assigned', d.deliveryProviderId, updated);
    return this.detail(id);
  }

  async update(id: string, dto: UpdateJobDto, by: string): Promise<DispatchJobDetail> {
    const job = await this.jobs.byId(id);
    if (!LIVE.includes(job.status)) throw new BadRequestException('این سفارش لغو یا تحویل شده است');
    const deadline = dto.deadline ? new Date(dto.deadline) : undefined;
    if (!dto.priority && !deadline) return this.detail(id);
    const now = new Date();
    const updated = await this.jobModel.findOneAndUpdate(
      { _id: job._id, status: job.status },
      {
        $set: { ...(dto.priority ? { priority: dto.priority } : {}), ...(deadline ? { deadline } : {}) },
        ...(deadline ? { $unset: { deadlineNotifiedAt: 1, lateAlertedAt: 1 } } : {}),
        $push: { history: this.jobs.entry(job, 'updated', job.status, { byRole: 'admin', by }, now) },
      },
      { returnDocument: 'after' },
    );
    if (!updated) throw raced();
    if (deadline && job.providerId) await this.notifier.provider('job_changed', job.providerId, updated);
    return this.detail(id);
  }

  /** On behalf of the provider (`provider_order_accept`), e.g. after a phone call. */
  async respond(id: string, dto: RespondDto, by: string): Promise<DispatchJobDetail> {
    if (!dto.reason?.trim()) throw new BadRequestException('دلیل را بنویسید');
    const job = await this.jobs.byId(id);
    if (dto.accept) await this.jobs.move(String(job._id), 'accepted', { actor: 'admin', byRole: 'admin', by, reason: dto.reason });
    else await this.jobs.giveBack(job, 'rejected', { byRole: 'admin', by, reason: dto.reason, reasonCode: dto.reasonCode ?? 'other', ...(job.assignment ? { assignmentId: String(job.assignment.id) } : {}) });
    return this.detail(String(job._id));
  }

  /** On behalf of the provider/courier (`provider_order_status`), incl. «تکمیل» and pickups at a Digital Sarv point. */
  async setStatus(id: string, dto: AdminJobStatusDto, by: string): Promise<DispatchJobDetail> {
    if (!dto.reason?.trim()) throw new BadRequestException('دلیل را بنویسید');
    const job = await this.jobs.byId(id);
    await this.jobs.move(String(job._id), dto.status, { actor: 'admin', byRole: 'admin', by, reason: dto.reason, reasonCode: dto.reasonCode });
    return this.detail(String(job._id));
  }

  /** v1 `PATCH /admin/orders/:id/assign { centerId }` — delegated with the dispatch permissions (the v1 route has none). */
  async assignFromOrder(orderId: string, centerId: string | null, by: string): Promise<void> {
    assertObjectId(orderId, 'سفارش');
    let job = await this.jobs.forOrder(new Types.ObjectId(orderId));
    if (!job) {
      const order = await this.liveOrder(new Types.ObjectId(orderId));
      job = await this.jobs.ensureJob(order as never);
      if (!job) throw new NotFoundException('سفارش یافت نشد');
    }
    if (centerId === null) {
      if (!HELD.includes(job.status)) return;
      await this.requirePermission(by, 'dispatch_reassign');
      await this.unassign(String(job._id), 'لغو از صفحه سفارش', by);
      return;
    }
    if (job.status === 'unassigned') {
      await this.requirePermission(by, 'dispatch_assign');
      await this.assign(String(job._id), { providerId: centerId } as AssignJobDto, by);
      return;
    }
    await this.requirePermission(by, 'dispatch_reassign');
    await this.reassign(String(job._id), { providerId: centerId, reason: 'تغییر از صفحه سفارش' } as AssignJobDto, by);
  }
```

```ts
export const PROVIDER_JOB_TARGETS = ['in_production', 'ready', 'out_for_delivery', 'ready_for_pickup', 'delivered', 'failed'] as const;

export class RejectJobDto {
  @IsIn(REJECT_REASONS, { message: 'دلیل رد را انتخاب کنید' }) reasonCode: RejectReason;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() @MaxLength(300, { message: 'توضیح حداکثر ۳۰۰ کاراکتر است' }) note?: string;
}

export class ProviderJobStatusDto {
  @IsIn(PROVIDER_JOB_TARGETS, { message: 'وضعیت معتبر نیست' }) status: (typeof PROVIDER_JOB_TARGETS)[number];
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(REJECT_REASONS, { message: 'دلیل را انتخاب کنید' }) reasonCode?: RejectReason;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() @MaxLength(300, { message: 'توضیح حداکثر ۳۰۰ کاراکتر است' }) note?: string;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Provider views** — append to `job-json.ts` (imports: `DELIVERY_METHOD_LABELS` from constants; `qcLabelsOf` from order-helpers; `fa` from `../../common/utils/fa.js`; `DELIVERY_VISIBLE, productionBlocked` from job-model; `uploadJson` type via `ReturnType`):

```ts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------- provider views (§57, §62, §64)

export type ProviderRole = 'production' | 'delivery';
export type UploadView = { id: string; name: string; size: number; mime: string; purpose: string; pages?: number };

export interface ProviderJobView {
  id: string; orderCode: string; status: JobStatus; statusLabel: string; roles: ProviderRole[];
  priority: JobPriority; deadline: string; late: boolean; assignedAt?: string; respondBy?: string;
  services: ProviderService[]; summary: string; delivery: { method: DeliveryMethod; label: string; pickupAt?: PickupAt };
  providerSubtotal: number | null;
}
export interface ProviderWithdrawnView { id: string; orderCode: string; withdrawn: true; at: string; label: string }
export interface ProviderJobLine { kind: 'child' | 'service'; label: string; detail: string; quantity: number; options: Record<string, string | number | boolean>; childName?: string; note?: string }
export interface ProviderJobDetail extends ProviderJobView {
  lines: ProviderJobLine[]; files: UploadView[]; qc: { labels: string[]; done: boolean[] } | null;
  pricing: { lines: { label: string; unitPrice: number; quantity: number; subtotal: number }[]; missing: string[]; subtotal: number | null } | null;
  contact: { name: string; phone: string; address: string; note: string } | null;
  collectFrom: { name: string; address: string; phone: string } | null;
  homePickup: boolean; itemsReceived: boolean;
  history: { at: string; status: JobStatus; label: string; note?: string }[];
}

/** The caller's roles on a job: production (holder) and/or delivery (from `ready` on). */
export function rolesOf(job: Pick<JobRow, 'providerId' | 'deliveryProviderId' | 'status'>, me: string): ProviderRole[] {
  const roles: ProviderRole[] = [];
  if (job.providerId && String(job.providerId) === me) roles.push('production');
  if (job.deliveryProviderId && String(job.deliveryProviderId) === me && DELIVERY_VISIBLE.includes(job.status)) roles.push('delivery');
  return roles;
}

export function providerJobView(job: JobRow, me: string, summary: string, now: Date): ProviderJobView {
  const roles = rolesOf(job, me);
  const production = roles.includes('production');
  const own = production && job.providerPricing?.providerId === me ? job.providerPricing : null;
  return {
    id: String(job._id), orderCode: job.orderCode, status: job.status, statusLabel: JOB_STATUS_LABELS[job.status], roles,
    priority: job.priority, deadline: iso(job.deadline)!, late: isLate(job, now),
    ...(production && job.assignment ? { assignedAt: iso(job.assignment.assignedAt), ...(job.status === 'assigned' ? { respondBy: iso(job.assignment.respondBy) } : {}) } : {}),
    services: production ? (job.services ?? []) : [], summary: production ? summary : 'ارسال سفارش',
    delivery: { method: job.deliveryMethod, label: DELIVERY_METHOD_LABELS[job.deliveryMethod] + (job.pickupAt === 'provider' ? ' (در محل شما)' : ''), ...(job.pickupAt ? { pickupAt: job.pickupAt } : {}) },
    providerSubtotal: own ? own.providerSubtotal : null,
  };
}

const WITHDRAWN_LABEL: Partial<Record<JobAction, string>> = { rejected: 'رد شده توسط شما', timeout: 'بدون پاسخ در مهلت', failed: 'اعلام عدم امکان انجام', unassigned: 'از شما گرفته شد', reassigned: 'از شما گرفته شد' };

/** A job the caller lost: only the order code, when and why (§64 — the job now belongs to someone else or nobody). */
export function withdrawnView(job: JobRow, me: string): ProviderWithdrawnView {
  const e = [...(job.history ?? [])].reverse().find((h) =>
    (h.action === 'reassigned' && String(h.previousProviderId) === me) ||
    (['rejected', 'timeout', 'failed', 'unassigned'].includes(h.action) && String(h.providerId) === me));
  return { id: String(job._id), orderCode: job.orderCode, withdrawn: true, at: iso(e?.at ?? job.updatedAt)!, label: (e && WITHDRAWN_LABEL[e.action]) || 'از شما گرفته شد' };
}

const OPTION_KEYS: Record<string, string[]> = {
  black_white_printing: ['pages', 'scope', 'from', 'to', 'size', 'sides', 'copies', 'binding', 'spiralColor', 'staple', 'laminate'],
  color_printing: ['pages', 'scope', 'from', 'to', 'size', 'sides', 'copies', 'colorType', 'paper', 'mixed', 'colorPages', 'binding', 'spiralColor', 'staple', 'laminate'],
  print: ['pages', 'scope', 'from', 'to', 'size', 'ink', 'paper', 'sides', 'copies', 'colorPages', 'binding', 'spiralColor', 'staple', 'laminate'],
  docs: ['pages', 'scope', 'pagePages', 'ink', 'sides', 'copies', 'binding', 'bindColor', 'spiralColor', 'stamp', 'colorPages', 'coverTitle', 'coverBack', 'fullName'],
  flyer: ['mode', 'qty', 'ink', 'size', 'paper', 'sides'],
  cart: ['brand', 'model', 'cartType', 'type', 'count'],
  repair: ['brand', 'model', 'problem', 'device', 'warranty'],
};
const primitive = (v: unknown): v is string | number | boolean => typeof v === 'string' || typeof v === 'number' || typeof v === 'boolean';

function lineOptions(kind: string, spec: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, string | number | boolean> {
  const out: Record<string, string | number | boolean> = {};
  for (const k of OPTION_KEYS[kind] ?? []) if (primitive(spec[k]) && spec[k] !== '') out[k] = spec[k] as string | number | boolean;
  const brief = spec.brief && typeof spec.brief === 'object' ? (spec.brief as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(brief)) if (primitive(v) && v !== '') out[`brief_${k}`] = v;
  return out;
}

function lineQuantity(s: { kind: string; spec?: unknown; pricing?: { lines: { comboKey: string; quantity: number }[] } }): number {
  const spec = (s.spec ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (s.kind === 'black_white_printing' || s.kind === 'color_printing') return (s.pricing?.lines ?? []).filter((l) => /^(bw|color)\|/.test(l.comboKey)).reduce((t, l) => t + l.quantity, 0);
  if (s.kind === 'flyer') return Number(spec.qty) || 0;
  if (s.kind === 'cart') return Number(spec.count) || 1;
  return Number(spec.copies) || 1;
}

/** §57 work lines: service, options, quantity (paper, size, colour, side, binding are in `options` and `detail`). */
export function providerLines(order: Pick<Order, 'children' | 'services'>, names: { colors: Map<string, string>; extras: Map<string, string> }): ProviderJobLine[] {
  const children: ProviderJobLine[] = (order.children ?? []).map((c) => ({
    kind: 'child', label: 'فنری کتاب مدرسه', childName: c.name, quantity: c.books ?? 0,
    detail: [c.grade, `${fa(c.books ?? 0)} کتاب`, `فنری ${names.colors.get(c.color) ?? c.color}`, c.lined ? `${fa(c.linedCount ?? 0)} برگ خط‌دار` : '',
      (c.extras ?? []).map((k) => names.extras.get(k) ?? k).join('، ')].filter(Boolean).join(' · '),
    options: { grade: c.grade, books: c.books ?? 0, color: names.colors.get(c.color) ?? c.color, lined: !!c.lined, ...(c.lined ? { linedCount: c.linedCount ?? 0, linedPos: c.linedPos ?? 'all' } : {}), ...(c.labelText ? { labelText: c.labelText } : {}) },
    ...(c.note ? { note: c.note } : {}),
  }));
  const services: ProviderJobLine[] = (order.services ?? []).map((s) => {
    const spec = (s.spec ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
    return {
      kind: 'service', label: s.label, detail: s.detail, quantity: lineQuantity(s), options: lineOptions(s.kind, spec),
      ...(s.childName ? { childName: s.childName } : {}), ...(typeof spec.desc === 'string' && spec.desc ? { note: spec.desc } : {}),
    };
  });
  return [...children, ...services];
}

type ProviderOrder = Pick<Order, 'children' | 'services' | 'status' | 'customerName' | 'customerPhone' | 'pickup' | 'delivery' | 'qc' | 'qcLabels'>;

export function providerJobDetail(job: JobRow, order: ProviderOrder, me: string, ctx: {
  summary: string; lines: ProviderJobLine[]; files: UploadView[]; collectFrom: ProviderJobDetail['collectFrom']; now: Date;
}): ProviderJobDetail {
  const view = providerJobView(job, me, ctx.summary, ctx.now);
  const production = view.roles.includes('production');
  const delivering = view.roles.includes('delivery');
  const handover = production && job.deliveryMethod === 'customer_pickup' && job.pickupAt === 'provider';
  const note = deliveryOf(order).note;
  const own = production && job.providerPricing?.providerId === me ? job.providerPricing : null;
  const labels = qcLabelsOf(order);
  return {
    ...view,
    lines: production ? ctx.lines : [],
    files: production ? ctx.files : [],
    qc: production ? { labels, done: labels.map((_, i) => order.qc?.[i] === true) } : null,
    pricing: own ? { lines: own.lines.map((l) => ({ label: l.label, unitPrice: l.unitPrice, quantity: l.quantity, subtotal: l.subtotal })), missing: own.missing.map((m) => m.label), subtotal: own.providerSubtotal } : null,
    // §62: only who delivers (or hands over at its shop) gets the customer's contact; the address only for delivery
    contact: delivering || handover ? { name: order.customerName, phone: order.pickup?.phone || order.customerPhone, address: delivering ? (order.pickup?.address ?? '') : '', note: delivering ? note : '' } : null,
    collectFrom: delivering && !production ? ctx.collectFrom : null,
    homePickup: job.homePickup, itemsReceived: !productionBlocked({ status: order.status, homePickup: job.homePickup }),
    history: (job.history ?? [])
      .filter((e) => String(e.providerId) === me || String(e.deliveryProviderId) === me)
      .map((e) => ({ at: iso(e.at)!, status: e.status, label: JOB_STATUS_LABELS[e.status], ...(e.byRole === 'provider' && e.reason ? { note: e.reason } : {}) })),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `apps/api/src/modules/dispatch/provider-jobs.service.ts`**

```ts
import { BadRequestException, Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
import { Model, Types } from 'mongoose';
import { JOB_STATUS_LABELS, REJECT_REASON_LABELS } from '../../common/constants.js';
import { assertObjectId } from '../../common/utils/object-id.js';
import { pageParams } from '../../common/utils/pagination.js';
import { Color } from '../catalog/schemas/color.schema.js';
import { Extra } from '../catalog/schemas/extra.schema.js';
import { Center } from '../centers/center.schema.js';
import { orderSummary, qcLabelsOf } from '../orders/order-helpers.js';
import { Order } from '../orders/order.schema.js';
import { draftFileRefs, isObjectIdString } from '../uploads/upload-rules.js';
import { Upload, uploadJson } from '../uploads/upload.schema.js';
import type { ProviderJobStatusDto, ProviderJobsQueryDto, RejectJobDto } from './dto/dispatch.dto.js';
import {
  providerJobDetail, providerJobView, providerLines, rolesOf, withdrawnView,
  type JobRow, type ProviderJobDetail, type ProviderJobView, type ProviderWithdrawnView, type UploadView,
} from './job-json.js';
import { DELIVERY_VISIBLE, PROVIDER_TABS, canMove, providerTabFilter, type ProviderTab } from './job-model.js';
import { Job } from './job.schema.js';
import { JobsService, raced } from './jobs.service.js';

type OrderRow = Order & { _id: Types.ObjectId };
const NOT_FOUND = 'سفارش یافت نشد';

/** «سرو مجری» orders (§57–59): only the caller's jobs, with the information it is allowed to see (§62, §64). */
@Injectable()
export class ProviderJobsService {
  constructor(
    @InjectModel(Job.name) private readonly jobModel: Model<Job>,
    @InjectModel(Order.name) private readonly orders: Model<Order>,
    @InjectModel(Center.name) private readonly centers: Model<Center>,
    @InjectModel(Upload.name) private readonly uploads: Model<Upload>,
    @InjectModel(Color.name) private readonly colors: Model<Color>,
    @InjectModel(Extra.name) private readonly extras: Model<Extra>,
    private readonly jobs: JobsService,
  ) {}

  private tabFilter(tab: ProviderTab, pid: Types.ObjectId): Record<string, unknown> {
    if (tab === 'cancelled') return { $or: [{ providerId: pid, status: 'cancelled' }, { releasedProviderIds: pid, providerId: { $ne: pid } }] };
    return providerTabFilter(tab, pid);
  }

  async list(providerId: string, q: ProviderJobsQueryDto): Promise<{ items: (ProviderJobView | ProviderWithdrawnView)[]; total: number; page: number; limit: number; counts: Record<ProviderTab, number> }> {
    const pid = new Types.ObjectId(providerId);
    const { page, limit, skip } = pageParams(q);
    const tab = q.tab ?? 'new';
    const filter = this.tabFilter(tab, pid);
    const sort = tab === 'completed' || tab === 'cancelled' ? { updatedAt: -1 as const } : { deadline: 1 as const, _id: 1 as const };
    const [rows, total, ...counts] = await Promise.all([
      this.jobModel.find(filter).sort(sort).skip(skip).limit(limit).lean(),
      this.jobModel.countDocuments(filter),
      ...PROVIDER_TABS.map((t) => this.jobModel.countDocuments(this.tabFilter(t, pid))),
    ]);
    const jobs = rows as JobRow[];
    const ids = jobs.map((j) => j.orderId);
    const orders = ids.length ? ((await this.orders.find({ _id: { $in: ids } }, { children: 1, services: 1 }).lean()) as OrderRow[]) : [];
    const summaries = new Map(orders.map((o) => [String(o._id), orderSummary(o)]));
    const now = new Date();
    return {
      items: jobs.map((j) => (tab === 'cancelled' && String(j.providerId) !== providerId ? withdrawnView(j, providerId) : providerJobView(j, providerId, summaries.get(String(j.orderId)) ?? '', now))),
      total, page, limit,
      counts: Object.fromEntries(PROVIDER_TABS.map((t, i) => [t, counts[i]])) as Record<ProviderTab, number>,
    };
  }

  /** The caller's job (production holder, or delivery provider from `ready` on) — anything else is 404. */
  private async own(providerId: string, id: string): Promise<JobRow> {
    assertObjectId(id, 'سفارش');
    const pid = new Types.ObjectId(providerId);
    const job = await this.jobModel.findOne({ _id: new Types.ObjectId(id), $or: [{ providerId: pid }, { deliveryProviderId: pid, status: { $in: DELIVERY_VISIBLE } }] }).lean();
    if (!job) throw new NotFoundException(NOT_FOUND);
    return job as JobRow;
  }

  private async render(job: JobRow, providerId: string): Promise<ProviderJobDetail> {
    const order = (await this.orders.findById(job.orderId).lean()) as OrderRow | null;
    if (!order) throw new NotFoundException(NOT_FOUND);
    const production = rolesOf(job, providerId).includes('production');
    const [colors, extras, files, collectFrom] = await Promise.all([
      production ? this.colors.find().lean() : [],
      production ? this.extras.find().lean() : [],
      production ? this.files(order) : [],
      !production && job.providerId ? this.collectFrom(job.providerId) : null,
    ]);
    const names = { colors: new Map(colors.map((c) => [c.key, c.name])), extras: new Map(extras.map((x) => [x.key, x.label])) };
    return providerJobDetail(job, order, providerId, { summary: orderSummary(order), lines: production ? providerLines(order, names) : [], files, collectFrom, now: new Date() });
  }

  /** The order's line files (never the courier's pickup photos, §65). */
  private async files(order: OrderRow): Promise<UploadView[]> {
    const ids = [...new Set(draftFileRefs(order.services).map((r) => r.id).filter(isObjectIdString))];
    if (!ids.length) return [];
    const docs = await this.uploads.find({ _id: { $in: ids.map((i) => new Types.ObjectId(i)) }, purpose: { $ne: 'pickup' }, deletedAt: { $exists: false } }).lean();
    return docs.map((d) => uploadJson(d as never));
  }

  private async collectFrom(providerId: unknown): Promise<ProviderJobDetail['collectFrom']> {
    const c = await this.centers.findById(providerId, { name: 1, businessName: 1, address: 1, phone: 1 }).lean();
    return c ? { name: c.businessName || c.name, address: c.address ?? '', phone: c.phone ?? '' } : null;
  }

  async detail(providerId: string, id: string): Promise<ProviderJobDetail> {
    let job = await this.own(providerId, id);
    if (job.status === 'assigned' && String(job.providerId) === providerId && !job.assignment?.seenAt) {
      await this.jobModel.updateOne({ _id: job._id, status: 'assigned', 'assignment.seenAt': { $exists: false } }, { $set: { 'assignment.seenAt': new Date() } });
      job = await this.own(providerId, id);
    }
    return this.render(job, providerId);
  }

  private assertWaiting(job: JobRow, providerId: string) {
    if (job.status !== 'assigned' || String(job.providerId) !== providerId) throw new BadRequestException('این سفارش منتظر پاسخ شما نیست');
  }

  async accept(providerId: string, id: string, userId: string): Promise<ProviderJobDetail> {
    this.assertWaiting(await this.own(providerId, id), providerId);
    await this.jobs.move(id, 'accepted', { actor: 'production', byRole: 'provider', by: userId, providerId });
    return this.render(await this.own(providerId, id), providerId);
  }

  async reject(providerId: string, id: string, dto: RejectJobDto, userId: string): Promise<{ ok: true }> {
    this.assertWaiting(await this.own(providerId, id), providerId);
    if (dto.reasonCode === 'other' && !dto.note?.trim()) throw new BadRequestException('برای «سایر» توضیح بنویسید');
    const job = await this.jobs.byId(id);
    await this.jobs.giveBack(job, 'rejected', {
      byRole: 'provider', by: userId, providerId, reasonCode: dto.reasonCode, reason: dto.note?.trim() || REJECT_REASON_LABELS[dto.reasonCode],
      ...(job.assignment ? { assignmentId: String(job.assignment.id) } : {}),
    });
    return { ok: true };
  }

  async setStatus(providerId: string, id: string, dto: ProviderJobStatusDto, userId: string): Promise<ProviderJobDetail | { ok: true }> {
    const job = await this.own(providerId, id);
    if (job.status === 'cancelled') throw new BadRequestException('این سفارش لغو شده است');
    const ctx = { method: job.deliveryMethod, pickupAt: job.pickupAt };
    const actor = rolesOf(job, providerId).find((r) => canMove(job.status, dto.status, r, ctx));
    if (!actor) throw new BadRequestException(`تغییر وضعیت از «${JOB_STATUS_LABELS[job.status]}» به «${JOB_STATUS_LABELS[dto.status]}» مجاز نیست`);
    if (dto.status === 'failed' && !dto.reasonCode) throw new BadRequestException('دلیل را انتخاب کنید');
    await this.jobs.move(id, dto.status, {
      actor, byRole: 'provider', by: userId, providerId, reasonCode: dto.reasonCode,
      reason: dto.note?.trim() || (dto.reasonCode ? REJECT_REASON_LABELS[dto.reasonCode] : undefined),
    });
    if (dto.status === 'failed') return { ok: true };
    return this.render(await this.own(providerId, id), providerId);
  }

  /** The production provider ticks the order's QC checklist (the `ready` step needs it complete). One item per write. */
  async setQc(providerId: string, id: string, index: number, done: boolean): Promise<ProviderJobDetail> {
    const job = await this.own(providerId, id);
    if (String(job.providerId) !== providerId || !['accepted', 'in_production'].includes(job.status)) {
      throw new BadRequestException('چک‌لیست کنترل کیفیت فقط حین انجام سفارش ثبت می‌شود');
    }
    const order = (await this.orders.findById(job.orderId).lean()) as OrderRow | null;
    if (!order) throw new NotFoundException(NOT_FOUND);
    if (index >= qcLabelsOf(order).length) throw new BadRequestException('ردیف چک‌لیست معتبر نیست');
    const res = await this.orders.updateOne({ _id: order._id, status: { $nin: ['cancelled', 'delivered'] } }, { $set: { [`qc.${index}`]: done }, $inc: { __v: 1 } });
    if (!res.modifiedCount) throw raced();
    return this.render(await this.own(providerId, id), providerId);
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Controller** — append to `dispatch.controller.ts` (imports `Patch`, `Post`, `HttpCode`, `CurrentUser`, `AuthUser`, `CurrentProvider`, `ProviderScopeGuard` from `../centers/provider-scope.guard.js`, `QcDto` from `../orders/dto/orders.dto.js`):

```ts
/** «سرو مجری» orders — provider id only from ProviderScopeGuard (never URL/body, §64). */
@Controller('provider/jobs')
@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, RolesGuard, ProviderScopeGuard)
@Roles('provider')
export class ProviderJobsController {
  constructor(private readonly jobs: ProviderJobsService) {}

  @Get() list(@CurrentProvider() pid: string, @Query() q: ProviderJobsQueryDto) { return this.jobs.list(pid, q); }
  @Get(':id') detail(@CurrentProvider() pid: string, @Param('id') id: string) { return this.jobs.detail(pid, id); }

  @Post(':id/accept') @HttpCode(200)
  accept(@CurrentProvider() pid: string, @Param('id') id: string, @CurrentUser() user: AuthUser) { return this.jobs.accept(pid, id, user.id); }

  @Post(':id/reject') @HttpCode(200)
  reject(@CurrentProvider() pid: string, @Param('id') id: string, @Body() dto: RejectJobDto, @CurrentUser() user: AuthUser) { return this.jobs.reject(pid, id, dto, user.id); }

  @Post(':id/status') @HttpCode(200)
  status(@CurrentProvider() pid: string, @Param('id') id: string, @Body() dto: ProviderJobStatusDto, @CurrentUser() user: AuthUser) { return this.jobs.setStatus(pid, id, dto, user.id); }

  @Patch(':id/qc')
  qc(@CurrentProvider() pid: string, @Param('id') id: string, @Body() dto: QcDto) { return this.jobs.setQc(pid, id, dto.index, dto.done); }
}
```

```ts
  const schoolDraft = { children: [{ name: 'a', grade: 'g', color: 'blue', books: 1 }], services: [], payMethod: 'wallet', pickup: { address: 'addr', phone: '09123456789', date: tomorrow, slot: '۱۰ تا ۱۲' } } as unknown as OrderDraftDto;

  it('a wallet order gets its job when it is placed', async () => {
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `CourierService`:

Constructor gains (last) `@InjectModel(Job.name) private readonly jobs: Model<Job>, @InjectModel(Center.name) private readonly centers: Model<Center>, private readonly fulfilment: JobsService`. `CourierModule` imports `DispatchModule`.

```ts
export interface Place { name: string; address: string; phone: string }
export interface CourierTask { /* … existing fields … */ from?: Place; dropoff?: Place }

type JobInfo = { status: JobStatus; deliveryMethod: DeliveryMethod; place?: Place };

  /** Each order's job (status, delivery method) and its provider's address (§62: the courier needs where to collect/drop). */
  private async jobInfo(orders: OrderDocument[]): Promise<Map<string, JobInfo>> {
    if (!orders.length) return new Map();
    const jobs = await this.jobs.find({ orderId: { $in: orders.map((o) => o._id) }, seq: 1 }, { orderId: 1, status: 1, deliveryMethod: 1, providerId: 1 }).lean();
    const ids = [...new Set(jobs.filter((j) => j.providerId).map((j) => String(j.providerId)))];
    const providers = ids.length ? await this.centers.find({ _id: { $in: ids.map((i) => new Types.ObjectId(i)) } }, { name: 1, businessName: 1, address: 1, phone: 1 }).lean() : [];
    const places = new Map(providers.map((p) => [String(p._id), { name: p.businessName || p.name, address: p.address ?? '', phone: p.phone ?? '' }]));
    return new Map(jobs.map((j) => [String(j.orderId), { status: j.status, deliveryMethod: j.deliveryMethod, ...(j.providerId && places.get(String(j.providerId)) ? { place: places.get(String(j.providerId)) } : {}) }]));
  }
```

```ts
describe('v4.2 provider file access (§65)', () => {
  const P = new Types.ObjectId();
  const Q = new Types.ObjectId();
  const userP = new Types.ObjectId();
  const userQ = new Types.ObjectId();
  const orderId = new Types.ObjectId();
  const otherOrder = new Types.ObjectId();
  const as = (id: Types.ObjectId) => ({ id: String(id), role: 'provider' }) as never;

  function world() {
    const s = setup();
    s.users.docs.push({ _id: userP, role: 'provider', providerId: P }, { _id: userQ, role: 'provider', providerId: Q });
    s.centers.docs.push({ _id: P, name: 'P', userId: userP }, { _id: Q, name: 'Q', userId: userQ });
    s.jobs.docs.push({ _id: new Types.ObjectId(), orderId, seq: 1, status: 'in_production', providerId: P });
    const file = { _id: new Types.ObjectId(), ownerId: new Types.ObjectId(), purpose: 'docs', orderIds: [orderId] };
    const photo = { ...file, _id: new Types.ObjectId(), purpose: 'pickup' };
    const foreign = { ...file, _id: new Types.ObjectId(), orderIds: [otherOrder] };
    return { s, file, photo, foreign };
  }

  it('the provider holding the job reads the order’s line files, never the courier’s pickup photos', async () => {
    const { s, file, photo } = world();
    await expect(s.svc.canRead(file as never, as(userP))).resolves.toBe(true);
    await expect(s.svc.canRead(photo as never, as(userP))).resolves.toBe(false);
  });
  it('IDOR: another provider, another order, or a stale login is refused', async () => {
    const { s, file, foreign } = world();
    await expect(s.svc.canRead(file as never, as(userQ))).resolves.toBe(false);
    await expect(s.svc.canRead(foreign as never, as(userP))).resolves.toBe(false);
    s.centers.docs[0].userId = new Types.ObjectId(); // the login was relinked
    await expect(s.svc.canRead(file as never, as(userP))).resolves.toBe(false);
  });
  it('released provider loses file access; finished jobs give no access', async () => {
    const { s, file } = world();
    Object.assign(s.jobs.docs[0], { providerId: Q, releasedProviderIds: [P] });
    await expect(s.svc.canRead(file as never, as(userP))).resolves.toBe(false);
    Object.assign(s.jobs.docs[0], { providerId: P, status: 'delivered' });
    await expect(s.svc.canRead(file as never, as(userP))).resolves.toBe(false);
  });
});
```
