```ts
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { fakeModel, type Doc } from '../../../test/fake-model.js';
import { DEFAULT_DISPATCH } from '../catalog/catalog.defaults.js';
import { DispatchScheduler } from './dispatch.scheduler.js';
import { JobsService } from './jobs.service.js';

const P = new Types.ObjectId();
const NOW = new Date('2026-10-16T10:00:00Z');
const min = (m: number) => new Date(NOW.getTime() + m * 60_000);

function setup(jobOver: Doc = {}, settings: Doc = {}, centerOver: Doc = {}) {
  const order = { _id: new Types.ObjectId(), code: '10700', customerId: new Types.ObjectId(), status: 'confirmed', children: [], services: [], timeline: [], qc: [], payMethod: 'wallet', paid: true, quote: { total: 1 }, __v: 0 };
  const job = {
    _id: new Types.ObjectId(), orderId: order._id, seq: 1, orderCode: '10700', status: 'assigned', providerId: P, deliveryMethod: 'courier',
    deadline: min(600), attempts: 1, releasedProviderIds: [], history: [],
    assignment: { id: new Types.ObjectId(), providerId: P, assignedAt: min(-130), respondBy: min(-10) }, ...jobOver,
  };
  const jobsModel = fakeModel([job]);
  const orders = fakeModel([order]);
  const centers = fakeModel([{ _id: P, name: 'P', status: 'active', availableForOrders: true, ...centerOver }]);
  const notifier = { provider: vi.fn(async () => undefined), admins: vi.fn(async () => undefined), customer: vi.fn(async () => undefined) };
  const catalog = { getSettings: vi.fn(async () => ({ dispatch: { ...DEFAULT_DISPATCH, ...settings } })) };
  const jobs = new JobsService(jobsModel as never, orders as never, catalog as never, notifier as never);
  const sched = new DispatchScheduler(jobsModel as never, centers as never, jobs, catalog as never, notifier as never);
  return { sched, jobs, jobsModel, orders, notifier, job, id: String(job._id) };
}

describe('DispatchScheduler (D21)', () => {
  it('an unanswered assignment returns to the queue once, alerting the admins and telling the provider', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    expect(await s.sched.timeouts(NOW)).toBe(1);
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'unassigned' });
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0].history.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ action: 'timeout', byRole: 'system' });
    expect(await s.sched.timeouts(NOW)).toBe(0);
    expect(s.notifier.admins).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(s.notifier.admins).toHaveBeenCalledWith('job_timeout', expect.anything());
    expect(s.notifier.provider).toHaveBeenCalledWith('job_withdrawn', P, expect.anything());
  });
  it('not before respondBy', async () => {
    const s = setup({ assignment: { id: new Types.ObjectId(), providerId: P, assignedAt: min(-10), respondBy: min(110) } });
    expect(await s.sched.timeouts(NOW)).toBe(0);
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0].status).toBe('assigned');
  });
  it('timeout vs accept: one wins, no double alert', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const r = await Promise.allSettled([s.sched.timeouts(NOW), s.jobs.move(s.id, 'accepted', { actor: 'production', byRole: 'provider', providerId: String(P) })]);
    const status = s.jobsModel.docs[0].status;
    expect(['accepted', 'unassigned']).toContain(status);
    const timeoutAlerts = s.notifier.admins.mock.calls.filter(([e]) => e === 'job_timeout').length;
    if (status === 'accepted') {
      expect(timeoutAlerts).toBe(0);
    } else {
      expect(timeoutAlerts).toBe(1);
      expect(r[1].status).toBe('rejected');
    }
  });
  it('deadline reminder to the provider once; late alert to the admins once', async () => {
    const s = setup({ status: 'in_production', deadline: min(60) });
    expect(await s.sched.reminders(NOW, 120)).toBe(1);
    expect(await s.sched.reminders(NOW, 120)).toBe(0);
    expect(s.notifier.provider).toHaveBeenCalledWith('job_deadline', P, expect.anything());
    s.jobsModel.docs[0].deadline = min(-1);
    expect(await s.sched.late(NOW)).toBe(1);
    expect(await s.sched.late(NOW)).toBe(0);
    expect(s.notifier.admins).toHaveBeenCalledWith('job_late', expect.anything());
    expect(await setup({ status: 'in_production', deadline: min(60) }).sched.reminders(NOW, 0)).toBe(0); // 0 = off
  });
  it('a suspended provider holding a job, or a closed one with a pending assignment, alerts the admins once', async () => {
    const s = setup({ status: 'accepted' }, {}, { status: 'suspended' });
    expect(await s.sched.unavailable()).toBe(1);
    expect(await s.sched.unavailable()).toBe(0);
    expect(s.notifier.admins).toHaveBeenCalledWith('provider_unavailable', expect.anything());
    expect(await setup({ status: 'accepted' }, {}, { availableForOrders: false }).sched.unavailable()).toBe(0);
    expect(await setup({ status: 'assigned' }, {}, { availableForOrders: false }).sched.unavailable()).toBe(1);
  });
  it('delivered jobs complete after autoCompleteHours (0 = off)', async () => {
    const s = setup({ status: 'delivered', deliveredAt: min(-25 * 60) });
    s.orders.docs[0].status = 'delivered';
    expect(await s.sched.autoComplete(NOW, 24)).toBe(1);
    expect(s.jobsModel.docs[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'completed' });
    const t = setup({ status: 'delivered', deliveredAt: min(-60) });
    expect(await t.sched.autoComplete(NOW, 24)).toBe(0);
    expect(await setup({ status: 'delivered', deliveredAt: min(-48 * 60) }).sched.autoComplete(NOW, 0)).toBe(0);
  });
  it('tick runs every step with the stored settings and never throws', async () => {
    const s = setup({}, { responseTimeoutMinutes: 30 });
    await expect(s.sched.tick(NOW)).resolves.toMatchObject({ timeouts: 1 });
    s.jobsModel.find.mockImplementationOnce(() => { throw new Error('mongo down'); });
    await expect(s.sched.tick(NOW)).resolves.toBeDefined();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `apps/api/src/modules/dispatch/dispatch.scheduler.ts`**

```ts
import { Injectable, Logger, OnApplicationBootstrap } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
import { Interval } from '@nestjs/schedule';
import { Model } from 'mongoose';
import { CatalogService } from '../catalog/catalog.service.js';
import { Center } from '../centers/center.schema.js';
import { DispatchNotifier } from './dispatch-notifier.js';
import { HELD } from './job-model.js';
import { Job } from './job.schema.js';
import { JobsService } from './jobs.service.js';

export interface TickResult { timeouts: number; reminders: number; late: number; unavailable: number; completed: number; reconciled: { created: number; cancelled: number; delivered: number } }
const EMPTY: TickResult = { timeouts: 0, reminders: 0, late: 0, unavailable: 0, completed: 0, reconciled: { created: 0, cancelled: 0, delivered: 0 } };
const BATCH = 200;

/**
 * Dispatch housekeeping (D21, §68), every minute and at boot. Multi-instance safe: every effect is a conditional
 * update (status + assignment id, or a not-yet-set alert flag) and only the instance that wins it notifies.
 */
@Injectable()
export class DispatchScheduler implements OnApplicationBootstrap {
  private readonly logger = new Logger('DispatchScheduler');
  private running = false;

  constructor(
    @InjectModel(Job.name) private readonly jobModel: Model<Job>,
    @InjectModel(Center.name) private readonly centers: Model<Center>,
    private readonly jobs: JobsService,
    private readonly catalog: CatalogService,
    private readonly notifier: DispatchNotifier,
  ) {}

  onApplicationBootstrap() {
    void this.tick();
  }

  @Interval('dispatch-tick', 60_000)
  async tick(now: Date = new Date()): Promise<TickResult> {
    if (this.running) return EMPTY;
    this.running = true;
    try {
      const { dispatch } = await this.catalog.getSettings();
      return {
        timeouts: await this.timeouts(now),
        reminders: await this.reminders(now, dispatch.deadlineReminderMinutes),
        late: await this.late(now),
        unavailable: await this.unavailable(),
        completed: await this.autoComplete(now, dispatch.autoCompleteHours),
        reconciled: await this.jobs.reconcile(),
      };
    } catch (err) {
      this.logger.warn(`dispatch tick failed: ${(err as Error).message}`);
      return EMPTY;
    } finally {
      this.running = false;
    }
  }

  /** D21: no answer by `respondBy` → «تخصیص‌نیافته» + admin alert; the CAS on the assignment id lets a reassignment or an accept win. */
  async timeouts(now: Date): Promise<number> {
    const due = await this.jobModel.find({ status: 'assigned', 'assignment.respondBy': { $lte: now } }).limit(BATCH);
    let n = 0;
    for (const job of due) {
      try {
        await this.jobs.giveBack(job, 'timeout', { byRole: 'system', assignmentId: String(job.assignment!.id) });
        n++;
      } catch {
        // lost the race (accepted, rejected or reassigned meanwhile) — nothing to do
      }
    }
    return n;
  }

  /** Once per job: the deadline is within `minutes` and the work is not ready yet. */
  async reminders(now: Date, minutes: number): Promise<number> {
    if (minutes <= 0) return 0;
    const soon = new Date(now.getTime() + minutes * 60_000);
    const due = await this.jobModel.find({ status: { $in: ['accepted', 'in_production'] }, deadlineNotifiedAt: { $exists: false }, deadline: { $lte: soon, $gt: now } }).limit(BATCH).lean();
    let n = 0;
    for (const j of due) {
      const won = await this.jobModel.updateOne({ _id: j._id, deadlineNotifiedAt: { $exists: false } }, { $set: { deadlineNotifiedAt: now } });
      if (won.modifiedCount !== 1) continue;
      await this.notifier.provider('job_deadline', j.providerId, j);
      n++;
    }
    return n;
  }

  /** §68 «Delay»: once per job, the deadline passed and it is not ready. */
  async late(now: Date): Promise<number> {
    const due = await this.jobModel.find({ status: { $in: ['unassigned', ...HELD] }, lateAlertedAt: { $exists: false }, deadline: { $lt: now } }).limit(BATCH).lean();
    let n = 0;
    for (const j of due) {
      const won = await this.jobModel.updateOne({ _id: j._id, lateAlertedAt: { $exists: false } }, { $set: { lateAlertedAt: now } });
      if (won.modifiedCount !== 1) continue;
      await this.notifier.admins('job_late', j);
      n++;
    }
    return n;
  }

  /** §68 «Provider unavailable»: a suspended/inactive provider holding work, or a closed one with an unanswered assignment. */
  async unavailable(): Promise<number> {
    const [down, closed] = await Promise.all([
      this.centers.find({ status: { $in: ['inactive', 'suspended'] } }, { _id: 1 }).lean(),
      this.centers.find({ availableForOrders: false }, { _id: 1 }).lean(),
    ]);
    if (!down.length && !closed.length) return 0;
    const due = await this.jobModel.find({
      unavailableAlertedAt: { $exists: false },
      $or: [
        { providerId: { $in: down.map((c) => c._id) }, status: { $in: HELD } },
        { providerId: { $in: closed.map((c) => c._id) }, status: 'assigned' },
      ],
    }).limit(BATCH).lean();
    let n = 0;
    for (const j of due) {
      const won = await this.jobModel.updateOne({ _id: j._id, unavailableAlertedAt: { $exists: false } }, { $set: { unavailableAlertedAt: new Date() } });
      if (won.modifiedCount !== 1) continue;
      await this.notifier.admins('provider_unavailable', j);
      n++;
    }
    return n;
  }

  /** «تکمیل‌شده» after `hours` (0 = admins complete by hand). */
  async autoComplete(now: Date, hours: number): Promise<number> {
    if (hours <= 0) return 0;
    const due = await this.jobModel.find({ status: 'delivered', deliveredAt: { $lte: new Date(now.getTime() - hours * 3_600_000) } }, { _id: 1 }).limit(BATCH).lean();
    let n = 0;
    for (const j of due) {
      try {
        await this.jobs.move(String(j._id), 'completed', { actor: 'system', byRole: 'system' });
        n++;
      } catch {
        // completed by an admin meanwhile
      }
    }
    return n;
  }
}
```

```ts
const at = (m: number) => new Date(T0.getTime() + m * 60_000);
const range = { from: new Date('2026-10-01T00:00:00Z'), to: new Date('2026-11-01T00:00:00Z') };
const NOW = new Date('2026-10-16T08:00:00Z');
const providers = [{ id: String(P), name: 'P' }, { id: String(Q), name: 'Q' }, { id: String(R), name: 'R' }];

          { at: at(5), action: 'rejected', status: 'rejected', providerId: Q, byRole: 'provider', reasonCode: 'paper_unavailable' },
          { at: at(6), action: 'assigned', status: 'assigned', providerId: P, byRole: 'admin' },
          { at: at(36), action: 'accepted', status: 'accepted', providerId: P, byRole: 'provider' },
          { at: at(36 + 150), action: 'status', status: 'ready', providerId: P, byRole: 'provider' },
          { at: at(36 + 210), action: 'status', status: 'delivered', providerId: P, byRole: 'courier' },
          { at: at(24 * 60 + 300), action: 'completed', status: 'completed', providerId: P, byRole: 'system' },
        ],
      },
      { // R never answers; P takes it and is late; then the order is cancelled during production
        status: 'cancelled', providerId: P, deadline: at(60), cancel: { at: at(400), stage: 'in_production', providerId: P, byRole: 'customer' },

      { // P currently producing, past its deadline

      },
    ];
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `apps/api/src/modules/dispatch/performance.ts`**

```ts
/** §67 provider performance — pure, from the job history. Collected for admins only; never used to penalize. */
import { REJECT_REASON_LABELS, type JobStatus, type RejectReason } from '../../common/constants.js';
import { HELD, isLate, type JobHistoryEntry } from './job-model.js';

export interface ProviderPerformance {
  providerId: string; name: string; assigned: number; accepted: number; rejected: number; timeouts: number; failed: number;
  rejectReasons: { code: RejectReason; label: string; count: number }[]; completed: number; cancelled: number; late: number;
  avgResponseMinutes: number | null; avgProductionMinutes: number | null; avgDeliveryMinutes: number | null;
  activeJobs: number; todaySheets: number;
}
export interface PerfJob {
  status: JobStatus; providerId?: unknown; deadline: Date; readyAt?: Date; sheets?: number;
  assignment?: { assignedAt: Date }; history: JobHistoryEntry[]; cancel?: { at: Date; providerId?: unknown; stage?: string; byRole?: string };
}

const ACTIVE: JobStatus[] = [...HELD, 'ready', 'ready_for_pickup', 'out_for_delivery'];
type Acc = Omit<ProviderPerformance, 'avgResponseMinutes' | 'avgProductionMinutes' | 'avgDeliveryMinutes' | 'rejectReasons'> & {
  response: number[]; production: number[]; delivery: number[]; reasons: Map<RejectReason, number>;
};
const avg = (xs: number[]) => (xs.length ? Math.round(xs.reduce((s, x) => s + x, 0) / xs.length) : null);
const minutes = (a: Date, b: Date) => (b.getTime() - a.getTime()) / 60_000;

export function performanceRows(jobs: PerfJob[], providers: { id: string; name: string }[], range: { from: Date; to: Date }, now: Date, todayStart?: Date): ProviderPerformance[] {
  const acc = new Map<string, Acc>(providers.map((p) => [p.id, {
    providerId: p.id, name: p.name, assigned: 0, accepted: 0, rejected: 0, timeouts: 0, failed: 0, completed: 0, cancelled: 0, late: 0,
    activeJobs: 0, todaySheets: 0, response: [], production: [], delivery: [], reasons: new Map(),
  }]));
  const within = (d: Date) => d.getTime() >= range.from.getTime() && d.getTime() < range.to.getTime();
  for (const j of jobs) {
    let assignedAt: Date | undefined;
    let acceptedAt: Date | undefined;
    let readyAt: Date | undefined;
    for (const e of j.history ?? []) {
      const t = new Date(e.at);
      const a = e.providerId ? acc.get(String(e.providerId)) : undefined;
      const counted = !!a && within(t);
      switch (e.action) {
        case 'assigned':
        case 'reassigned':
          assignedAt = t; acceptedAt = undefined; readyAt = undefined;
          if (counted) a!.assigned++;
          break;
        case 'accepted':
          if (counted) { a!.accepted++; if (assignedAt) a!.response.push(minutes(assignedAt, t)); }
          acceptedAt = t;
          break;
        case 'rejected':
          if (counted) { a!.rejected++; if (e.reasonCode) a!.reasons.set(e.reasonCode, (a!.reasons.get(e.reasonCode) ?? 0) + 1); }
          break;
        case 'timeout': if (counted) a!.timeouts++; break;
        case 'failed': if (counted) a!.failed++; break;
        case 'completed': if (counted) a!.completed++; break;
        case 'cancelled': if (counted) a!.cancelled++; break;
        case 'status':
          if (e.status === 'ready') {
            readyAt = t;
            if (counted && acceptedAt) a!.production.push(minutes(acceptedAt, t));
          } else if (e.status === 'delivered' && readyAt) {
            const d = acc.get(String(e.deliveryProviderId ?? e.providerId));
            if (d && within(t)) d.delivery.push(minutes(readyAt, t));
          }
          break;
        default:
          break;
      }
    }
    const holder = j.providerId ? acc.get(String(j.providerId)) : undefined;
    if (!holder) continue;
    if (isLate(j, now) && (!j.readyAt || within(new Date(j.readyAt)))) holder.late++;
    if (ACTIVE.includes(j.status)) holder.activeJobs++;
    if (todayStart && j.assignment?.assignedAt && new Date(j.assignment.assignedAt) >= todayStart && j.status !== 'cancelled') holder.todaySheets += j.sheets ?? 0;
  }
  return [...acc.values()].map(({ response, production, delivery, reasons, ...rest }) => ({
    ...rest,
    avgResponseMinutes: avg(response), avgProductionMinutes: avg(production), avgDeliveryMinutes: avg(delivery),
    rejectReasons: [...reasons].map(([code, count]) => ({ code, label: REJECT_REASON_LABELS[code], count })),
  }));
}
```

(Note on the test: job 2 is `cancelled`, so `isLate` is false for it; job 3 is open past its deadline → late 1. P's `accepted` = 3 → responses 30, 10, 10.)

```ts
export class PerformanceQueryDto {
  @IsOptional() @Matches(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/, ymd) from?: string;
  @IsOptional() @Matches(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/, ymd) to?: string;
}
```

`DispatchService`:

```ts
  /** §67 per-provider counters (default: the last 30 Tehran days). */
  async performance(q: PerformanceQueryDto): Promise<ProviderPerformance[]> {
    const now = new Date();
    const todayStart = tehranDayStart(tehranYmd(now));
    const to = q.to ? addDays(tehranDayStart(q.to), 1) : addDays(todayStart, 1);
    const from = q.from ? tehranDayStart(q.from) : addDays(to, -30);
    const [providers, jobs] = await Promise.all([
      this.centers.find({}, { name: 1 }).sort({ name: 1 }).lean(),
      this.jobModel.find({ $or: [{ updatedAt: { $gte: from } }, { status: { $in: LIVE } }] }).lean(),
    ]);
    return performanceRows(jobs as never, providers.map((p) => ({ id: String(p._id), name: p.name })), { from, to }, now, todayStart);
  }
```

- Produces (labels): `JOB_STATUS_LABEL`, `JOB_STATUS_TONE`, `DELIVERY_METHOD_LABEL`, `PRIORITY_LABEL`, `PRIORITY_TONE`, `REJECT_REASON_LABEL`, `REJECT_REASONS`, `DISPATCH_TABS`, `PROVIDER_TABS`, `DELIVERY_STATUS_LABEL`, `CHECK_LABEL`, `NOTIFICATION_EVENT_LABEL`, `NOTIFICATION_AUDIENCE_LABEL`, `audienceOfEvent(event)`, `JOB_OPTION_LABEL`.
- Produces (rule): `lib/delivery.ts` — `needsHomePickup(draft: { children: unknown[]; services: { kind: string }[] }): boolean` (same rule as the server, contract «Delivery on the order»; not a price).
- Produces (hooks): `lib/notifications.ts` — `inboxKeys(audience)`, `useInboxUnread(audience)`, `useInbox(audience)`, `useMarkInboxRead(audience)`; `lib/push.ts` (moved, unchanged API); `dispatch-api.ts` — `dk`, `useDispatchJobs(params)`, `useDispatchJob(id)`, `useEligibility(id, deliveryMethod?)`, `useDispatchPerformance(range)`, `useDispatchMutations(jobId)` (`assign`, `reassign`, `unassign`, `delivery`, `update`, `respond`, `status`); `jobs-api.ts` — `pjk`, `useProviderJobs(tab, page)`, `useProviderJob(id)`, `useProviderJobMutations(id)` (`accept`, `reject`, `status`, `qc`).

```ts
// ── v4.2 dispatch & fulfillment (contract «v4.2») ──────────────────────────
export type JobStatus = 'unassigned' | 'assigned' | 'accepted' | 'in_production' | 'ready' | 'out_for_delivery' | 'ready_for_pickup' | 'delivered' | 'completed' | 'rejected' | 'cancelled' | 'failed'
export type DeliveryMethod = 'courier' | 'provider' | 'customer_pickup'
export type PickupAt = 'point' | 'provider'
export type JobPriority = 'normal' | 'high' | 'urgent'
export type RejectReason = 'equipment_unavailable' | 'paper_unavailable' | 'capacity_full' | 'machine_problem' | 'outside_hours' | 'other'
export type CancelStage = 'before_production' | 'in_production' | 'after_production'
export type DispatchTab = 'new' | 'unassigned' | 'assigned' | 'in_progress' | 'ready' | 'delivery' | 'completed' | 'cancelled'
export type ProviderTab = 'new' | 'pending' | 'accepted' | 'in_production' | 'ready' | 'delivery' | 'completed' | 'cancelled'
export type DeliveryStatus = 'pending' | 'ready' | 'on_the_way' | 'done'
export interface PickupPoint { id: string; name: string; address: string; phone: string; hours: string; on: boolean }
export interface PlaceSnapshot { id: string; name: string; address: string; phone: string; hours: string; kind: 'point' | 'provider' }
export interface OrderDelivery { method: DeliveryMethod; homePickup: boolean; pickupAt?: PickupAt; pickupPoint?: PlaceSnapshot; providerShop?: PlaceSnapshot; note: string }
export interface CustomerFulfillment { status: JobStatus; label: string; place?: PlaceSnapshot }
export interface DispatchSettings { responseTimeoutMinutes: number; leadHours: number; urgentLeadHours: number; deadlineReminderMinutes: number; autoCompleteHours: number }
export type DispatchEvent = 'job_assigned' | 'job_delivery_assigned' | 'job_withdrawn' | 'job_cancelled' | 'job_changed' | 'job_deadline'
  | 'job_rejected' | 'job_timeout' | 'job_failed' | 'job_late' | 'provider_unavailable' | 'assigned' | 'ready_for_pickup'
export type NotificationEvent = OrderStatus | DispatchEvent
export type NotificationAudience = 'customer' | 'provider' | 'admin'
export interface ProviderPricingSnapshot {
  providerId: string; snapshotAt: string; currency: 'toman'
  lines: { serviceIndex: number; comboKey: string; label: string; versionId: string; version: number; unitPrice: number; quantity: number; subtotal: number }[]
  missing: { serviceIndex?: number; childIndex?: number; comboKey: string | null; label: string }[]
  providerSubtotal: number | null; delivery: { providerId: string; fee: number } | null; payoutBasis: number | null; confirmedMissing: boolean
}
export interface DispatchJobRow {
  id: string; orderId: string; orderCode: string; customerName: string; zone?: string; services: ProviderService[]; summary: string
  status: JobStatus; orderStatus: OrderStatus; priority: JobPriority; deadline: string; late: boolean; attempts: number
  provider: { id: string; name: string } | null; deliveryProvider: { id: string; name: string } | null
  deliveryMethod: DeliveryMethod; pickupAt?: PickupAt; respondBy?: string
  lastException?: { kind: 'rejected' | 'timeout' | 'failed' | 'unassigned'; reason: string; providerName: string; at: string }
  createdAt: string
}
export type JobAction = 'created' | 'migrated' | 'assigned' | 'reassigned' | 'accepted' | 'rejected' | 'timeout' | 'unassigned' | 'status' | 'failed' | 'delivery_changed' | 'updated' | 'cancelled' | 'completed'
export interface JobHistoryView {
  at: string; action: JobAction; status: JobStatus; label: string; providerName?: string; deliveryProviderName?: string; byName?: string
  byRole: 'admin' | 'provider' | 'courier' | 'customer' | 'system'; reason?: string; reasonCode?: RejectReason; override?: boolean; stage?: CancelStage
}
export interface AssignmentView {
  providerId: string; providerName: string; assignedByName?: string; assignedAt: string; acceptedAt?: string; rejectedAt?: string; completedAt?: string; endedAt?: string
  reason?: string; reasonCode?: RejectReason; override?: boolean; outcome: 'pending' | 'active' | 'rejected' | 'timeout' | 'withdrawn' | 'failed' | 'cancelled' | 'completed'
}
export interface DispatchJobDetail extends DispatchJobRow {
  homePickup: boolean; sheets: number; units: Partial<Record<ProviderService, number>>; requirements: string[]
  order: { status: OrderStatus; urgent: boolean; payMethod: PayMethod; paid: boolean; total: number; delivery: OrderDelivery; pickup: { address: string; phone: string; date?: string; slot?: string }; qcDone: boolean; courierId?: string }
  providerPricing: ProviderPricingSnapshot | null; history: JobHistoryView[]; assignments: AssignmentView[]; cancel?: { at: string; stage: CancelStage; providerName?: string }
}
export interface DispatchList { items: DispatchJobRow[]; total: number; page: number; limit: number; counts: Record<DispatchTab, number> }
export interface ProviderCheck { key: 'capability' | 'status' | 'available' | 'hours' | 'zone' | 'capacity' | 'delivery'; ok: boolean; hard: boolean; message: string }
export interface EligibilityRow {
  providerId: string; name: string; businessName: string; status: ProviderStatus; current: boolean; eligible: boolean; assignable: boolean; checks: ProviderCheck[]
  price: { subtotal: number | null; missing: string[]; lines: { label: string; unitPrice: number; quantity: number; subtotal: number }[] }
  capacityLeft: { sheets: number | null; jobs: number | null; perService: { service: ProviderService; left: number }[] }
  hourlySheets?: number; openNow: boolean | null; activeJobs: number; hasLogin: boolean; canDeliver: boolean; deliveryFee: number; allowCustomerPickup: boolean
}
export interface Eligibility { jobId: string; requirements: string[]; eligible: EligibilityRow[]; others: EligibilityRow[]; deliveryProviders: { id: string; name: string; deliveryFee: number; zoneOk: boolean }[] }
export interface ProviderPerformance {
  providerId: string; name: string; assigned: number; accepted: number; rejected: number; timeouts: number; failed: number
  rejectReasons: { code: RejectReason; label: string; count: number }[]; completed: number; cancelled: number; late: number
  avgResponseMinutes: number | null; avgProductionMinutes: number | null; avgDeliveryMinutes: number | null; activeJobs: number; todaySheets: number
}
export interface ProviderJobView {
  id: string; orderCode: string; status: JobStatus; statusLabel: string; roles: ('production' | 'delivery')[]; priority: JobPriority; deadline: string; late: boolean
  assignedAt?: string; respondBy?: string; services: ProviderService[]; summary: string; delivery: { method: DeliveryMethod; label: string; pickupAt?: PickupAt }

}
export interface ProviderWithdrawnView { id: string; orderCode: string; withdrawn: true; at: string; label: string }
export interface ProviderJobLine { kind: 'child' | 'service'; label: string; detail: string; quantity: number; options: Record<string, string | number | boolean>; childName?: string; note?: string }
export interface ProviderJobDetail extends ProviderJobView {

  contact: { name: string; phone: string; address: string; note: string } | null; collectFrom: { name: string; address: string; phone: string } | null
  homePickup: boolean; itemsReceived: boolean; history: { at: string; status: JobStatus; label: string; note?: string }[]
}
export interface ProviderJobList { items: (ProviderJobView | ProviderWithdrawnView)[]; total: number; page: number; limit: number; counts: Record<ProviderTab, number> }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Shared push + inbox** — move `apps/web/src/features/customer/lib/push.ts` to `apps/web/src/lib/push.ts` unchanged and leave `features/customer/lib/push.ts` as `export * from '@/lib/push'`. Create `apps/web/src/lib/notifications.ts`:

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { api } from '@/lib/api'
import { queryClient } from '@/lib/query'
import type { NotificationAudience, Paged, UserNotification } from '@/lib/types'
import { useAuth } from '@/stores/auth'

type InboxPage = Paged<UserNotification> & { unread: number }

/** One inbox per app/role (contract v4.2: `/notifications/mine` returns only the caller's audience). */
export const inboxKeys = (audience: NotificationAudience) => ({
  all: ['inbox', audience] as const,
  unread: ['inbox', audience, 'unread'] as const,
  list: ['inbox', audience, 'list'] as const,
})

const useEnabled = (audience: NotificationAudience) => {
  const token = useAuth((s) => s.token)
  const role = useAuth((s) => s.user?.role)
  return !!token && role === audience
}

export function useInboxUnread(audience: NotificationAudience): number {
  const enabled = useEnabled(audience)
  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: inboxKeys(audience).unread,
    queryFn: () => api.get<InboxPage>('/notifications/mine', { page: 1, limit: 1 }),
    enabled, refetchOnWindowFocus: true, refetchInterval: 60_000, staleTime: 20_000,
  })
  return enabled ? (query.data?.unread ?? 0) : 0
}

export function useInbox(audience: NotificationAudience) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: inboxKeys(audience).list,
    queryFn: () => api.get<InboxPage>('/notifications/mine', { page: 1, limit: 50 }),
    enabled: useEnabled(audience), refetchOnWindowFocus: true,
  })
}

export function useMarkInboxRead(audience: NotificationAudience) {
  const keys = inboxKeys(audience)
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (ids?: string[]) => api.post<{ ok: true; unread: number }>('/notifications/mine/read', ids ? { ids } : {}),
    onSuccess: ({ unread }, ids) => {
      const patch = (page: InboxPage | undefined) => page && { ...page, unread, items: page.items.map((n) => (!ids || ids.includes(n.id) ? { ...n, read: true } : n)) }
      queryClient.setQueryData<InboxPage>(keys.list, patch)
      queryClient.setQueryData<InboxPage>(keys.unread, patch)
    },
  })
}
```

Replace the body of `features/customer/hooks/notifications.ts` with thin wrappers (same exports as before, so the customer screens are untouched):

```ts
import { inboxKeys, useInbox, useInboxUnread, useMarkInboxRead } from '@/lib/notifications'

export const notificationKeys = inboxKeys('customer')
export const useUnreadCount = () => useInboxUnread('customer')
export const useNotifications = () => useInbox('customer')
export const useMarkRead = () => useMarkInboxRead('customer')
```

- [ ] **Step 4b: Home-pickup rule** — `apps/web/src/lib/delivery.ts`:

```ts
/** Contract v4.2 «Delivery on the order»: school books, cartridges and devices are collected at home (mirrors the API's `needsHomePickup`). */
export const HOME_PICKUP_KINDS = ['cart', 'repair']
export function needsHomePickup(d: { children: unknown[]; services: { kind: string }[] }): boolean {
  return d.children.length > 0 || d.services.some((s) => HOME_PICKUP_KINDS.includes(s.kind))
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Service worker** — in `apps/web/public/sw.js` `notificationclick`, focus/navigate a window of the **target's** app instead of always `/app`:

```js
self.addEventListener('notificationclick', (event) => {
  event.notification.close()
  const target = new URL((event.notification.data && event.notification.data.url) || '/app/notifications', self.location.origin)
  const app = '/' + (target.pathname.split('/')[1] || '')   // '/app' | '/provider' | '/admin'
  event.waitUntil(
    self.clients.matchAll({ type: 'window', includeUncontrolled: true }).then((windows) => {
      const exact = windows.find((w) => w.url === target.href)
      if (exact) return exact.focus()
      const appWindow = windows.find((w) => new URL(w.url).pathname.startsWith(app))
      if (appWindow && 'navigate' in appWindow) return appWindow.navigate(target.href).then((w) => (w || appWindow).focus())
      return self.clients.openWindow(target.href)
    }),
  )
})
```

(`VERSION` is stamped at build time, so clients pick up the new worker with the next build.)

- [ ] **Step 6: Admin hooks** — `apps/web/src/features/admin/dispatch-api.ts`

```ts
import { keepPreviousData, useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { api } from '@/lib/api'
import type {
  DeliveryMethod, DeliveryStatus, DispatchJobDetail, DispatchList, DispatchTab, Eligibility, JobPriority, JobStatus, PickupAt, ProviderPerformance, ProviderService, RejectReason,
} from '@/lib/types'
import { ak } from './api'

export interface DispatchParams {
  tab: DispatchTab; page: number; limit: number; providerId?: string; service?: ProviderService; status?: JobStatus; priority?: JobPriority
  deliveryMethod?: DeliveryMethod; deliveryStatus?: DeliveryStatus; from?: string; to?: string; q?: string
}
export interface AssignBody { providerId: string; deliveryMethod?: DeliveryMethod; deliveryProviderId?: string; pickupAt?: PickupAt; override?: boolean; reason?: string; confirmMissingPrice?: boolean }

export const dk = {
  all: ['admin', 'dispatch'] as const,
  list: (p: DispatchParams) => ['admin', 'dispatch', 'list', p] as const,
  job: (id: string) => ['admin', 'dispatch', 'job', id] as const,
  eligibility: (id: string, m?: DeliveryMethod) => ['admin', 'dispatch', 'eligibility', id, m ?? ''] as const,
  performance: (from?: string, to?: string) => ['admin', 'dispatch', 'performance', from ?? '', to ?? ''] as const,
}

export const useDispatchJobs = (p: DispatchParams, enabled = true) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: dk.list(p), enabled, placeholderData: keepPreviousData, refetchInterval: 30_000, queryFn: () => api.get<DispatchList>('/admin/dispatch/jobs', { ...p }) })
export const useDispatchJob = (id: string | undefined) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: dk.job(id ?? ''), enabled: !!id, queryFn: () => api.get<DispatchJobDetail>(`/admin/dispatch/jobs/${id}`) })
export const useEligibility = (id: string | undefined, deliveryMethod?: DeliveryMethod, enabled = true) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: dk.eligibility(id ?? '', deliveryMethod), enabled: !!id && enabled, queryFn: () => api.get<Eligibility>(`/admin/dispatch/jobs/${id}/eligibility`, { deliveryMethod }) })
export const useDispatchPerformance = (from?: string, to?: string, enabled = true) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: dk.performance(from, to), enabled, queryFn: () => api.get<ProviderPerformance[]>('/admin/dispatch/performance', { from, to }) })

/** Every write refreshes the dispatch center and the order views it mirrors into. */
export function useDispatchMutations(id: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const refresh = (job: DispatchJobDetail) => {
    qc.setQueryData(dk.job(id), job)
    return Promise.all([qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: dk.all }), qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ak.orders }), qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ak.order(job.orderId) }), qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ak.production })])
  }
  const quiet = { quietCodes: ['PROVIDER_NOT_ELIGIBLE', 'PROVIDER_PRICE_MISSING'] }
  const post = (path: string) => (body: object) => api.post<DispatchJobDetail>(`/admin/dispatch/jobs/${id}/${path}`, body)
  return {
    assign: useMutation({ mutationFn: post('assign') as (b: AssignBody) => Promise<DispatchJobDetail>, onSuccess: refresh, meta: quiet }),
    reassign: useMutation({ mutationFn: post('reassign') as (b: AssignBody & { reason: string }) => Promise<DispatchJobDetail>, onSuccess: refresh, meta: quiet }),
    unassign: useMutation({ mutationFn: post('unassign') as (b: { reason: string }) => Promise<DispatchJobDetail>, onSuccess: refresh }),
    respond: useMutation({ mutationFn: post('respond') as (b: { accept: boolean; reasonCode?: RejectReason; reason: string }) => Promise<DispatchJobDetail>, onSuccess: refresh }),
    status: useMutation({ mutationFn: post('status') as (b: { status: JobStatus; reason: string; reasonCode?: RejectReason }) => Promise<DispatchJobDetail>, onSuccess: refresh }),
    delivery: useMutation({ mutationFn: (b: { deliveryMethod?: DeliveryMethod; deliveryProviderId?: string; pickupAt?: PickupAt; reason?: string }) => api.patch<DispatchJobDetail>(`/admin/dispatch/jobs/${id}/delivery`, b), onSuccess: refresh }),
    update: useMutation({ mutationFn: (b: { priority?: JobPriority; deadline?: string }) => api.patch<DispatchJobDetail>(`/admin/dispatch/jobs/${id}`, b), onSuccess: refresh }),
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Provider hooks** — `apps/web/src/features/provider/jobs-api.ts`

```ts
import { keepPreviousData, useMutation, useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { api } from '@/lib/api'
import { queryClient } from '@/lib/query'
import type { ProviderJobDetail, ProviderJobList, ProviderTab, RejectReason } from '@/lib/types'
import { providerKeys } from './api'

export const pjk = {
  all: [...providerKeys.all, 'jobs'] as const,
  list: (tab: ProviderTab, page: number) => [...providerKeys.all, 'jobs', 'list', tab, page] as const,
  job: (id: string) => [...providerKeys.all, 'jobs', 'job', id] as const,
}

export const useProviderJobs = (tab: ProviderTab, page = 1) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: pjk.list(tab, page), placeholderData: keepPreviousData, refetchInterval: 30_000, refetchOnWindowFocus: true, queryFn: () => api.get<ProviderJobList>('/provider/jobs', { tab, page, limit: 20 }) })
export const useProviderJob = (id: string) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: pjk.job(id), queryFn: () => api.get<ProviderJobDetail>(`/provider/jobs/${id}`), refetchOnWindowFocus: true })

export function useProviderJobMutations(id: string) {
  const done = (job?: ProviderJobDetail | { ok: true }) => {
    if (job && 'id' in job) queryClient.setQueryData(pjk.job(id), job)
    return queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: pjk.all })
  }
  // a refused action usually means the job changed meanwhile (taken away, cancelled): show the fresh state
  const onError = () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: pjk.all })
  return {
    accept: useMutation({ mutationFn: () => api.post<ProviderJobDetail>(`/provider/jobs/${id}/accept`), onSuccess: done, onError }),
    reject: useMutation({ mutationFn: (b: { reasonCode: RejectReason; note?: string }) => api.post<{ ok: true }>(`/provider/jobs/${id}/reject`, b), onSuccess: done, onError }),
    status: useMutation({ mutationFn: (b: { status: string; reasonCode?: RejectReason; note?: string }) => api.post<ProviderJobDetail | { ok: true }>(`/provider/jobs/${id}/status`, b), onSuccess: done, onError }),
    qc: useMutation({ mutationFn: (b: { index: number; done: boolean }) => api.patch<ProviderJobDetail>(`/provider/jobs/${id}/qc`, b), onSuccess: done, onError }),
  }
}
```

```ts
import { jalali, jalaliDayTime } from '@/lib/format'

/** «شنبه ۱۴:۳۰ · ۲۵ مهر» */
export const when = (iso: string) => `${jalaliDayTime(iso)} · ${jalali(iso, { day: 'numeric', month: 'long' })}`
/** ISO → the value of an `<input type="datetime-local">` (browser time zone). */
export function toLocalInput(iso: string): string {
  const d = new Date(iso)
  const p = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0')
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${p(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${p(d.getDate())}T${p(d.getHours())}:${p(d.getMinutes())}`
}
```

```tsx
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { DELIVERY_METHOD_LABEL, DELIVERY_STATUS_LABEL, JOB_STATUS_LABEL, PRIORITY_LABEL } from '@/lib/dispatch-labels'
import { PROVIDER_SERVICE_LABEL, PROVIDER_SERVICES } from '@/lib/provider-labels'
import type { DeliveryMethod, DeliveryStatus, JobPriority, JobStatus, ProviderService } from '@/lib/types'
import { useCenters } from '../../api'
import { AdminSelect } from '../../components/controls'
import { SearchInput } from '../../components/table'

export interface Filters {
  providerId?: string; service?: ProviderService; status?: JobStatus; priority?: JobPriority
  deliveryMethod?: DeliveryMethod; deliveryStatus?: DeliveryStatus; from?: string; to?: string; q?: string
}
const ALL = '__all'
const opts = <K extends string>(labels: Record<K, string>, all: string, skip: K[] = []) =>
  [{ value: ALL, label: all }, ...(Object.keys(labels) as K[]).filter((k) => !skip.includes(k)).map((k) => ({ value: k, label: labels[k] }))]

/** §55 filters: provider, service, status, date, customer, priority, delivery. */
export function DispatchFilters({ value, onChange }: { value: Filters; onChange: (f: Filters) => void }) {
  const centers = useCenters()
  const set = (k: keyof Filters) => (v: string) => onChange({ ...value, [k]: v === ALL || v === '' ? undefined : v })
  return (
    <div className="mb-4 grid gap-2.5 [grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(min(100%,180px),1fr))]">
      <SearchInput value={value.q ?? ''} onChange={(q) => onChange({ ...value, q })} placeholder="کد سفارش، نام یا موبایل مشتری…" />
      <AdminSelect ariaLabel="مجری" value={value.providerId ?? ALL} onValueChange={set('providerId')}
        options={[{ value: ALL, label: 'همه مجریان' }, ...(centers.data ?? []).map((c) => ({ value: c.id, label: c.name }))]} />
      <AdminSelect ariaLabel="سرویس" value={value.service ?? ALL} onValueChange={set('service')}
        options={[{ value: ALL, label: 'همه سرویس‌ها' }, ...PROVIDER_SERVICES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: PROVIDER_SERVICE_LABEL[s] }))]} />
      <AdminSelect ariaLabel="وضعیت" value={value.status ?? ALL} onValueChange={set('status')} options={opts(JOB_STATUS_LABEL, 'وضعیت بخش', ['rejected', 'failed'])} />
      <AdminSelect ariaLabel="اولویت" value={value.priority ?? ALL} onValueChange={set('priority')} options={opts(PRIORITY_LABEL, 'همه اولویت‌ها')} />
      <AdminSelect ariaLabel="روش تحویل" value={value.deliveryMethod ?? ALL} onValueChange={set('deliveryMethod')} options={opts(DELIVERY_METHOD_LABEL, 'همه روش‌های تحویل')} />
      <AdminSelect ariaLabel="وضعیت تحویل" value={value.deliveryStatus ?? ALL} onValueChange={set('deliveryStatus')} options={opts(DELIVERY_STATUS_LABEL, 'همه وضعیت‌های تحویل')} />
      <label className="grid gap-1 text-[12px] font-bold text-muted-2">
        ثبت از تاریخ (میلادی)
        <Input type="date" dir="ltr" value={value.from ?? ''} onChange={(e) => set('from')(e.target.value)} className="h-11" />
      </label>
      <label className="grid gap-1 text-[12px] font-bold text-muted-2">
        تا تاریخ (میلادی)
        <Input type="date" dir="ltr" value={value.to ?? ''} onChange={(e) => set('to')(e.target.value)} className="h-11" />
      </label>
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { TONES } from '@/components/brand'
import { DELIVERY_METHOD_LABEL, JOB_STATUS_LABEL, JOB_STATUS_TONE, PRIORITY_LABEL, PRIORITY_TONE } from '@/lib/dispatch-labels'
import { fa } from '@/lib/format'
import type { DispatchJobRow } from '@/lib/types'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import { Tag } from '../../components/controls'
import { AdminRow, AdminTable } from '../../components/table'
import { when } from './format'

const COLUMNS = '96px minmax(0,1.3fr) minmax(0,1.3fr) 150px 140px 150px 80px'
const EXCEPTION = { rejected: 'رد شد', timeout: 'بی‌پاسخ', failed: 'ناموفق', unassigned: 'لغو تخصیص' } as const

export function JobsTable({ rows, onOpen, footer }: { rows: DispatchJobRow[]; onOpen: (id: string) => void; footer?: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <AdminTable columns={COLUMNS} minWidth={1000} head={['سفارش', 'مشتری و اقلام', 'مجری', 'وضعیت', 'تحویل', 'مهلت', 'اولویت']} footer={footer}>
      {rows.map((r) => {
        const st = TONES[JOB_STATUS_TONE[r.status]]
        const pr = TONES[PRIORITY_TONE[r.priority]]
        return (
          <AdminRow key={r.id} onClick={() => onOpen(r.id)} className="py-3">
            <span className="font-black">{fa(r.orderCode)}</span>
            <span className="min-w-0">
              <span className="block truncate font-bold">{r.customerName}</span>
              <span className="block truncate text-[11.5px] text-muted-2">{r.summary}</span>
            </span>
            <span className="min-w-0 text-[12.5px]">
              {r.provider ? <span className="block truncate font-bold">{r.provider.name}</span> : <span className="text-muted-2">—</span>}
              {r.lastException && (
                <span className="block truncate text-[11.5px] font-bold text-pink-dark">
                  {EXCEPTION[r.lastException.kind]}: {r.lastException.providerName}{r.lastException.reason ? ` — ${r.lastException.reason}` : ''}
                </span>
              )}
              {r.respondBy && <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-muted-2">پاسخ تا {when(r.respondBy)}</span>}
            </span>
            <span><Tag bg={st.soft} fg={st.ink}>{JOB_STATUS_LABEL[r.status]}</Tag></span>
            <span className="text-[12.5px] leading-6">
              {DELIVERY_METHOD_LABEL[r.deliveryMethod]}
              {r.deliveryProvider && r.deliveryProvider.id !== r.provider?.id && <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-muted-2">{r.deliveryProvider.name}</span>}
            </span>
            <span className={cn('text-[12.5px] font-bold', r.late ? 'text-pink-dark' : 'text-muted-1')}>
              {when(r.deadline)}{r.late && <span className="block text-[11.5px]">دیرکرد</span>}
            </span>
            <span><Tag bg={pr.soft} fg={pr.ink}>{PRIORITY_LABEL[r.priority]}</Tag></span>
          </AdminRow>
        )
      })}
    </AdminTable>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { Bell } from 'lucide-react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Popover, PopoverContent, PopoverTrigger } from '@/components/ui/popover'
import { fa } from '@/lib/format'
import { useInbox, useInboxUnread, useMarkInboxRead } from '@/lib/notifications'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import { when } from './format'

export function AlertsButton({ onOpen }: { onOpen: (jobId: string) => void }) {
  const unread = useInboxUnread('admin')
  const inbox = useInbox('admin')
  const markRead = useMarkInboxRead('admin')
  const items = inbox.data?.items ?? []
  return (
    <Popover>
      <PopoverTrigger asChild>
        <Button variant="outline" aria-label="هشدارهای تخصیص">
          <Bell className="size-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />
          هشدارها
          {unread > 0 && <span className="rounded-full bg-pink px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-black text-white">{fa(unread)}</span>}
        </Button>
      </PopoverTrigger>
      <PopoverContent data-role="admin" align="end" className="w-[340px] max-w-[92vw] rounded-[22px] border-line bg-white p-2">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-2 py-1.5">
          <span className="text-[14px] font-black">هشدارهای تخصیص</span>
          {unread > 0 && (
            <button type="button" className="cursor-pointer text-[12px] font-bold text-accent" onClick={() => markRead.mutate(undefined)}>همه خوانده شد</button>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="grid max-h-[360px] gap-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {items.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-4 text-center text-[12.5px] text-muted-2">هشداری نیست</div>
          ) : (
            items.map((n) => (
              <button key={n.id} type="button"
                onClick={() => { if (!n.read) markRead.mutate([n.id]); if (n.jobId) onOpen(n.jobId) }}
                className={cn('cursor-pointer rounded-[14px] p-2.5 text-start hover:bg-accent-soft/60', !n.read && 'bg-pink-soft/50')}>
                <div className="text-[12.5px] leading-6 font-bold">{n.text}</div>
                <div className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-muted-2">سفارش {fa(n.orderCode)} · {when(n.createdAt)}</div>
              </button>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </PopoverContent>
    </Popover>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `DispatchPage.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useSearchParams } from 'react-router'
import { Chip, InfoBanner } from '@/components/brand'
import { DISPATCH_TABS } from '@/lib/dispatch-labels'
import { fa } from '@/lib/format'
import { useCan } from '@/lib/permissions'
import type { DispatchTab } from '@/lib/types'
import { PageHeader, QueryView } from '../../components/kit'
import { Pager } from '../../components/table'
import { useDispatchJobs } from '../../dispatch-api'
import { useDebounced } from '../../lib'
import { AlertsButton } from './AlertsButton'
import { DispatchFilters, type Filters } from './DispatchFilters'
import { JobSheet } from './JobSheet'
import { JobsTable } from './JobsTable'
import { PerformanceTab } from './PerformanceTab'

const LIMIT = 20
type View = DispatchTab | 'performance'

/** §55 «مرکز تخصیص سفارش». Admin picks the provider — the server never auto-selects (§52, §54). */
export function DispatchPage() {
  const canView = useCan('dispatch_view')
  const canPerformance = useCan('provider_order_view')
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams()
  const [view, setView] = useState<View>((params.get('tab') as View | null) ?? 'new')
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<Filters>({ providerId: params.get('providerId') ?? undefined })
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1)
  const q = useDebounced(filters.q?.trim() ?? '')
  const tab: DispatchTab = view === 'performance' ? 'new' : view
  const query = useDispatchJobs({ ...filters, q: q || undefined, tab, page, limit: LIMIT }, canView && view !== 'performance')
  const openId = params.get('job') ?? undefined
  const open = (id?: string) => {
    const next = new URLSearchParams(params)
    if (id) next.set('job', id)
    else next.delete('job')
    setParams(next, { replace: true })
  }

  if (!canView) {
    return (
      <>
        <PageHeader title="مرکز تخصیص سفارش" />
        <InfoBanner>به مرکز تخصیص سفارش دسترسی ندارید. برای دریافت دسترسی با مدیر کل تماس بگیرید.</InfoBanner>
      </>
    )
  }
  const counts = query.data?.counts
  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="مرکز تخصیص سفارش" subtitle="هر سفارش را به مجری توانمند بسپارید و پاسخ، تولید و تحویل آن را دنبال کنید." actions={<AlertsButton onOpen={open} />} />
      <div className="no-scrollbar -mx-1 mb-3 flex gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto px-1 pb-1" role="tablist" aria-label="بخش‌ها">
        {DISPATCH_TABS.map((t) => (
          <Chip key={t.key} size="sm" role="tab" aria-selected={view === t.key} selected={view === t.key} className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap"
            onClick={() => { setView(t.key); setPage(1) }}>
            {t.label}{counts ? ` (${fa(counts[t.key])})` : ''}
          </Chip>
        ))}
        {canPerformance && (
          <Chip size="sm" role="tab" aria-selected={view === 'performance'} selected={view === 'performance'} className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap" onClick={() => setView('performance')}>
            عملکرد مجریان
          </Chip>
        )}
      </div>
      {view === 'performance' ? (
        <PerformanceTab />
      ) : (
        <>
          <DispatchFilters value={filters} onChange={(f) => { setFilters(f); setPage(1) }} />
          <QueryView query={query} rows={6} isEmpty={(d) => d.items.length === 0} empty={{ title: 'سفارشی در این بخش نیست', hint: 'بخش یا فیلترها را تغییر دهید.' }}>
            {(d) => <JobsTable rows={d.items} onOpen={open} footer={<Pager page={d.page} limit={d.limit} total={d.total} onPage={setPage} />} />}
          </QueryView>
        </>
      )}
      <JobSheet jobId={openId} onClose={() => open(undefined)} />
    </>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'
import { toast } from '@/components/ui/sonner'
import { REJECT_REASON_LABEL, REJECT_REASONS } from '@/lib/dispatch-labels'
import type { RejectReason } from '@/lib/types'
import { AdminSelect } from '../../components/controls'
import { Field, FormDialog } from '../../components/FormDialog'

/** Every admin override and on-behalf action records a reason (§71, §75). */
export function ReasonDialog({ open, onOpenChange, title, description, withCode = false, submitLabel = 'ثبت', pending, onSubmit }: {
  open: boolean; onOpenChange: (o: boolean) => void; title: string; description?: string; withCode?: boolean; submitLabel?: string; pending?: boolean
  onSubmit: (reason: string, code?: RejectReason) => void
}) {
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('')
  const [code, setCode] = useState<RejectReason>('other')
  const submit = () => {
    if (!reason.trim()) return toast.error('دلیل را بنویسید')
    onSubmit(reason.trim(), withCode ? code : undefined)
  }
  return (
    <FormDialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange} title={title} description={description} submitLabel={submitLabel} pending={pending} onSubmit={submit}>
      {withCode && (
        <Field label="علت">
          <AdminSelect ariaLabel="علت" value={code} onValueChange={(v) => setCode(v as RejectReason)} options={REJECT_REASONS.map((r) => ({ value: r, label: REJECT_REASON_LABEL[r] }))} />
        </Field>
      )}
      <Field label="دلیل (در تاریخچه ثبت می‌شود)">
        <Textarea rows={3} value={reason} onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)} maxLength={300} />
      </Field>
    </FormDialog>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `AssignPanel.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { DsSwitch, InfoBanner, TONES } from '@/components/brand'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { toast } from '@/components/ui/sonner'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'
import { ApiError } from '@/lib/api'
import { CHECK_LABEL, DELIVERY_METHOD_LABEL } from '@/lib/dispatch-labels'
import { fa, money } from '@/lib/format'
import { useCan } from '@/lib/permissions'
import { NO_PROVIDER_PRICE, PROVIDER_STATUS } from '@/lib/provider-labels'
import type { DeliveryMethod, DispatchJobDetail, EligibilityRow, PickupAt } from '@/lib/types'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import { AdminSelect, Tag } from '../../components/controls'
import { Field, FormDialog } from '../../components/FormDialog'
import { QueryView } from '../../components/kit'
import { useDispatchMutations, useEligibility } from '../../dispatch-api'

/** §52: each provider with capability, availability, its own price (or «قیمت مجری تنظیم نشده», §53) and capacity. Never auto-picked. */
export function AssignPanel({ job, mode }: { job: DispatchJobDetail; mode: 'assign' | 'reassign' }) {
  const allowed = useCan(mode === 'assign' ? 'dispatch_assign' : 'dispatch_reassign')
  const pickup = job.order.delivery.method === 'customer_pickup'
  const [method, setMethod] = useState<DeliveryMethod>(pickup ? 'customer_pickup' : job.deliveryMethod === 'provider' ? 'provider' : 'courier')
  const query = useEligibility(job.id, method)
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<EligibilityRow | null>(null)
  return (
    <section className="rounded-[22px] border border-line bg-white p-4">
      <div className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <h3 className="m-0 text-[15px]">{mode === 'assign' ? 'سپردن به مجری' : 'تغییر مجری'}</h3>
        {!pickup && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12.5px] font-bold">
            <span>ارسال توسط مجری</span>
            <DsSwitch checked={method === 'provider'} onCheckedChange={(on) => setMethod(on ? 'provider' : 'courier')} label="ارسال توسط مجری" />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <QueryView query={query} rows={3}>
        {(e) => (
          <>
            <div className="mb-2 text-[12.5px] leading-6 text-muted-1">نیاز سفارش: {e.requirements.join('، ') || '—'}</div>
            {e.eligible.length === 0 && <InfoBanner tone="amber" className="mb-2">هیچ مجری همه شرایط را ندارد؛ مجری‌های بدون مانع اصلی را می‌توانید با ذکر دلیل انتخاب کنید.</InfoBanner>}
            <div className="grid gap-2">
              {[...e.eligible, ...e.others].map((r) => (
                <ProviderCard key={r.providerId} row={r} disabled={!allowed || !r.assignable || r.current} onPick={() => setPicked(r)} />
              ))}
            </div>
            {picked && (
              <AssignDialog key={picked.providerId} job={job} row={picked} mode={mode} method={method} deliveryProviders={e.deliveryProviders} onClose={() => setPicked(null)} />
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </QueryView>
    </section>
  )
}

function ProviderCard({ row, disabled, onPick }: { row: EligibilityRow; disabled: boolean; onPick: () => void }) {
  const st = TONES[PROVIDER_STATUS[row.status].tone]
  const failed = row.checks.filter((c) => !c.ok)
  return (
    <div className={cn('rounded-[18px] border p-3', row.eligible ? 'border-green/40 bg-green-soft/30' : row.assignable ? 'border-amber/40 bg-amber-soft/30' : 'border-line bg-line-soft/40 opacity-80')}>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <span className="font-black">{row.name}</span>
        <Tag bg={st.soft} fg={st.ink}>{PROVIDER_STATUS[row.status].label}</Tag>
        {row.current && <Tag bg={TONES.violet.soft} fg={TONES.violet.ink}>مجری فعلی</Tag>}
        {!row.hasLogin && <Tag bg={TONES.amber.soft} fg={TONES.amber.ink}>بدون حساب ورود</Tag>}
        <span className="flex-1" />
        <span className={cn('text-[13px] font-black', row.price.subtotal === null && 'text-pink-dark')}>{row.price.subtotal === null ? NO_PROVIDER_PRICE : money(row.price.subtotal)}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-[11.5px] text-muted-1">
        <span>سفارش باز: {fa(row.activeJobs)}</span>
        {row.capacityLeft.sheets !== null && <span>ظرفیت امروز: {fa(row.capacityLeft.sheets)} برگ</span>}
        {row.capacityLeft.jobs !== null && <span>سفارش باقی‌مانده امروز: {fa(row.capacityLeft.jobs)}</span>}
        {row.hourlySheets !== undefined && <span>ظرفیت ساعتی: {fa(row.hourlySheets)} برگ</span>}
        <span>{row.openNow === null ? 'ساعت کاری ثبت نشده' : row.openNow ? 'اکنون باز' : 'اکنون بسته'}</span>
        {row.canDeliver && <span>ارسال: {money(row.deliveryFee)}</span>}
      </div>
      {failed.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="m-0 mt-1.5 list-none p-0 text-[11.5px] leading-6">
          {failed.map((c) => <li key={c.key} className={c.hard ? 'font-bold text-pink-dark' : 'text-amber-ink'}>{CHECK_LABEL[c.key]}: {c.message}</li>)}
        </ul>
      )}
      {row.price.missing.length > 0 && row.price.subtotal === null && row.assignable && (
        <div className="mt-1 text-[11.5px] text-muted-2">بدون قیمت مجری: {row.price.missing.join('، ')}</div>
      )}
      <div className="mt-2 flex justify-end">
        <Button size="sm" disabled={disabled} onClick={onPick}>{row.assignable ? 'سپردن به این مجری' : 'امکان انجام ندارد'}</Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function AssignDialog({ job, row, mode, method, deliveryProviders, onClose }: {
  job: DispatchJobDetail; row: EligibilityRow; mode: 'assign' | 'reassign'; method: DeliveryMethod
  deliveryProviders: { id: string; name: string; deliveryFee: number; zoneOk: boolean }[]; onClose: () => void
}) {
  const m = useDispatchMutations(job.id)
  const [deliveryProviderId, setDeliveryProviderId] = useState(row.canDeliver ? row.providerId : (deliveryProviders[0]?.id ?? ''))
  const [pickupAt, setPickupAt] = useState<PickupAt>('point')
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('')
  const [confirmMissing, setConfirmMissing] = useState(false)
  const override = !row.eligible
  const missing = row.price.subtotal === null
  const needsReason = override || mode === 'reassign'
  const pending = m.assign.isPending || m.reassign.isPending

  const submit = () => {
    if (needsReason && !reason.trim()) return toast.error(override ? 'دلیل نادیده‌گرفتن شرایط را بنویسید' : 'دلیل تغییر مجری را بنویسید')
    if (missing && !confirmMissing) return toast.error('قیمت مجری تنظیم نشده؛ برای ادامه آن را تأیید کنید')
    const body = {
      providerId: row.providerId, deliveryMethod: method,
      ...(method === 'provider' && deliveryProviderId ? { deliveryProviderId } : {}),
      ...(method === 'customer_pickup' ? { pickupAt } : {}),
      ...(override ? { override: true } : {}), ...(reason.trim() ? { reason: reason.trim() } : {}), ...(missing ? { confirmMissingPrice: true } : {}),
    }
    const onError = (e: unknown) => {
      // the conditions changed after the list was loaded: say so instead of a raw code
      if (e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 409) toast.error(e.message)
    }
    if (mode === 'assign') m.assign.mutate(body, { onSuccess: onClose, onError })
    else m.reassign.mutate({ ...body, reason: reason.trim() }, { onSuccess: onClose, onError })
  }

  return (
    <FormDialog open onOpenChange={(o) => !o && onClose()} title={`${mode === 'assign' ? 'سپردن' : 'تغییر مجری'} — ${row.name}`}
      description={`سفارش ${fa(job.orderCode)} · ${DELIVERY_METHOD_LABEL[method]}`} submitLabel={mode === 'assign' ? 'سپردن' : 'تغییر مجری'} pending={pending} onSubmit={submit}>
      {method === 'provider' && (
        <Field label="مجری ارسال‌کننده">
          <AdminSelect ariaLabel="مجری ارسال‌کننده" value={deliveryProviderId} onValueChange={setDeliveryProviderId}
            options={deliveryProviders.map((d) => ({ value: d.id, label: `${d.name} · ${money(d.deliveryFee)}${d.zoneOk ? '' : ' · خارج از منطقه'}` }))} />
        </Field>
      )}
      {method === 'customer_pickup' && (
        <Field label="محل تحویل حضوری">
          <AdminSelect ariaLabel="محل تحویل حضوری" value={pickupAt} onValueChange={(v) => setPickupAt(v as PickupAt)}
            options={[{ value: 'point', label: job.order.delivery.pickupPoint?.name ?? 'محل انتخابی مشتری' }, ...(row.allowCustomerPickup ? [{ value: 'provider', label: `فروشگاه ${row.name}` }] : [])]} />
        </Field>
      )}
      {override && <InfoBanner tone="amber">این مجری همه شرایط را ندارد: {row.checks.filter((c) => !c.ok).map((c) => c.message).join('، ')}</InfoBanner>}
      {missing && (
        <label className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-[16px] bg-pink-soft/50 p-3 text-[12.5px] leading-6">
          <input type="checkbox" checked={confirmMissing} onChange={(e) => setConfirmMissing(e.target.checked)} className="mt-1" />
          <span><b>{NO_PROVIDER_PRICE}</b> — {row.price.missing.join('، ')}. با این حال سپرده شود (پرداخت مجری بعداً تعیین می‌شود).</span>
        </label>
      )}
      {needsReason && (
        <Field label={override ? 'دلیل نادیده‌گرفتن شرایط' : 'دلیل تغییر مجری'}>
          <Textarea rows={3} value={reason} onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)} maxLength={300} />
        </Field>
      )}
    </FormDialog>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `JobActions.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { notify } from '@/components/ui/sonner'
import { DELIVERY_METHOD_LABEL, JOB_STATUS_LABEL, PRIORITY_LABEL } from '@/lib/dispatch-labels'
import { useCan } from '@/lib/permissions'
import type { DeliveryMethod, DispatchJobDetail, JobPriority, JobStatus, PickupAt } from '@/lib/types'
import { AdminSelect } from '../../components/controls'
import { useDispatchMutations, useEligibility } from '../../dispatch-api'
import { toLocalInput } from './format'
import { ReasonDialog } from './ReasonDialog'

const HELD: JobStatus[] = ['assigned', 'accepted', 'in_production']
const EDITABLE: JobStatus[] = ['unassigned', ...HELD, 'ready']

/** Next statuses an admin may record on behalf of the provider/courier (contract «Job flow»). */
function nextStatuses(job: DispatchJobDetail): JobStatus[] {
  const c = job.deliveryMethod
  switch (job.status) {
    case 'accepted': return ['in_production', 'failed']
    case 'in_production': return ['ready', 'failed']
    case 'ready': return c === 'customer_pickup' ? ['ready_for_pickup'] : c === 'courier' ? ['out_for_delivery', 'delivered'] : ['out_for_delivery']
    case 'out_for_delivery': return ['delivered']
    case 'ready_for_pickup': return ['delivered']
    case 'delivered': return ['completed']
    default: return []
  }
}

export function JobActions({ job }: { job: DispatchJobDetail }) {
  const canAssign = useCan('dispatch_assign')
  const canReassign = useCan('dispatch_reassign')
  const canRespond = useCan('provider_order_accept')
  const canStatus = useCan('provider_order_status')
  const m = useDispatchMutations(job.id)
  const [priority, setPriority] = useState<JobPriority>(job.priority)
  const [deadline, setDeadline] = useState(toLocalInput(job.deadline))
  const pickup = job.order.delivery.method === 'customer_pickup'
  const [method, setMethod] = useState<DeliveryMethod>(job.deliveryMethod)
  const [deliveryProviderId, setDeliveryProviderId] = useState(job.deliveryProvider?.id ?? '')
  const [pickupAt, setPickupAt] = useState<PickupAt>(job.pickupAt ?? 'point')
  const elig = useEligibility(job.id, undefined, canAssign && method === 'provider')
  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState<null | { kind: 'unassign' | 'reject' | 'accept' | JobStatus }>(null)
  const done = (msg: string) => () => { setDialog(null); notify(msg) }

  return (
    <section className="grid gap-3 rounded-[22px] border border-line bg-white p-4">
      <h3 className="m-0 text-[15px]">اقدام‌ها</h3>
      {canAssign && EDITABLE.includes(job.status) && (
        <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_1.4fr_auto] sm:items-end">
          <AdminSelect ariaLabel="اولویت" value={priority} onValueChange={(v) => setPriority(v as JobPriority)} options={(Object.keys(PRIORITY_LABEL) as JobPriority[]).map((p) => ({ value: p, label: `اولویت ${PRIORITY_LABEL[p]}` }))} />
          <Input type="datetime-local" dir="ltr" aria-label="مهلت" value={deadline} onChange={(e) => setDeadline(e.target.value)} className="h-11" />
          <Button variant="outline" disabled={m.update.isPending} onClick={() => m.update.mutate({ priority, deadline: new Date(deadline).toISOString() }, { onSuccess: done('اولویت و مهلت ذخیره شد') })}>ذخیره</Button>
        </div>
      )}
      {canAssign && EDITABLE.includes(job.status) && (
        <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_1.4fr_auto] sm:items-end">
          {pickup ? (
            <AdminSelect ariaLabel="محل تحویل حضوری" value={pickupAt} onValueChange={(v) => setPickupAt(v as PickupAt)}
              options={[{ value: 'point', label: job.order.delivery.pickupPoint?.name ?? 'محل انتخابی مشتری' }, { value: 'provider', label: 'فروشگاه مجری' }]} />
          ) : (
            <AdminSelect ariaLabel="روش تحویل" value={method} onValueChange={(v) => setMethod(v as DeliveryMethod)}
              options={(['courier', 'provider'] as DeliveryMethod[]).map((d) => ({ value: d, label: DELIVERY_METHOD_LABEL[d] }))} />
          )}
          {!pickup && method === 'provider' ? (
            <AdminSelect ariaLabel="مجری ارسال‌کننده" value={deliveryProviderId} onValueChange={setDeliveryProviderId} placeholder="مجری ارسال‌کننده"
              options={(elig.data?.deliveryProviders ?? []).map((d) => ({ value: d.id, label: d.name }))} />
          ) : <span />}
          <Button variant="outline" disabled={m.delivery.isPending}
            onClick={() => m.delivery.mutate(pickup ? { pickupAt } : { deliveryMethod: method, ...(method === 'provider' && deliveryProviderId ? { deliveryProviderId } : {}) }, { onSuccess: done('روش تحویل ذخیره شد') })}>
            ذخیره تحویل
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {canRespond && job.status === 'assigned' && (
          <>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: 'accept' })}>پذیرش از طرف مجری</Button>
            <Button variant="destructive" onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: 'reject' })}>رد از طرف مجری</Button>
          </>
        )}
        {canStatus && nextStatuses(job).map((s) => (
          <Button key={s} variant={s === 'failed' ? 'destructive' : 'outline'} onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: s })}>
            {s === 'failed' ? 'ثبت عدم امکان انجام' : `ثبت «${JOB_STATUS_LABEL[s]}»`}
          </Button>
        ))}
        {canReassign && HELD.includes(job.status) && <Button variant="destructive" onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: 'unassign' })}>لغو تخصیص</Button>}
      </div>
      {dialog && (
        <ReasonDialog open onOpenChange={(o) => !o && setDialog(null)} withCode={dialog.kind === 'reject' || dialog.kind === 'failed'}
          title={dialog.kind === 'unassign' ? 'لغو تخصیص و برگشت به صف' : dialog.kind === 'accept' ? 'پذیرش از طرف مجری' : dialog.kind === 'reject' ? 'رد از طرف مجری' : `ثبت «${JOB_STATUS_LABEL[dialog.kind]}» از طرف مجری`}
          pending={m.unassign.isPending || m.respond.isPending || m.status.isPending}
          onSubmit={(reason, code) => {
            if (dialog.kind === 'unassign') m.unassign.mutate({ reason }, { onSuccess: done('سفارش به تخصیص‌نیافته‌ها برگشت') })
            else if (dialog.kind === 'accept') m.respond.mutate({ accept: true, reason }, { onSuccess: done('پذیرش ثبت شد') })
            else if (dialog.kind === 'reject') m.respond.mutate({ accept: false, reason, reasonCode: code }, { onSuccess: done('رد ثبت شد؛ سفارش به صف برگشت') })
            else m.status.mutate({ status: dialog.kind, reason, ...(code ? { reasonCode: code } : {}) }, { onSuccess: done('وضعیت ثبت شد') })
          }} />
      )}
    </section>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `JobHistory.tsx`**

```tsx
import { REJECT_REASON_LABEL } from '@/lib/dispatch-labels'
import type { AssignmentView, DispatchJobDetail } from '@/lib/types'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import { when } from './format'

const OUTCOME: Record<AssignmentView['outcome'], string> = {
  pending: 'در انتظار پاسخ', active: 'در حال انجام', rejected: 'رد کرد', timeout: 'بدون پاسخ', withdrawn: 'گرفته شد', failed: 'اعلام عدم امکان', cancelled: 'سفارش لغو شد', completed: 'تکمیل کرد',
}
const ACTION: Record<string, string> = {
  created: 'ثبت در صف', migrated: 'انتقال از نسخه قبل', assigned: 'سپرده شد', reassigned: 'تغییر مجری', accepted: 'پذیرفت', rejected: 'رد کرد',
  timeout: 'بدون پاسخ در مهلت', unassigned: 'لغو تخصیص', status: 'تغییر وضعیت', failed: 'عدم امکان انجام', delivery_changed: 'تغییر تحویل',
  updated: 'تغییر اولویت/مهلت', cancelled: 'لغو سفارش', completed: 'تکمیل',
}
const BY: Record<string, string> = { admin: 'مدیر', provider: 'مجری', courier: 'پیک', customer: 'مشتری', system: 'سیستم' }

/** §75 assignment history (provider, by whom, when assigned/accepted/rejected/completed, reason) and the full timeline. */
export function JobHistory({ job }: { job: DispatchJobDetail }) {
  return (
    <section className="grid gap-3 rounded-[22px] border border-line bg-white p-4">
      <h3 className="m-0 text-[15px]">تاریخچه تخصیص</h3>
      {job.assignments.length === 0 ? <div className="text-[12.5px] text-muted-2">هنوز به مجری سپرده نشده است.</div> : (
        <div className="grid gap-1.5">
          {job.assignments.map((a, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="rounded-[16px] bg-line-soft/60 p-2.5 text-[12.5px] leading-6">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-2"><b>{a.providerName}</b><span className="font-bold">{OUTCOME[a.outcome]}{a.override ? ' · با نادیده‌گرفتن شرایط' : ''}</span></div>
              <div className="text-muted-1">سپرده: {when(a.assignedAt)}{a.assignedByName ? ` · توسط ${a.assignedByName}` : ''}</div>
              {a.acceptedAt && <div className="text-muted-1">پذیرش: {when(a.acceptedAt)}</div>}
              {a.rejectedAt && <div className="text-muted-1">رد: {when(a.rejectedAt)}</div>}
              {a.completedAt && <div className="text-muted-1">تکمیل: {when(a.completedAt)}</div>}
              {(a.reasonCode || a.reason) && <div className="text-pink-dark">دلیل: {a.reasonCode ? REJECT_REASON_LABEL[a.reasonCode] : ''}{a.reason && a.reason !== (a.reasonCode && REJECT_REASON_LABEL[a.reasonCode]) ? ` — ${a.reason}` : ''}</div>}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <ol className="m-0 grid list-none gap-0 p-0">
        {[...job.history].reverse().map((h, i) => (
          <li key={`${h.at}-${i}`} className="flex gap-3">
            <span className={cn('mt-1.5 size-3 shrink-0 rounded-full', i === 0 ? 'bg-accent' : 'bg-green')} />
            <div className="pb-2.5 text-[12.5px] leading-6">
              <div className="font-bold">{ACTION[h.action] ?? h.action} · {h.label}{h.providerName ? ` · ${h.providerName}` : ''}</div>
              <div className="text-muted-2">{when(h.at)} · {h.byName ?? BY[h.byRole]}{h.stage ? ` · مرحله: ${h.stage === 'before_production' ? 'قبل از تولید' : h.stage === 'in_production' ? 'حین تولید' : 'پس از تولید'}` : ''}</div>
              {h.reason && <div className="text-muted-1">{h.reason}</div>}
            </div>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
    </section>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `JobSheet.tsx`**

```tsx
import { ErrorState, LoadingBlock, TONES } from '@/components/brand'
import { Sheet, SheetContent, SheetDescription, SheetHeader, SheetTitle } from '@/components/ui/sheet'
import { DELIVERY_METHOD_LABEL, JOB_STATUS_LABEL, JOB_STATUS_TONE, PRIORITY_LABEL } from '@/lib/dispatch-labels'
import { fa, money } from '@/lib/format'
import { NO_PROVIDER_PRICE } from '@/lib/provider-labels'
import type { DispatchJobDetail } from '@/lib/types'
import { ORDER_STATUS_LABEL } from '@/lib/types'
import { Tag } from '../../components/controls'
import { useDispatchJob } from '../../dispatch-api'
import { AssignPanel } from './AssignPanel'
import { when } from './format'
import { JobActions } from './JobActions'
import { JobHistory } from './JobHistory'

export function JobSheet({ jobId, onClose }: { jobId?: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  return (
    <Sheet open={!!jobId} onOpenChange={(o) => !o && onClose()}>
      <SheetContent data-role="admin" side="right" className="w-full gap-0 overflow-y-auto border-line bg-shell p-0 sm:max-w-[620px]">
        {jobId && <Detail id={jobId} />}
      </SheetContent>
    </Sheet>
  )
}

function Detail({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const query = useDispatchJob(id)
  const job = query.data
  return (
    <>
      <SheetHeader className="border-b border-line bg-white px-5 pt-5 pb-4">
        <SheetTitle className="text-xl font-black">{job ? `سفارش ${fa(job.orderCode)}` : 'سفارش'}</SheetTitle>
        <SheetDescription className="text-[13px] text-muted-2">{job ? `${job.customerName} · ${job.summary}` : 'در حال بارگذاری…'}</SheetDescription>
        {job && (
          <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <Tag bg={TONES[JOB_STATUS_TONE[job.status]].soft} fg={TONES[JOB_STATUS_TONE[job.status]].ink}>{JOB_STATUS_LABEL[job.status]}</Tag>
            <Tag bg={TONES.ink.soft} fg={TONES.ink.ink}>سفارش: {ORDER_STATUS_LABEL[job.orderStatus]}</Tag>
            <Tag bg={TONES.amber.soft} fg={TONES.amber.ink}>اولویت {PRIORITY_LABEL[job.priority]}</Tag>
            <span className={job.late ? 'text-[12px] font-bold text-pink-dark' : 'text-[12px] text-muted-2'}>مهلت: {when(job.deadline)}{job.late ? ' · دیرکرد' : ''}</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </SheetHeader>
      <div className="grid gap-3.5 p-5">
        {query.isPending && <LoadingBlock rows={4} />}
        {query.isError && <ErrorState error={query.error} onRetry={() => void query.refetch()} />}
        {job && (
          <>
            <Summary job={job} />
            {job.status === 'unassigned' && <AssignPanel job={job} mode="assign" />}
            {['assigned', 'accepted', 'in_production'].includes(job.status) && <AssignPanel job={job} mode="reassign" />}
            <PricingCard job={job} />
            <JobActions job={job} />
            <JobHistory job={job} />
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

function Summary({ job }: { job: DispatchJobDetail }) {
  const d = job.order.delivery
  const place = d.pickupAt === 'provider' ? d.providerShop : d.pickupPoint
  return (
    <section className="grid gap-1.5 rounded-[22px] border border-line bg-white p-4 text-[13px] leading-6">
      <div><b>مجری:</b> {job.provider?.name ?? '—'}{job.respondBy ? ` · پاسخ تا ${when(job.respondBy)}` : ''}</div>
      <div><b>تحویل:</b> {DELIVERY_METHOD_LABEL[job.deliveryMethod]}{job.deliveryProvider ? ` · ${job.deliveryProvider.name}` : ''}{place ? ` · ${place.name}` : ''}</div>
      {job.homePickup && <div><b>تحویل‌گیری از منزل:</b> {job.order.pickup.date ? `${job.order.pickup.date} · ${job.order.pickup.slot ?? ''}` : '—'}</div>}
      <div><b>آدرس:</b> {job.order.pickup.address || '—'} · <span dir="ltr">{fa(job.order.pickup.phone)}</span></div>
      {d.note && <div><b>توضیحات تحویل:</b> {d.note}</div>}
      <div><b>برگ چاپ:</b> {fa(job.sheets)} · <b>کنترل کیفیت:</b> {job.order.qcDone ? 'کامل' : 'ناقص'} · <b>مبلغ سفارش:</b> {money(job.order.total)}</div>
      {job.cancel && <div className="font-bold text-pink-dark">لغو در مرحله {job.cancel.stage === 'before_production' ? 'قبل از تولید' : job.cancel.stage === 'in_production' ? 'حین تولید' : 'پس از تولید'}{job.cancel.providerName ? ` (${job.cancel.providerName})` : ''}</div>}
    </section>
  )
}

/** The provider-price snapshot of the current assignment (§40). Admin-only. */
function PricingCard({ job }: { job: DispatchJobDetail }) {
  const p = job.providerPricing
  if (!p) return null
  return (
    <section className="rounded-[22px] border border-line bg-white p-4 text-[12.5px] leading-6">
      <div className="mb-1 flex justify-between"><h3 className="m-0 text-[15px]">قیمت مجری (ثبت‌شده هنگام تخصیص)</h3><b>{p.providerSubtotal === null ? NO_PROVIDER_PRICE : money(p.providerSubtotal)}</b></div>
      {p.lines.map((l, i) => <div key={i} className="flex justify-between gap-2"><span>{l.label} × {fa(l.quantity)}</span><span>{money(l.subtotal)}</span></div>)}
      {p.missing.map((m, i) => <div key={`m${i}`} className="text-pink-dark">{NO_PROVIDER_PRICE}: {m.label}</div>)}
      {p.delivery && <div className="flex justify-between"><span>هزینه ارسال مجری</span><span>{money(p.delivery.fee)}</span></div>}
      {p.payoutBasis !== null && <div className="mt-1 flex justify-between font-bold"><span>مبنای پرداخت به مجری</span><span>{money(p.payoutBasis)}</span></div>}
    </section>
  )
}
```
