```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { fa } from '@/lib/format'
import { QueryView } from '../../components/kit'
import { AdminRow, AdminTable } from '../../components/table'
import { useDispatchPerformance } from '../../dispatch-api'

const COLUMNS = 'minmax(0,1.3fr) 70px 70px 70px 70px 70px 80px 80px 70px 90px 90px 90px'
const mins = (m: number | null) => (m === null ? '—' : m >= 60 ? `${fa(Math.round(m / 6) / 10)} ساعت` : `${fa(m)} دقیقه`)

/** §67: collected per provider for admins — informative only, never a penalty. */
export function PerformanceTab() {
  const [from, setFrom] = useState('')
  const [to, setTo] = useState('')
  const query = useDispatchPerformance(from || undefined, to || undefined)
  return (
    <>
      <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-end gap-2.5">
        <label className="grid gap-1 text-[12px] font-bold text-muted-2">از (میلادی)<Input type="date" dir="ltr" value={from} onChange={(e) => setFrom(e.target.value)} className="h-11 w-[170px]" /></label>
        <label className="grid gap-1 text-[12px] font-bold text-muted-2">تا (میلادی)<Input type="date" dir="ltr" value={to} onChange={(e) => setTo(e.target.value)} className="h-11 w-[170px]" /></label>
        <span className="text-[12px] text-muted-2">بدون تاریخ: ۳۰ روز گذشته. این آمار فقط برای بررسی است و جریمه‌ای خودکار ندارد.</span>
      </div>
      <QueryView query={query} rows={5} isEmpty={(d) => d.length === 0} empty={{ title: 'مجری‌ای ثبت نشده است' }}>
        {(rows) => (
          <AdminTable columns={COLUMNS} minWidth={1200}
            head={['مجری', 'سپرده', 'پذیرش', 'رد', 'بی‌پاسخ', 'ناموفق', 'تکمیل', 'لغو', 'دیرکرد', 'زمان پاسخ', 'زمان تولید', 'زمان تحویل']}>
            {rows.map((r) => (
              <AdminRow key={r.providerId} className="py-3">
                <span className="min-w-0">
                  <span className="block truncate font-bold">{r.name}</span>
                  <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-muted-2">باز: {fa(r.activeJobs)} · امروز {fa(r.todaySheets)} برگ</span>
                  {r.rejectReasons.length > 0 && <span className="block truncate text-[11px] text-pink-dark">{r.rejectReasons.map((x) => `${x.label} (${fa(x.count)})`).join('، ')}</span>}
                </span>
                <span>{fa(r.assigned)}</span><span>{fa(r.accepted)}</span><span>{fa(r.rejected)}</span><span>{fa(r.timeouts)}</span><span>{fa(r.failed)}</span>
                <span>{fa(r.completed)}</span><span>{fa(r.cancelled)}</span><span>{fa(r.late)}</span>
                <span className="text-[12px]">{mins(r.avgResponseMinutes)}</span><span className="text-[12px]">{mins(r.avgProductionMinutes)}</span><span className="text-[12px]">{mins(r.avgDeliveryMinutes)}</span>
              </AdminRow>
            ))}
          </AdminTable>
        )}
      </QueryView>
    </>
  )
}
```

`settings/DispatchCard.tsx`:

```tsx
import { fa } from '@/lib/format'
import type { DispatchSettings } from '@/lib/types'
import { NumInput } from '../../components/controls'
import { AdminCard, CardNote, CardTitle } from '../../components/kit'

export function dispatchProblem(d: DispatchSettings): string | null {
  if (d.responseTimeoutMinutes < 10 || d.responseTimeoutMinutes > 1440) return 'مهلت پاسخ مجری باید بین ۱۰ تا ۱۴۴۰ دقیقه باشد'
  if (d.leadHours < 1 || d.leadHours > 720 || d.urgentLeadHours < 1 || d.urgentLeadHours > 720) return 'مهلت انجام سفارش باید بین ۱ تا ۷۲۰ ساعت باشد'
  if (d.deadlineReminderMinutes < 0 || d.deadlineReminderMinutes > 1440) return 'یادآوری مهلت باید بین ۰ تا ۱۴۴۰ دقیقه باشد'
  if (d.autoCompleteHours < 0 || d.autoCompleteHours > 720) return 'تکمیل خودکار باید بین ۰ تا ۷۲۰ ساعت باشد'
  return null
}

const ROWS: { key: keyof DispatchSettings; label: string; unit: string; hint?: string }[] = [
  { key: 'responseTimeoutMinutes', label: 'مهلت پاسخ مجری', unit: 'دقیقه', hint: 'بی‌پاسخ ماندن تا این زمان، سفارش را به «تخصیص‌نیافته» برمی‌گرداند و به مدیران هشدار می‌دهد.' },
  { key: 'leadHours', label: 'مهلت انجام سفارش عادی', unit: 'ساعت' },
  { key: 'urgentLeadHours', label: 'مهلت انجام سفارش فوری', unit: 'ساعت' },
  { key: 'deadlineReminderMinutes', label: 'یادآوری مهلت به مجری', unit: 'دقیقه قبل', hint: '۰ = بدون یادآوری' },
  { key: 'autoCompleteHours', label: 'تکمیل خودکار پس از تحویل', unit: 'ساعت', hint: '۰ = تکمیل دستی' },
]

export function DispatchCard({ value, onChange, problem }: { value: DispatchSettings; onChange: (p: Partial<DispatchSettings>) => void; problem: string | null }) {
  return (
    <AdminCard>
      <CardTitle>تخصیص سفارش به مجریان</CardTitle>
      <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3">
        {ROWS.map((r) => (
          <div key={r.key} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="text-[13.5px] font-bold">{r.label}</div>
              {r.hint && <div className="text-[11.5px] leading-6 text-muted-2">{r.hint}</div>}
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <NumInput value={value[r.key]} onValueChange={(n) => onChange({ [r.key]: n })} aria-label={r.label} />
              <span className="text-[12px] text-muted-2">{r.unit}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      {problem && <div className="mt-2 text-[12.5px] font-bold text-pink-dark">{problem}</div>}
      <CardNote>مهلت پاسخ فعلی: {fa(value.responseTimeoutMinutes)} دقیقه؛ تغییر آن روی تخصیص‌های بعدی اعمال می‌شود.</CardNote>
    </AdminCard>
  )
}
```

`settings/PickupPointsCard.tsx`:

```tsx
import { DsSwitch } from '@/components/brand'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { toEnDigits } from '@/lib/format'
import type { PickupPoint } from '@/lib/types'
import { AddButton, IconAction } from '../../components/controls'
import { AdminCard, CardNote, CardTitle } from '../../components/kit'

export function pointsProblem(list: PickupPoint[]): string | null {
  if (list.length > 30) return 'حداکثر ۳۰ محل تحویل حضوری مجاز است'
  for (const p of list) {
    if (!p.name.trim()) return 'نام محل تحویل را وارد کنید'
    if (!p.address.trim()) return 'آدرس محل تحویل را وارد کنید'
    if (!/^\d{5,15}$/.test(toEnDigits(p.phone))) return 'شماره تماس محل تحویل معتبر نیست'
  }
  return null
}

/** D17 «محل‌های تحویل حضوری» of Digital Sarv (provider shops are chosen per order by the admin). */
export function PickupPointsCard({ value, onChange, problem }: { value: PickupPoint[]; onChange: (list: PickupPoint[]) => void; problem: string | null }) {
  const set = (i: number, patch: Partial<PickupPoint>) => onChange(value.map((p, j) => (j === i ? { ...p, ...patch } : p)))
  return (
    <AdminCard>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <CardTitle>محل‌های تحویل حضوری</CardTitle>
        <AddButton onClick={() => onChange([...value, { id: '', name: '', address: '', phone: '', hours: '', on: true }])}>افزودن محل</AddButton>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 grid gap-2.5">
        {value.length === 0 && <div className="text-[12.5px] text-muted-2">هنوز محلی ثبت نشده؛ تا آن زمان مشتری گزینه «تحویل حضوری» ندارد.</div>}
        {value.map((p, i) => (
          <div key={p.id || `new-${i}`} className="grid gap-2 rounded-[18px] border border-line p-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <Input placeholder="نام محل" value={p.name} onChange={(e) => set(i, { name: e.target.value })} />
            <Input placeholder="ساعت کاری، مثلاً ۹ تا ۱۸" value={p.hours} onChange={(e) => set(i, { hours: e.target.value })} />
            <Input placeholder="آدرس" value={p.address} onChange={(e) => set(i, { address: e.target.value })} className="sm:col-span-2" />
            <Input placeholder="تلفن" dir="ltr" inputMode="tel" value={p.phone} onChange={(e) => set(i, { phone: toEnDigits(e.target.value) })} className="text-start" />
            <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5">
              <span className="text-[12.5px] font-bold">{p.on ? 'فعال' : 'غیرفعال'}</span>
              <DsSwitch checked={p.on} onCheckedChange={(on) => set(i, { on })} label={`فعال — ${p.name}`} />
              <IconAction kind="delete" label="حذف" onClick={() => onChange(value.filter((_, j) => j !== i))} />
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      {problem && <div className="mt-2 text-[12.5px] font-bold text-pink-dark">{problem}</div>}
      <CardNote>تحویل در فروشگاه یک مجری فقط وقتی ممکن است که «اجازه تحویل حضوری از مجری» برای او روشن باشد؛ آن را مدیر هنگام تخصیص انتخاب می‌کند.</CardNote>
    </AdminCard>
  )
}
```

`api.ts`: `export interface SettingsPatch { …; dispatch?: Partial<DispatchSettings>; pickupPoints?: PickupPoint[] }` (import the types).

  const [dispatch, setDispatch] = useState<DispatchSettings>(initial.dispatch)
  const [points, setPoints] = useState<PickupPoint[]>(initial.pickupPoints)

  }

- [ ] **Step 2: `job-actions.ts`** (mirrors the contract flow; the server re-checks every move)

```ts
import type { ProviderJobDetail } from '@/lib/types'

export interface ProviderAction { status: 'accept' | 'reject' | 'in_production' | 'ready' | 'out_for_delivery' | 'ready_for_pickup' | 'delivered' | 'failed'; label: string; tone: 'primary' | 'danger' | 'secondary'; disabled?: string }

export function providerActions(j: ProviderJobDetail): ProviderAction[] {
  const production = j.roles.includes('production')
  const delivery = j.roles.includes('delivery')
  const shop = j.delivery.method === 'customer_pickup' && j.delivery.pickupAt === 'provider'
  const qcDone = !j.qc || j.qc.done.every(Boolean)
  const out: ProviderAction[] = []
  if (production && j.status === 'assigned') out.push({ status: 'accept', label: 'پذیرش سفارش', tone: 'primary' }, { status: 'reject', label: 'رد سفارش', tone: 'danger' })
  if (production && j.status === 'accepted') {
    out.push({ status: 'in_production', label: 'شروع تولید', tone: 'primary', ...(j.itemsReceived ? {} : { disabled: 'اقلام هنوز از مشتری تحویل گرفته نشده است' }) })
    out.push({ status: 'failed', label: 'اعلام عدم امکان انجام', tone: 'danger' })
  }
  if (production && j.status === 'in_production') {
    out.push({ status: 'ready', label: 'آماده شد', tone: 'primary', ...(qcDone ? {} : { disabled: 'ابتدا چک‌لیست کنترل کیفیت را کامل کنید' }) })
    out.push({ status: 'failed', label: 'اعلام عدم امکان انجام', tone: 'danger' })
  }
  if (production && shop && j.status === 'ready') out.push({ status: 'ready_for_pickup', label: 'آماده تحویل حضوری', tone: 'primary' })
  if (production && shop && j.status === 'ready_for_pickup') out.push({ status: 'delivered', label: 'به مشتری تحویل داده شد', tone: 'primary' })
  if (delivery && j.delivery.method === 'provider' && j.status === 'ready') out.push({ status: 'out_for_delivery', label: 'ارسال شد', tone: 'primary' })
  if (delivery && j.delivery.method === 'provider' && j.status === 'out_for_delivery') out.push({ status: 'delivered', label: 'تحویل داده شد', tone: 'primary' })
  return out
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `RejectSheet.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Chip } from '@/components/brand'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Sheet, SheetContent, SheetDescription, SheetHeader, SheetTitle } from '@/components/ui/sheet'
import { toast } from '@/components/ui/sonner'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'
import { REJECT_REASON_LABEL, REJECT_REASONS } from '@/lib/dispatch-labels'
import type { RejectReason } from '@/lib/types'

/** §59 reasons (required); «سایر» needs a note. `failed` = the provider cannot finish after accepting. */
export function RejectSheet({ open, mode, pending, onClose, onSubmit }: {
  open: boolean; mode: 'reject' | 'failed'; pending?: boolean; onClose: () => void; onSubmit: (code: RejectReason, note: string) => void
}) {
  const [code, setCode] = useState<RejectReason | null>(null)
  const [note, setNote] = useState('')
  const submit = () => {
    if (!code) return toast.error('دلیل را انتخاب کنید')
    if (code === 'other' && !note.trim()) return toast.error('برای «سایر» توضیح بنویسید')
    onSubmit(code, note.trim())
  }
  return (
    <Sheet open={open} onOpenChange={(o) => !o && onClose()}>
      <SheetContent data-role="provider" side="bottom" className="mx-auto max-w-[520px] rounded-t-[26px] border-none bg-white p-5">
        <SheetHeader className="p-0 text-start">
          <SheetTitle className="text-lg font-black">{mode === 'reject' ? 'رد سفارش' : 'اعلام عدم امکان انجام'}</SheetTitle>
          <SheetDescription className="text-[12.5px] text-muted-2">سفارش به دیجیتال سرو برمی‌گردد تا به مجری دیگری سپرده شود.</SheetDescription>
        </SheetHeader>
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
          {REJECT_REASONS.map((r) => <Chip key={r} size="sm" selected={code === r} onClick={() => setCode(r)}>{REJECT_REASON_LABEL[r]}</Chip>)}
        </div>
        <Textarea rows={3} value={note} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} maxLength={300} placeholder="توضیح (برای «سایر» الزامی است)" className="mt-3" />
        <Button variant="destructive" block className="mt-3 h-12" disabled={pending} onClick={submit}>{pending ? 'در حال ثبت…' : 'ثبت'}</Button>
      </SheetContent>
    </Sheet>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router'
import { Bell, ChevronLeft, ClipboardList } from 'lucide-react'
import { Chip, EmptyState, HeaderIconButton, LoadingBlock, MobileHeader, ScreenBody, ToneTag } from '@/components/brand'
import { JOB_STATUS_TONE, PRIORITY_LABEL, PROVIDER_TABS } from '@/lib/dispatch-labels'
import { fa, jalali, jalaliDayTime, money } from '@/lib/format'
import { useInboxUnread } from '@/lib/notifications'
import type { ProviderJobView, ProviderTab, ProviderWithdrawnView } from '@/lib/types'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import { useProviderJobs } from './jobs-api'
import { ProviderError } from './ProviderError'

const when = (iso: string) => `${jalaliDayTime(iso)} · ${jalali(iso, { day: 'numeric', month: 'long' })}`
const isWithdrawn = (j: ProviderJobView | ProviderWithdrawnView): j is ProviderWithdrawnView => 'withdrawn' in j

export function JobsScreen() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<ProviderTab>('new')
  const query = useProviderJobs(tab)
  const unread = useInboxUnread('provider')
  const counts = query.data?.counts
  return (
    <>
      <MobileHeader title="سفارش‌ها" subtitle="سفارش‌هایی که به شما سپرده شده" icon={ClipboardList} tone="cyan"
        actions={
          <HeaderIconButton aria-label="اعلان‌ها" onClick={() => navigate('/provider/notifications')}>
            <Bell className="size-[19px]" strokeWidth={2.4} />
            {unread > 0 && <span className="absolute -top-1 -end-1 rounded-full bg-pink px-1.5 text-[10.5px] font-black text-white">{fa(unread)}</span>}
          </HeaderIconButton>
        } />
      <ScreenBody>
        <div className="no-scrollbar -mx-1 flex gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto px-1 pb-1" role="tablist" aria-label="وضعیت سفارش">
          {PROVIDER_TABS.map((t) => (
            <Chip key={t.key} size="sm" role="tab" aria-selected={tab === t.key} selected={tab === t.key} onClick={() => setTab(t.key)} className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap">
              {t.label}{counts && counts[t.key] ? ` (${fa(counts[t.key])})` : ''}
            </Chip>
          ))}
        </div>
        {query.isPending ? <LoadingBlock rows={4} className="mt-3" />
          : query.isError ? <div className="mt-3"><ProviderError error={query.error} onRetry={() => void query.refetch()} /></div>
          : query.data.items.length === 0 ? <EmptyState className="mt-3" title="سفارشی در این بخش نیست" hint={tab === 'new' ? 'سفارش‌های تازه همین‌جا و در اعلان‌ها نمایش داده می‌شوند.' : undefined} />
          : (
            <div className="mt-3 grid gap-2.5">
              {query.data.items.map((j) => isWithdrawn(j) ? (
                <div key={j.id} className="rounded-[22px] border border-line bg-white p-4 opacity-80">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between"><span className="font-black">سفارش {fa(j.orderCode)}</span><ToneTag tone="pink">{j.label}</ToneTag></div>
                  <div className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-muted-2">{when(j.at)}</div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <button key={j.id} type="button" onClick={() => navigate(`/provider/jobs/${j.id}`)}
                  className="flex w-full cursor-pointer items-center gap-3 rounded-[22px] border border-line bg-white p-4 text-start hover:bg-accent-soft/40">
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="text-[14.5px] font-black">سفارش {fa(j.orderCode)}</span>
                      <ToneTag tone={JOB_STATUS_TONE[j.status]}>{j.statusLabel}</ToneTag>
                      {j.priority !== 'normal' && <ToneTag tone="pink">{PRIORITY_LABEL[j.priority]}</ToneTag>}
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-1 truncate text-[12.5px] text-muted-1">{j.summary} · {j.delivery.label}</div>
                    <div className={cn('mt-1 text-[12px]', j.late ? 'font-bold text-pink-dark' : 'text-muted-2')}>
                      مهلت: {when(j.deadline)}{j.respondBy ? ` · پاسخ تا ${jalaliDayTime(j.respondBy)}` : ''}
                    </div>
                    {j.providerSubtotal !== null && <div className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] font-black text-ink">سهم شما: {money(j.providerSubtotal)}</div>}
                  </div>
                  <ChevronLeft className="size-5 shrink-0 text-muted-3" strokeWidth={2.4} />
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
      </ScreenBody>
    </>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `JobScreen.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router'
import { ClipboardList, FileText } from 'lucide-react'
import { EmptyState, InfoBanner, LoadingBlock, MobileHeader, Panel, ScreenBody, ToneTag } from '@/components/brand'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { notify, toast } from '@/components/ui/sonner'
import { ApiError, openUpload } from '@/lib/api'
import { JOB_OPTION_LABEL, JOB_STATUS_TONE } from '@/lib/dispatch-labels'
import { fa, jalali, jalaliDayTime, money } from '@/lib/format'
import { NO_PROVIDER_PRICE } from '@/lib/provider-labels'
import { useCatalog } from '@/lib/query'
import type { ProviderJobDetail } from '@/lib/types'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import { providerActions, type ProviderAction } from './job-actions'
import { useProviderJob, useProviderJobMutations } from './jobs-api'
import { ProviderError } from './ProviderError'
import { RejectSheet } from './RejectSheet'

const when = (iso: string) => `${jalaliDayTime(iso)} · ${jalali(iso, { day: 'numeric', month: 'long' })}`

export function JobScreen() {
  const { id = '' } = useParams()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const query = useProviderJob(id)
  const gone = query.error instanceof ApiError && query.error.status === 404
  return (
    <>
      <MobileHeader title={query.data ? `سفارش ${fa(query.data.orderCode)}` : 'سفارش'} subtitle={query.data?.statusLabel} icon={ClipboardList} tone="cyan" onBack={() => navigate('/provider')} />
      <ScreenBody>
        {query.isPending ? <LoadingBlock rows={5} />
          : gone ? <EmptyState title="این سفارش دیگر در دسترس شما نیست" hint="ممکن است به مجری دیگری سپرده یا لغو شده باشد." action={<Button size="sm" onClick={() => navigate('/provider')}>بازگشت به سفارش‌ها</Button>} />
          : query.isError ? <ProviderError error={query.error} onRetry={() => void query.refetch()} />
          : <JobBody job={query.data} />}
      </ScreenBody>
    </>
  )
}

function Section({ title, children }: { title: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <Panel className="mt-3">
      <div className="mb-2 text-[13.5px] font-black">{title}</div>
      <div className="grid gap-1.5 text-[13px] leading-6 text-muted-1">{children}</div>
    </Panel>
  )
}

function JobBody({ job }: { job: ProviderJobDetail }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const m = useProviderJobMutations(job.id)
  const catalog = useCatalog()
  const paper = (k: string) => catalog.data?.papers.find((p) => p.key === k)?.name ?? k
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState<'reject' | 'failed' | null>(null)
  const actions = providerActions(job)
  const busy = m.accept.isPending || m.status.isPending || m.reject.isPending

  const act = (a: ProviderAction) => {
    if (a.disabled) return toast.error(a.disabled)
    if (a.status === 'reject' || a.status === 'failed') return setSheet(a.status)
    if (a.status === 'accept') return m.accept.mutate(undefined, { onSuccess: () => notify('سفارش پذیرفته شد') })
    m.status.mutate({ status: a.status }, { onSuccess: () => notify(a.label) })
  }

  return (
    <>
      <Panel>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <ToneTag tone={JOB_STATUS_TONE[job.status]}>{job.statusLabel}</ToneTag>
          <ToneTag tone="ink">{job.delivery.label}</ToneTag>
          {job.late && <ToneTag tone="pink">دیرکرد</ToneTag>}
        </div>
        <div className="mt-2 text-[13px] leading-6 text-muted-1">
          مهلت انجام: <b className="text-ink">{when(job.deadline)}</b>
          {job.respondBy && <><br />مهلت پاسخ: <b className="text-ink">{when(job.respondBy)}</b></>}
        </div>
      </Panel>
      {!job.itemsReceived && job.roles.includes('production') && <InfoBanner tone="amber" className="mt-3">اقلام این سفارش هنوز از مشتری تحویل گرفته نشده است؛ پس از رسیدن پیک تولید را شروع کنید.</InfoBanner>}
      {job.roles.includes('production') && job.delivery.method === 'courier' && job.status === 'ready' && <InfoBanner tone="cyan" className="mt-3">پیک دیجیتال سرو برای دریافت سفارش مراجعه می‌کند.</InfoBanner>}

      {job.lines.length > 0 && (
        <Section title="اقلام سفارش">
          {job.lines.map((l, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="border-t border-line-soft pt-2 first:border-t-0 first:pt-0">
              <div className="font-extrabold text-ink">{l.label}{l.childName ? ` — ${l.childName}` : ''} · {fa(l.quantity)}</div>
              <div className="text-[12.5px]"><bdi>{l.detail}</bdi></div>
              <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                {Object.entries(l.options).map(([k, v]) => (
                  <span key={k} className="rounded-full bg-line-soft px-2.5 py-1 text-[11.5px]">
                    {JOB_OPTION_LABEL[k] ?? k}: {typeof v === 'boolean' ? (v ? 'بله' : 'خیر') : k === 'paper' ? paper(String(v)) : typeof v === 'number' ? fa(v) : v}
                  </span>
                ))}
              </div>
              {l.note && <div className="mt-1 text-[12px]">یادداشت مشتری: <bdi>{l.note}</bdi></div>}
            </div>
          ))}
        </Section>
      )}

      {job.files.length > 0 && (
        <Section title="فایل‌ها">
          {job.files.map((f) => (
            <button key={f.id} type="button" onClick={() => void openUpload(f.id).catch((e: unknown) => toast.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'فایل باز نشد'))}
              className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-[14px] bg-line-soft px-3 py-2 text-start text-[12.5px] font-bold text-ink hover:bg-accent-soft">
              <FileText className="size-4" strokeWidth={2.4} /> <bdi className="truncate">{f.name}</bdi>{f.pages ? ` · ${fa(f.pages)} صفحه` : ''}
            </button>
          ))}
        </Section>
      )}

      {job.qc && ['accepted', 'in_production'].includes(job.status) && (
        <Section title="کنترل کیفیت">
          {job.qc.labels.map((label, i) => (
            <label key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={job.qc!.done[i]} disabled={m.qc.isPending} onChange={(e) => m.qc.mutate({ index: i, done: e.target.checked })} className="size-4" />
              <span className={cn(job.qc!.done[i] && 'text-ink')}>{label}</span>
            </label>
          ))}
        </Section>
      )}

      {job.roles.includes('production') && (
        <Section title="سهم شما (قیمت مجری)">
          {job.pricing ? (
            <>
              {job.pricing.lines.map((l, i) => <div key={i} className="flex justify-between gap-2"><span>{l.label} × {fa(l.quantity)}</span><span>{money(l.subtotal)}</span></div>)}
              {job.pricing.missing.map((x, i) => <div key={`m${i}`} className="text-pink-dark">{NO_PROVIDER_PRICE}: {x}</div>)}
              <div className="flex justify-between font-black text-ink"><span>جمع</span><span>{job.pricing.subtotal === null ? NO_PROVIDER_PRICE : money(job.pricing.subtotal)}</span></div>
            </>
          ) : <div>{NO_PROVIDER_PRICE}</div>}
        </Section>
      )}

      {job.contact && (
        <Section title={job.roles.includes('delivery') ? 'تحویل به مشتری' : 'تحویل حضوری در محل شما'}>
          <div className="font-bold text-ink">{job.contact.name}</div>
          <a href={`tel:${job.contact.phone}`} dir="ltr" className="text-start font-bold text-accent">{fa(job.contact.phone)}</a>
          {job.contact.address && <div>{job.contact.address}</div>}
          {job.contact.note && <div>توضیحات: {job.contact.note}</div>}
        </Section>
      )}
      {job.collectFrom && (
        <Section title="دریافت از">
          <div className="font-bold text-ink">{job.collectFrom.name}</div>
          <div>{job.collectFrom.address}</div>
          <a href={`tel:${job.collectFrom.phone}`} dir="ltr" className="text-start font-bold text-accent">{fa(job.collectFrom.phone)}</a>
        </Section>
      )}

      {job.history.length > 0 && (
        <Section title="سابقه">
          {[...job.history].reverse().map((h, i) => <div key={i}>{h.label} · {when(h.at)}{h.note ? ` — ${h.note}` : ''}</div>)}
        </Section>
      )}

      {actions.length > 0 && (
        <div className="sticky bottom-0 mt-4 grid gap-2 bg-shell pt-2 pb-1">
          {actions.map((a) => (
            <Button key={a.status} block className="h-12" variant={a.tone === 'danger' ? 'destructive' : a.tone === 'secondary' ? 'secondary' : 'default'}
              disabled={busy} aria-disabled={!!a.disabled} onClick={() => act(a)}>
              {a.label}
            </Button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <RejectSheet open={!!sheet} mode={sheet ?? 'reject'} pending={m.reject.isPending || m.status.isPending} onClose={() => setSheet(null)}
        onSubmit={(code, note) => {
          const after = () => { setSheet(null); notify('ثبت شد؛ سفارش به دیجیتال سرو برگشت'); navigate('/provider') }
          if (sheet === 'reject') m.reject.mutate({ reasonCode: code, ...(note ? { note } : {}) }, { onSuccess: after })
          else m.status.mutate({ status: 'failed', reasonCode: code, ...(note ? { note } : {}) }, { onSuccess: after })
        }} />
    </>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router'
import { Bell } from 'lucide-react'
import { EmptyState, LoadingBlock, MobileHeader, Panel, ScreenBody } from '@/components/brand'
import { PushRow } from '@/components/notifications/PushRow'
import { fa, jalali, jalaliDayTime } from '@/lib/format'
import { useInbox, useMarkInboxRead } from '@/lib/notifications'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import { ProviderError } from './ProviderError'

/** D19: provider notifications = in-app inbox + browser push (no SMS). */
export function NotificationsScreen() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const inbox = useInbox('provider')
  const markRead = useMarkInboxRead('provider')
  const unread = inbox.data?.unread ?? 0
  return (
    <>
      <MobileHeader title="اعلان‌ها" subtitle={unread ? `${fa(unread)} خوانده‌نشده` : 'همه خوانده شده'} icon={Bell} tone="cyan"
        actions={unread > 0 ? <button type="button" className="cursor-pointer text-[12px] font-extrabold text-accent" onClick={() => markRead.mutate(undefined)}>همه خوانده شد</button> : undefined} />
      <ScreenBody>
        <Panel><PushRow /></Panel>
        {inbox.isPending ? <LoadingBlock rows={4} className="mt-3" />
          : inbox.isError ? <div className="mt-3"><ProviderError error={inbox.error} onRetry={() => void inbox.refetch()} /></div>
          : inbox.data.items.length === 0 ? <EmptyState className="mt-3" title="اعلانی ندارید" />
          : (
            <div className="mt-3 grid gap-2">
              {inbox.data.items.map((n) => (
                <button key={n.id} type="button" onClick={() => { if (!n.read) markRead.mutate([n.id]); navigate(n.url) }}
                  className={cn('cursor-pointer rounded-[20px] border border-line bg-white p-3.5 text-start', !n.read && 'border-cyan/40 bg-cyan-soft/40')}>
                  <div className="text-[13.5px] font-bold">{n.text}</div>
                  <div className="mt-1 text-[11.5px] text-muted-2">سفارش {fa(n.orderCode)} · {jalaliDayTime(n.createdAt)} · {jalali(n.createdAt, { day: 'numeric', month: 'long' })}</div>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
      </ScreenBody>
    </>
  )
}
```

- Produces: draft `delivery: { method: 'courier' | 'customer_pickup'; pickupPointId?: string; note: string }`, `setDelivery(patch)`; `selectQuoteInput` is **unchanged** (the quote request stays byte-identical to M2 — D22); `selectOrderDraft` adds the `delivery`; `DeliveryChooser({ urgent })`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Draft store** — `stores/draft.ts`:

```ts
export interface DraftDelivery { method: 'courier' | 'customer_pickup'; pickupPointId?: string; note: string }
const emptyDelivery = (): DraftDelivery => ({ method: 'courier', note: '' })
```

`DraftData` gains `delivery: DraftDelivery`; `DraftActions` gains `setDelivery: (patch: Partial<DraftDelivery>) => void`; `initial()` adds `delivery: emptyDelivery()`; implement `setDelivery: (patch) => set((s) => ({ delivery: { ...s.delivery, ...patch } }))`; `applyDraft` sets `delivery: { ...emptyDelivery(), ...(draft.delivery ?? {}) }`; `partialize` keeps `delivery`; bump `version: 3` with `migrate` adding `if (!s.delivery) s.delivery = emptyDelivery()` for every older version. Then:

```ts
// selectQuoteInput stays exactly as in M2 (no `delivery`): the delivery choice never changes the price (D22)
export function selectOrderDraft(s: DraftData): OrderDraft {
  const delivery = {
    method: s.delivery.method,
    ...(s.delivery.method === 'customer_pickup' && s.delivery.pickupPointId ? { pickupPointId: s.delivery.pickupPointId } : {}),
    ...(s.delivery.note.trim() ? { note: s.delivery.note.trim() } : {}),
  }
  return { ...selectQuoteInput(s), delivery, pickup: s.pickup, payMethod: s.payMethod }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `DeliveryChooser.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router'
import { MapPin, Store, Truck } from 'lucide-react'
import { Chip, FieldLabel, Panel } from '@/components/brand'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { notify } from '@/components/ui/sonner'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'
import { fa, toEnDigits } from '@/lib/format'
import { useCatalog } from '@/lib/query'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import { useAuth } from '@/stores/auth'
import { useDraft } from '@/stores/draft'
import { Screen } from './Screen'
import { CtaButton } from './parts'

const PHONE_RE = /^(?:\+98|0)?\d{10}$/

/** D12: print, thesis and flyer orders are not collected at home — the customer picks delivery to an address or pickup (D17 points). */
export function DeliveryChooser({ urgent }: { urgent?: ReactNode }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const catalog = useCatalog()
  const points = catalog.data?.pickupPoints ?? []
  const delivery = useDraft((s) => s.delivery)
  const setDelivery = useDraft((s) => s.setDelivery)
  const pickup = useDraft((s) => s.pickup)
  const setPickup = useDraft((s) => s.setPickup)
  const userPhone = useAuth((s) => s.user?.phone)
  const filled = useRef(false)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (filled.current || !userPhone) return
    filled.current = true
    if (!useDraft.getState().pickup.phone) setPickup({ phone: userPhone })
  }, [userPhone, setPickup])
  // a switched-off point disappears from the catalog: fall back to delivery
  useEffect(() => {
    if (delivery.method === 'customer_pickup' && catalog.data && !points.some((p) => p.id === delivery.pickupPointId)) setDelivery({ pickupPointId: points[0]?.id, ...(points.length ? {} : { method: 'courier' }) })
  }, [catalog.data, points, delivery.method, delivery.pickupPointId, setDelivery])

  const next = () => {
    if (!PHONE_RE.test(toEnDigits(pickup.phone))) return notify('شماره تماس معتبر نیست')
    if (delivery.method === 'courier' && !pickup.address.trim()) return notify('آدرس ارسال را وارد کنید')
    if (delivery.method === 'customer_pickup' && !points.some((p) => p.id === delivery.pickupPointId)) return notify('محل تحویل حضوری را انتخاب کنید')
    navigate('/app/membership')
  }

  return (
    <Screen title="روش و آدرس تحویل" subtitle="سفارش را برایتان می‌فرستیم یا حضوری تحویل بگیرید" icon={Truck} tone="green" back="/app/summary">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2" role="radiogroup" aria-label="روش تحویل">
          <Chip role="radio" aria-checked={delivery.method === 'courier'} selected={delivery.method === 'courier'} onClick={() => setDelivery({ method: 'courier' })}>
            <Truck className="me-1.5 inline size-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />ارسال به آدرس
          </Chip>
          {points.length > 0 && (
            <Chip role="radio" aria-checked={delivery.method === 'customer_pickup'} selected={delivery.method === 'customer_pickup'} onClick={() => setDelivery({ method: 'customer_pickup', pickupPointId: delivery.pickupPointId ?? points[0].id })}>
              <Store className="me-1.5 inline size-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />تحویل حضوری
            </Chip>
          )}
        </div>

        {delivery.method === 'courier' ? (
          <div>
            <FieldLabel htmlFor="delivery-address" className="mb-[7px]">آدرس ارسال</FieldLabel>
            <Textarea id="delivery-address" rows={3} value={pickup.address} onChange={(e) => setPickup({ address: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid gap-2">
            <FieldLabel className="mb-0">محل تحویل حضوری</FieldLabel>
            {points.map((p) => (
              <button key={p.id} type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={delivery.pickupPointId === p.id} onClick={() => setDelivery({ pickupPointId: p.id })}
                className={cn('cursor-pointer rounded-[20px] border bg-white p-3.5 text-start', delivery.pickupPointId === p.id ? 'border-accent shadow-glow-sm' : 'border-line')}>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[14px] font-extrabold"><MapPin className="size-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />{p.name}</div>
                <div className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] leading-6 text-muted-2">{p.address}{p.hours ? ` · ${p.hours}` : ''}</div>
                {p.phone && <div dir="ltr" className="text-start text-[12px] text-muted-2">{fa(p.phone)}</div>}
              </button>
            ))}
            <div className="text-[11.5px] text-muted-2">وقتی سفارش آماده شد اعلان می‌گیرید؛ ممکن است محل تحویل به فروشگاه همکار نزدیک‌تر تغییر کند و در اعلان و صفحه رهگیری نمایش داده شود.</div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div>
          <FieldLabel htmlFor="delivery-phone" className="mb-[7px]">شماره تماس</FieldLabel>
          <Input id="delivery-phone" inputMode="tel" dir="ltr" value={fa(pickup.phone)} onChange={(e) => setPickup({ phone: toEnDigits(e.target.value).replace(/[^\d+]/g, '') })} className="text-end" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <FieldLabel htmlFor="delivery-note" className="mb-[7px]">توضیحات تحویل (اختیاری)</FieldLabel>
          <Textarea id="delivery-note" rows={2} maxLength={300} value={delivery.note} onChange={(e) => setDelivery({ note: e.target.value })} placeholder="مثلاً زنگ دوم، بعد از ساعت ۱۶" />
        </div>
        {urgent}
        <Panel className="text-[12px] leading-6 text-muted-2">این سفارش از منزل تحویل گرفته نمی‌شود؛ فقط آدرس ارسال یا محل تحویل حضوری را مشخص کنید.</Panel>
        <CtaButton onClick={next}>ادامه</CtaButton>
      </div>
    </Screen>
  )
}
```

export function useMarkCollected(orderId: string) {

- [ ] **Step 1: Append to `apps/api/test/app.e2e-spec.ts`** (inside the main `describe`, before «protects admin routes»):

```ts
  // ---- v4.2 dispatch and fulfillment (§86, §88, §89, §91) ---------------------------------------------
  const glossy = (pages = 200) => ({ kind: 'color_printing', spec: { pages, scope: 'all', size: 'A4', sides: 'single', copies: 1, colorType: 'full', paper: 'glossy200', binding: 'none', staple: false, laminate: 'none' } });
  /** A COD print order (no pickup slot, D12). Extra body fields are sent as-is (smuggling tests). */
  async function placeCod(token: string, services: unknown[], extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
    const res = await http().post('/api/orders').set(authH(token))
      .send({ children: [], services, payMethod: 'cod', pickup: { address: 'تهران، خیابان آزمون، پلاک ۲', phone: '09123456789' }, ...extra });
    expect(res.status, JSON.stringify(res.body)).toBe(201);
    return res.body as { id: string; code: string };
  }
  const cap = (service: string, sizes: string[] = [], papers: string[] = []) => ({
    service, sizes, colorTypes: service === 'color_printing' ? ['text', 'full'] : [], papers, sides: sizes.length ? ['single', 'double'] : [], addons: [],
  });
  /** §86 test providers A–F (find-or-create; profile reset every run) with logins 0912555000N. */
  async function testProviders(admin: string) {
    const spec: [string, unknown[], Record<string, unknown>][] = [
      ['آزمون A', [cap('color_printing', ['A3'], ['glossy200', 'tahrir80'])], {}],
      ['آزمون B', [cap('black_white_printing', ['A4'])], {}],
      ['آزمون C', [cap('color_printing', ['A4'], ['glossy200'])], { canDeliver: true, deliveryFee: 30000 }],
      ['آزمون D', [cap('school'), cap('docs')], {}],
      ['آزمون E', [cap('color_printing', ['A3', 'A4'], ['tahrir80'])], {}],
      ['آزمون F', [], { canDeliver: true, deliveryFee: 20000 }],
    ];
    const ids = await providersByName(admin);
    const out: Record<string, { id: string; phone: string }> = {};
    let linked = false;
    for (const [i, [name, capabilities, extra]] of spec.entries()) {
      const body = { name, status: 'active', availableForOrders: true, workingHours: [], serviceZoneIds: [], capacity: { perService: [] }, capabilities, canDeliver: false, deliveryFee: 0, ...extra };
      const id = ids.get(name) ?? ((await http().post('/api/admin/providers').set(authH(admin)).send(body).expect(201)).body.id as string);
      await http().patch(`/api/admin/providers/${id}`).set(authH(admin)).send(body).expect(200);
      const phone = `0912555000${i + 1}`;
      const p = (await http().get(`/api/admin/providers/${id}`).set(authH(admin)).expect(200)).body;
      if (p.login?.phone !== phone) {
        await http().put(`/api/admin/providers/${id}/login`).set(authH(admin)).send({ phone }).expect(200);
        tokens.delete(phone);
        linked = true;
      }
      out[name.slice(-1)] = { id, phone };
    }
    if (linked) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1100)); // linking revokes earlier tokens (per-second iat)
    return out;
  }
  const jobOf = async (admin: string, orderId: string) => (await http().get(`/api/admin/dispatch/jobs/${orderId}`).set(authH(admin)).expect(200)).body;

  it('§86 + §88: only capable providers can receive it; C accepts, produces, delivers; the admin completes', async () => {
    const admin = await login('09120000000');
    const cust = await login('09123456789');
    const P = await testProviders(admin);
    await http().post('/api/admin/price-book/versions').set(authH(admin))
      .send({ layer: 'provider', providerId: P.C.id, comboKey: 'color|A4|full|glossy200|single', mode: 'manual', price: 8000, publish: true, reason: 'e2e §88', confirmNegativeMargin: true }).expect(201);
    const order = await placeCod(cust, [glossy()]);
    const job = await jobOf(admin, order.id);
    expect(job).toMatchObject({ status: 'unassigned', homePickup: false, sheets: 200 });

    const e = (await http().get(`/api/admin/dispatch/jobs/${job.id}/eligibility`).set(authH(admin)).expect(200)).body;
    const tests = [...e.eligible, ...e.others].filter((r: any) => r.name.startsWith('آزمون'));
    expect(tests.filter((r: any) => r.assignable).map((r: any) => r.name)).toEqual(['آزمون C']); // A, B, D, E, F cannot fulfil it
    expect(e.eligible.find((r: any) => r.name === 'آزمون C').price.subtotal).toBe(1_600_000);
    const noPrice = tests.find((r: any) => r.name === 'آزمون E');
    expect(noPrice.price.subtotal).toBeNull(); // «قیمت مجری تنظیم نشده», never 0 (§53)
    await http().post(`/api/admin/dispatch/jobs/${job.id}/assign`).set(authH(admin)).send({ providerId: P.A.id, override: true, reason: 'e2e' }).expect(400);

    const assigned = (await http().post(`/api/admin/dispatch/jobs/${job.id}/assign`).set(authH(admin)).send({ providerId: P.C.id, deliveryMethod: 'provider' }).expect(200)).body;
    expect(assigned).toMatchObject({ status: 'assigned', deliveryMethod: 'provider', providerPricing: { providerSubtotal: 1_600_000, payoutBasis: 1_630_000 } });

    const c = await login(P.C.phone);
    const inbox = (await http().get('/api/notifications/mine').set(authH(c)).expect(200)).body;
    expect(inbox.items.some((n: any) => n.event === 'job_assigned' && n.jobId === job.id && n.audience === 'provider')).toBe(true); // §88 «C receives notification»
    const step = async (status: string) => (await http().post(`/api/provider/jobs/${job.id}/status`).set(authH(c)).send({ status }).expect(200)).body;
    expect((await http().post(`/api/provider/jobs/${job.id}/accept`).set(authH(c)).expect(200)).body.status).toBe('accepted');
    await step('in_production');
    const detail = (await http().get(`/api/provider/jobs/${job.id}`).set(authH(c)).expect(200)).body;
    for (let i = 0; i < detail.qc.labels.length; i++) await http().patch(`/api/provider/jobs/${job.id}/qc`).set(authH(c)).send({ index: i, done: true }).expect(200);
    await step('ready');
    expect((await http().get(`/api/orders/${order.id}`).set(authH(cust)).expect(200)).body).toMatchObject({ status: 'packing', fulfillment: { status: 'ready', label: 'آماده شد' } });
    await step('out_for_delivery');
    await step('delivered');
    const done = (await http().get(`/api/orders/${order.id}`).set(authH(cust)).expect(200)).body;
    expect(done).toMatchObject({ status: 'delivered', paid: true, paidVia: 'cod' });
    expect(JSON.stringify(done)).not.toMatch(/centerId|providerPricing|providerId|آزمون C/);
    const completed = (await http().post(`/api/admin/dispatch/jobs/${job.id}/status`).set(authH(admin)).send({ status: 'completed', reason: 'e2e' }).expect(200)).body;
    expect(completed.status).toBe('completed');
    expect(completed.history.map((h: any) => h.status)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['assigned', 'accepted', 'in_production', 'ready', 'out_for_delivery', 'delivered', 'completed']));
  });

  it('§89: the rejection is recorded with reason and time, returns to the queue, the admin reassigns, the history keeps the previous provider', async () => {
    const admin = await login('09120000000');
    const cust = await login('09123456789');
    const P = await testProviders(admin);
    const order = await placeCod(cust, [glossy(10)]);
    const { id } = await jobOf(admin, order.id);
    await http().post(`/api/admin/dispatch/jobs/${id}/assign`).set(authH(admin)).send({ providerId: P.C.id, confirmMissingPrice: true }).expect(200);
    const c = await login(P.C.phone);
    await http().post(`/api/provider/jobs/${id}/reject`).set(authH(c)).send({ reasonCode: 'other' }).expect(400);
    await http().post(`/api/provider/jobs/${id}/reject`).set(authH(c)).send({ reasonCode: 'equipment_unavailable' }).expect(200);
    const back = await jobOf(admin, id);
    expect(back).toMatchObject({ status: 'unassigned', lastException: { kind: 'rejected', reason: 'تجهیزات در دسترس نیست', providerName: 'آزمون C' } });
    expect(back.assignments[0]).toMatchObject({ providerName: 'آزمون C', outcome: 'rejected', reasonCode: 'equipment_unavailable', rejectedAt: expect.any(String) });
    const alerts = (await http().get('/api/notifications/mine').set(authH(admin)).expect(200)).body;
    expect(alerts.items.some((n: any) => n.event === 'job_rejected' && n.jobId === id && n.audience === 'admin')).toBe(true);

    const negin = (await providersByName(admin)).get('چاپ نگین')!;
    const again = (await http().post(`/api/admin/dispatch/jobs/${id}/assign`).set(authH(admin))
      .send({ providerId: negin, override: true, reason: 'e2e §89 (ساعت کاری)', confirmMissingPrice: true }).expect(200)).body;
    expect(again.assignments.map((a: any) => [a.providerName, a.outcome])).toEqual([['آزمون C', 'rejected'], ['چاپ نگین', 'pending']]);
    await http().get(`/api/provider/jobs/${id}`).set(authH(c)).expect(404);
    const stubs = (await http().get('/api/provider/jobs?tab=cancelled').set(authH(c)).expect(200)).body.items as any[];
    expect(stubs.find((s) => s.id === id)).toEqual({ id, orderCode: order.code, withdrawn: true, at: expect.any(String), label: 'رد شده توسط شما' });
    await http().patch(`/api/admin/orders/${order.id}/status`).set(authH(admin)).send({ status: 'cancelled' }).expect(200); // keep the demo provider clean
  });

  it('§91: IDOR through job / order / file ids, another provider’s assignment, a smuggled provider id, internal data', async () => {
    const admin = await login('09120000000');
    const cust = await login('09123456789');
    const P = await testProviders(admin);
    const up = await http().post('/api/uploads?purpose=docs').set(authH(cust))
      .attach('file', Buffer.from('%PDF-1.4\n%%EOF\n'), { filename: 'e2e.pdf', contentType: 'application/pdf' }).expect(201);
    const order = await placeCod(cust, [{ kind: 'color_printing', spec: { ...glossy(1).spec, fileId: up.body.id } }], { centerId: P.C.id, providerId: P.C.id });
    expect((await http().get(`/api/admin/orders/${order.id}`).set(authH(admin)).expect(200)).body.centerId).toBeUndefined(); // customer changing provider_id
    const { id, status } = await jobOf(admin, order.id);
    expect(status).toBe('unassigned');
    await http().post(`/api/admin/dispatch/jobs/${id}/assign`).set(authH(admin)).send({ providerId: P.C.id, confirmMissingPrice: true }).expect(200);
    const a = await login(P.A.phone);
    const c = await login(P.C.phone);
    await http().get(`/api/provider/jobs/${id}`).set(authH(a)).expect(404);
    await http().get(`/api/provider/jobs/${order.id}`).set(authH(a)).expect(404);
    await http().post(`/api/provider/jobs/${id}/accept`).set(authH(a)).expect(404);
    await http().post(`/api/provider/jobs/${id}/status`).set(authH(a)).send({ status: 'in_production' }).expect(404);
    await http().patch(`/api/provider/jobs/${id}/qc`).set(authH(a)).send({ index: 0, done: true }).expect(404);
    await http().get(`/api/uploads/${up.body.id}`).set(authH(a)).expect(403);
    await http().get(`/api/uploads/${up.body.id}`).set(authH(c)).expect(200);
    const view = (await http().get(`/api/provider/jobs/${id}`).set(authH(c)).expect(200)).body;
    expect(JSON.stringify(view)).not.toMatch(/"price"|quote|chargedAmount|09123456789|margin|آزمون A|customerPhone/);
    await http().get('/api/admin/dispatch/jobs').set(authH(c)).expect(403);
    await http().get('/api/admin/dispatch/jobs').set(authH(cust)).expect(403);
    await http().get('/api/provider/jobs').set(authH(cust)).expect(403);
    expect(JSON.stringify((await http().get(`/api/orders/${order.id}`).set(authH(cust)).expect(200)).body)).not.toMatch(/centerId|providerPricing|providerId/);
    await http().patch(`/api/admin/orders/${order.id}/status`).set(authH(admin)).send({ status: 'cancelled' }).expect(200);
    expect((await http().get(`/api/provider/jobs/${id}`).set(authH(c)).expect(200)).body.status).toBe('cancelled');
    await http().get(`/api/uploads/${up.body.id}`).set(authH(c)).expect(403); // no file access after cancellation
  });

  it('D12/D17/D22: a print order can be picked up at a Digital Sarv point; the quote is identical to M2', async () => {
    const cust = await login('09123456789');
    const cat = (await http().get('/api/catalog').expect(200)).body;
    const point = cat.pickupPoints?.[0];
    expect(point, 'pnpm seed adds «دفتر مرکزی دیجیتال سرو»').toBeTruthy();
    const draft = { children: [], services: [glossy(2)], delivery: { method: 'customer_pickup', pickupPointId: point.id } };
    const q = (await http().post('/api/orders/quote').send(draft).expect(200)).body;
    const m2 = (await http().post('/api/orders/quote').send({ children: [], services: draft.services }).expect(200)).body;
    expect(q).toEqual(m2); // D22: the delivery choice never changes fees or totals
    expect(q).not.toHaveProperty('homePickup');
    await http().post('/api/orders').set(authH(cust)).send({ ...draft, delivery: { method: 'customer_pickup', pickupPointId: 'nope' }, payMethod: 'cod', pickup: { phone: '09123456789' } }).expect(400);
    const order = await placeCod(cust, draft.services, { delivery: draft.delivery, pickup: { phone: '09123456789' } });
    const got = (await http().get(`/api/orders/${order.id}`).set(authH(cust)).expect(200)).body;
    expect(got).toMatchObject({ delivery: { method: 'customer_pickup', homePickup: false, pickupPoint: { id: point.id } }, fulfillment: { status: 'unassigned', place: { id: point.id } } });
    const admin = await login('09120000000');
    await http().patch(`/api/admin/orders/${order.id}/status`).set(authH(admin)).send({ status: 'cancelled' }).expect(200);
  });

  it('§81: dispatch permissions apply — view-only staff cannot assign', async () => {
    const admin = await login('09120000000');
    const phone = '09127777778';
    const list = (await http().get('/api/admin/staff').set(authH(admin)).expect(200)).body as any[];
    const existing = list.find((s) => s.phone === phone);
    const member = existing
      ? (await http().patch(`/api/admin/staff/${existing.id}`).set(authH(admin)).send({ permissions: ['dispatch_view'] }).expect(200)).body
      : (await http().post('/api/admin/staff').set(authH(admin)).send({ phone, name: 'e2e dispatch', permissions: ['dispatch_view'] }).expect(201)).body;
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1100));
    tokens.delete(phone);
    const staff = await login(phone);
    const page = (await http().get('/api/admin/dispatch/jobs?tab=unassigned').set(authH(staff)).expect(200)).body;
    if (page.items[0]) await http().post(`/api/admin/dispatch/jobs/${page.items[0].id}/assign`).set(authH(staff)).send({ providerId: page.items[0].id }).expect(403);
    await http().get('/api/admin/dispatch/performance').set(authH(staff)).expect(403);
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1100));
    await http().delete(`/api/admin/staff/${member.id}`).set(authH(admin)).expect(200);
    tokens.delete(phone);
  });
```

## Handoff to M4 (ledger & settlement)

What M3 records and M4 must implement:
- **Provider credit basis:** `Order.providerPricing` (select: false) / `Job.providerPricing` — `providerSubtotal`, per-line `unitPrice` / `versionId` / `version` (provider_price_version), `payoutBasis` (production provider, including its own delivery fee) and `delivery.fee` of a separate delivery provider. Every earlier snapshot of a job is in `Job.pricingHistory`.
- **D23 — missing provider prices:** jobs assigned with `confirmMissingPrice` have `providerSubtotal = null`, `payoutBasis = null`, `confirmedMissing = true` and the missing lines in `missing[]` (school binding, flyer, cartridge, repair, thesis page printing, catalog extras, spiral-colour surcharges); migrated v1 assignments have no snapshot at all. M4 must extend the provider price layer to these services (provider combos for them) and/or let the admin enter the payout amount per job with a reason, and must never credit anything automatically for a null basis.
- **When to credit:** `Job.deliveredAt` (status `delivered` / `completed`); COD orders are marked `paid` atomically in the same order update.
- **Compensation:** `Job.cancel.stage` (`before_production` → no credit; `in_production` / `after_production` → admin decides 0 / partial / full) and the `stage` + `previousProviderId` on `reassigned` / `unassigned` / `failed` history entries for a provider who lost a job mid-work.
- **Fees (D22):** the customer's pickup and delivery fees stay in Digital Sarv's share; a provider's delivery fee is a provider credit only via `payoutBasis` / `delivery.fee`.
- **Weekly settlement (D20):** settlement weekday in «تنظیمات»; the performance counters (Task 16) stay informational and never adjust payouts.

### Placeholder scan
Every code step carries the code. Task 19 asks for two one-line placeholder components (`JobSheet`, `PerformanceTab`) that Tasks 20/21 replace in the same milestone, so each task builds on its own. Web tasks have no unit-test runner in this repo; their cycle is `tsc -b` (via `pnpm --filter web build`) + lint + the listed manual checks.

### Type consistency
`JobStatus`, `DeliveryMethod`, `PickupAt`, `JobPriority`, `RejectReason`, `NotificationEvent`, `ByRole` (with `customer`), `JobHistoryEntry`, `ProviderPricingSnapshot`, `DispatchJobRow`/`DispatchJobDetail`, `EligibilityRow`/`Eligibility`, `ProviderJobView`/`ProviderJobDetail`/`ProviderWithdrawnView`, `ProviderPerformance` use the same names and fields in the contract (Task 1), the API (Tasks 2–16) and the web types (Task 18). Service signatures used across tasks: `JobsService.move(jobId, to, { actor, byRole, by?, reason?, reasonCode?, providerId? })`, `giveBack(job, kind, { byRole, by?, reason?, reasonCode?, providerId?, assignmentId? })`, `cancelForOrder(order, byRole)`, `onOrderDelivered(orderId, byRole)`, `onOrderOutForDelivery(orderId)`, `courierCollected(orderId)`, `ensureJob(order)`, `fulfillmentFor(orders)`; `DispatchService.assign/reassign(id, dto, by)`, `unassign(id, reason, by)`, `assignFromOrder(orderId, centerId | null, by)`, `boardInfo(orderIds)`, `performance(q)`; `NotificationsService.notify(event, userIds, ref, suffix?)`, `dispatch(event, order, suffix?)`, `mine(userId, q, audience)`.
